 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
FOREWORD by thE ministER

 Our national curriculum is the culmination of our efforts over a period of seventeen
 years to transform the curriculum bequeathed to us by apartheid. From the start of
 democracy we have built our curriculum on the values that inspired our Constitution
 (Act 108 of 1996). the Preamble to the Constitution states that the aims of the
 Constitution are to:

 • heal the divisions of the past and establish a society based on democratic
 values, social justice and fundamental human rights;

 • improve the quality of life of all citizens and free the potential of each person;

 • lay the foundations for a democratic and open society in which government is
 based on the will of the people and every citizen is equally protected by law;
 and

• build a united and democratic South Africa able to take its rightful place as a sovereign state in the family of
 nations.

Education and the curriculum have an important role to play in realising these aims.

in 1997 we introduced outcomes-based education to overcome the curricular divisions of the past, but the experience
of implementation prompted a review in 2000. This led to the first curriculum revision: the Revised National Curriculum
Statement Grades R-9 and the National Curriculum Statement Grades 10-12 (2002).

Ongoing implementation challenges resulted in another review in 2009 and we revised the Revised National
Curriculum Statement (2002) and the National Curriculum Statement Grades 10-12 to produce this document.

From 2012 the two national Curriculum statements, for Grades R-9 and Grades 10-12 respectively, are combined
in a single document and will simply be known as the National Curriculum Statement Grades R-12. the National
Curriculum Statement for Grades R-12 builds on the previous curriculum but also updates it and aims to provide
clearer specification of what is to be taught and learnt on a term-by-term basis.

the National Curriculum Statement Grades R-12 represents a policy statement for learning and teaching in south
African schools and comprises of the following:

(a) Curriculum and Assessment Policy statements (CAPs) for all approved subjects listed in this document;

(b) National policy pertaining to the programme and promotion requirements of the National Curriculum Statement
 Grades R-12; and

(c) National Protocol for Assessment Grades R-12.

MRS ANGIE MOTSHEKGA, MP
MINISTER OF BASIC EDUCATION

YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA

1.1 IMvelaphi

INkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola yeBanga R-12 icacisa ipolisi yekharityhulam nokuhlola ezikolweni.

Ukuphucula ukusetyenziswa kwayo, INkcazelo yeKharityhulam yeSizwe ihlaziyiwe, yaye olu hlaziyo luya
kuqala ukusebenza ngeyoMqungu/ngoJanyuwari 2012. Isifundo ngasinye senzelwe uxwebhu olunye lwePolisi
yeKharityhulam nokuHlola endaweni yala maxwebhu madala angala: INkcazelo yeKharityhulam yeSizwe, isiKhokelo
seNkqubo yokuFunda kunye nesiKhokelo sokuHlola sesiFundo kwiBanga R-12.

1.2 AmaGqabantshintshi

(a) INkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola yeBanga R-12 (eyoMqungu/Janyuwari 2012) imele inkcazelo
 yepolisi yokufunda nokufundisa kwizikolo zoMzantsi Afrika yaye iquka oku kulandelayo:

 (i) INkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola yesifundo ngasinye kwezo zifundo zivunyiweyo zesikolo;

 (ii) Uxwebhu lwepolisi oluthi, National policy pertaining to the programme and promotion requirements of
 the National Curriculum Statement Grades R-12.; kunye

 (iii) Noxwebhu lwepolisi oluthi, National Protocol for Assessment Grades R-12 (eyoMqungu/Janyuwari
 2012).

(b) Uxwebhu oluthi, INkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola yeBanga R-12 (eyoMqungu/Janyuwari 2012)
 lungena endaweni yala maxwebhu eNkcazelo yeKharityhulam yeSizwe mabini alandelayo:

 (i) iRevised National Curriculum Statement Grades R-9, Government Gazette No. 23406 of 31 May 2002,
 kunye

 (ii) neNational Curriculum Statement Grade 10-12 Government Gazettes, No 25545 of 6 October 2003 and
 No. 27594 of 17 May 2005.

(c) Amaxwebhu eNkcazelo yeKharityhulam yeSizwe ekuthethwe ngawo kumhlathi b(i) no(ii), aquka la maxwebhu
 epolisi alandelayo, aza kuthi aye eyekiswa ngokuyekiswa, engenelelwa yiNkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam
 nokuHlola yeBanga R-12 (eyoMqungu/Janyuwari 2012) ngo2012 - 2014:

 (i) INkcazelo yeKharityhulam yeSizwe yezifundo ngezifundo, isiKhokelo seNkqubo yokuFundisa kunye
 nesiKhokelo sokuHlola sesiFundo seBanga R-9 neseBanga 10-12;

 (ii) Uxwebhu lwepolisi oluthi, National Policy on assessment and qualifications for schools in the General
 Education and Training Band, olubhengezwe kwiGovernment Notice No. 124 ekwiGovernment Gazette
 No. 29626 of 12 February 2007;

 (iii) Uxwebhu lwepolisi oluthi, the National Senior Certificate: A qualification at Level 4 on the National
 Qualifications Framework (NQF), olubhengezwe kwiGovernment Gazette No.27819 of 20 July 2005;

 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
 (iv) Uxwebhu lwepolisi oluthi, An addendum to the policy document, the National Senior Certificate: A
 qualification at Level 4 on the National Qualifications Framework (NQF), regarding learners with special
 needs, olupapashwe kwiGovernment Gazette, No.29466 of 11 December 2006, lufakwe kuxwebhu
 lwepolisi oluthi, National policy pertaining to the programme and promotion requirements of the National

 (v) Noxwebhu lwepolisi oluthi, An addendum to the policy document, the National Senior Certificate: A
 qualification at Level 4 on the National Qualifications Framework (NQF), regarding the National Protocol
 for Assessment (Grades R-12), olubhengezwe kwiGovernment Notice No.1267 kwiGovernment Gazette

(d) Uxwebhu lwepolisi oluthi, National policy pertaining to the programme and promotion requirements of the
 National Curriculum Statement Grades R-12; kunye namacandelo akwiPolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola
 njengoko ebonisiwe kwisahluko sesi-2, 3 nesesi-4 solu xwebhu aqulethe izithethe nemigangatho yeNkcazelo
 yeKharityhulam yeSizwe yeBanga R-12. Ngoko ke, olu xwebhu luya kuthi, ngokwecandelo 6A leSouth African
 Schools Act (uMthetho oLawula iZikolo zaseMzantsi Afrika), ka1996 (uMthetho wama-84 ka1996,) lwenze
 isiseko sokuxhobisa umPhathiswa wezeMfundo esisisiSeko ukuba akwazi ukubeka awona manqanaba
 aphantsi eziphumo nemigangatho, ngokunjalo neenkqubo nemigaqo yokuhlola impumelelo yabafundi,
 manqanaba lawo aza kusebenza kwizikolo zikarhulumente nezabucala.

1.3 IiNjongo eziPhangaleleyo zeKharityhulam yoMzantsi Afrika

(a) INkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola yeBanga R-12 inika isikhokelo kulwazi, izakhono nezithethe
 (izinto ezixabisekileyo) ezinokufundwa ezikolweni zaseMzantsi Afrika. Le kharityhulam ijonge ukuqinisekisa
 ukuba abafundi bafumana ulwazi bekwasebenzisa nezakhono ngeendlela ezihambelana nobomi babo
 bemihla ngemihla. Kungoko ke le kharityhulam ikhuthaza uluvo lokusebenzisa kakhulu iimeko zalapha, kodwa
 ibe ikwayise iso nemiba ebalulekileyo yehlabathi jikelele.

(b) INkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola yeBanga R-12 yenza oku kulandelayo:

 • Ixhobisa abafundi ngolwazi, izakhono nezithethe eziya kubanceda ukuba bakwazi ukuzimela nokuthabatha
 inxaxheba ekuhlaleni njengabemi belizwe elikhululekileyo; nokuba bavela kweyiphi imo yezentlalo, uhlanga,
 isini, bekhangeleka njani ngokomzimba okanye bekuliphi inqanaba lengqiqo;

 • Ivulela abafundi amathuba okuya kwimfundo ephakamileyo;

 • Incedisa abafundi ukuba bakwazi ukuphuma kumaziko emfundo baye kumaziko omsebenzi; yaye

 • Inika abaqeshi umfanekiso ocacileyo ngobuchule nolwazi abanalo abafundi.

(c) INkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola yeBanga R-12 isekelwe phezu kwale mithetho-siseko ilandelayo:

 • Ukuphuculwa kwezentlalo: ukuqinisekisa ukuba kuyalungiswa ukungalingani kwezemfundo okwabangelwa
 lixesha lobandlululo, nokuba bonke abemi banikwa amathuba emfundo alinganayo;

 • Ukufunda ngokubandakanyeka kusetyenziswa ingqiqo: ukukhuthaza indlela yokufundisa efuna abafundi
 ukuba bathathe inxaxheba bebonakalisa ingqiqo, endaweni yokufunda ngokubethelela izimvo nokufunda
 nje bengaziqiqisisi iinyaniso okanye izimvo abazinikwayo;

 4 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
 • Ulwazi nezakhono ezikumgangatho ophakamileyo: ukubekwa kweyona migangatho isezantsi yolwazi
 nezakhono ekufuneka abafundi bezibonakalise kwibanga ngalinye, kananjalo nokumiselwa kwemigangatho
 ephezulu enokufikelelwa kuzo zonke izifundo;

 • Ukuqhubela phambili: umxholo wokufundwayo neemeko ekufundwa phantsi kwazo kwibanga ngalinye
 ubonisa ukuqhubela phambili, ukususela kokulula ukuya koko kuntsonkothileyo;

 • Amalungelo oluntu, uqukaniso, ukukhathalelwa kokusingqongileyo nobulungisa bezentlalo: ukufakwa
 kwemithetho-siseko neendlela zokwenza ubulungisa kwezentlalo nokukhathalela indalo esingqongileyo
 kwanamalungelo oluntu, njengoko ebekiwe kuMgaqo-siseko weRiphablikhi yoMzantsi Afrika. INkcazelo
 yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola yeBanga 10-12 ibonakalisa uvakalelo kwimiba yeyantlukwano
 enjengendlala, ukungalingani, uhlanga, isini, ulwimi, ubudala, ukuba nenkubazeko neminye imiba;

 • Ukuxatyiswa kolwazi lwemveli: ukuthathela ingqalelo ubutyebi bembali kunye nelifa lemveli leli lizwe
 njengeentsika zokuxhasa izithethe eziqulethwe nguMgaqo-siseko; kunye

 • Nentembeko, umgangatho ophezulu, kunye negalelo elibonakalayo: ukunika imfundo enokuthelekiseka
 neyamanye amazwe ngokomgangatho, ukunatyiswa kwanokungena nzulu kwayo kwimiba efundiswayo.

(d) INkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola yeBanga R-12 ijonge ukuphuhlisa abafundi abakwaziyo:

 • ukuchonga nokusombulula iingxaki bathabathe izigqibo besebenzisa ingqiqo nokucinga okubonakalisa
 ubugcisa;

 • ukusebenza ngempumelelo bebodwa naxa bekunye nabanye njengamalungu eqela;

 • ukuzicwangcisa nokuzilawula bona buqu kunye nemisebenzi yabo, loo nto beyenza ngentembeko
 nangempumelelo;

 • ukuqokelela, ukuhlalutya, ukucwangcisa nokuhlaba amadlala ulwazi;

 • ukunxibelelana nabanye ngempumelelo besebenzisa ubuchule bokubonwayo, bemiqondiso kunye/okanye
 nezinye izakhono zolwimi ngeendlela ezahlukeneyo;

 • ukusebenzisa inzululwazi nobuchwepheshe ngempumelelo nangokubonisa ukuba noxanduva
 ngokunengqiqo ekukhathaleleni indalo esingqongileyo nempilo yabanye; kunye

 • nokubonisa ukuliqonda ihlabathi ukuba liyiseti yezinto ngezinto ezizalanayo ngokuqaphela ukuba iimeko
 ezisonjululwa phantsi kwazo iingxaki azizimelanga zodwa geqe.

(e) Ukuqukaniswa kwemfundo kufuneka kube ngundoqo ekulungiseleleni, ekucwangciseni nasekufundiseni
 kwisikolo ngasinye. Oku kunokwenzeka kuphela xa bonke ootitshala benolwazi oluphangaleleyo malunga
 nendlela yokuqonda nokusombulula izidingo zokufunda kunye nendlela yokucwangcisela abafundi
 abangafaniyo.

 Undoqo ekulawuleni ukuqukaniswa kwemfundo kukuqinisekisa ukuba izidingo ziyachongwa zize zisonjululwe
 ngawo onke amaqela enkxaso efanelekileyo asesikolweni, aquka ootitshala, amagosa esithili, amaQela
 eNkxaso amaZiko athile, abazali neZikolo zabaFundi abaneeMfuno eZizodwa njengamaZiko aXhobisa
 ngoLwazi. Ukusombulula izidingo eklasini, ootitshala mabasebenzise iindlela ngeendlela zokujongana
 neyantlukwano kwikharityhulam, ndlela ezo zifana nezo zikuxwebhu lweSebe leMfundo esisisiSeko oluthi
 Guidelines for Inclusive Teaching and Learning (2010).

CAPS 7
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
ICANDELO LESI-2: Ukwazisa iiLwimi

2.1 Intshayelelo

KwisiGaba esisisiSeko, izakhono eziphambili kwiKhaityhulam yoLwimi lokuQala eloNgezelelweyo zezi:

 Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha Ukucinga nokuQiqa nokwAkhiwa nokuSetyenziswa
 Ukufunda nezandi koLwimi, ezibandakanyeka kuzo zone izakhono zolwimi
 Ukubhala nokubhala ngesandla (ukuphulaphula, ukuthetha, ukufunda nokubhala).

Isiqulatho (ulwazi, ingqiqo, nezakhono) esikwiNkcazelo yeKhayityhulam yeSizwe, sibekwe ngendlela elandeleka
kakuhle kwiNkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola, ngekota nganye, kusetyenziswa ezi zihloko. Le Nkcazelo
yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola ixhobisa ootitshala ngoku:

• Intshayelelo equlethe izikhokelo ngokusetyenziswa kwalo mqulu wesiGaba esisisiSeko

• Umxholo, ingqiqo nezakhono emazifundiswe ngekota nganye

• Izikhokelo ngolwabiwo lwexesha

• Izinto ezilindelekileyo kwimiSebenzi yokuHlola okusesikweni neengcebiso ngokuhlola okungekho sesikweni

• Izintlu zezixhobo ezicetyiswayo kwibanga ngalinye

2.2 Ukufundwa kolwimi lwesibini kosongezwa kolwasekhaya

Abafundi bafika esikolweni benolwazi lolwimi lwasekhaya/oluthethwa ekhaya. Bangaluthetha bengathintilizi, kwaye
sebenamagama angamakhulu-khulu abawaziyo. Ukufumana ulwazi lokufunda nokubhala kwibanga labaQalayo (1)
kwakheleka kwesi siseko solwimi luthethwayo. Ngoko ke, kulula ukufumana ulwazi lokufunda nokubhala kulwimi
lwakho lwasekhaya.

Xa abafundi beqalisa ukufunda ulwimi oloNgezelelweyo kwiBanga labaQalayo okanye eloku-1, kufuneka ukufunda
bakwakhele kwisiseko solwimi oluthethwayo. Kufuneka beve kuthethwa kakhulu isiXhosa esingantsonkothanga
abanokusiva basiqonde kwiimeko ngeemeko. Ukuphulaphula utitshala efunda amabali kwiincwadi ezinemifanekiso
emikhulu (iiNcwadi eziNkulu) yindlela elungileyo yokwenza oku njengoko ikwaxhasa ukukhulisa ilitheresi yabafundi
esakhulayo. Njengokuba ukuqonda komfundi kusiya kukhula, kufuneka afumane amathuba amaninzi okuluthetha
ulwimi ngeendlela ezingantsonkothanga. Oku kunika isiseko sokufumana ulwazi lokufunda nokubhala kwiBanga
lesi-2 nelesi-3.

Abafundi banakho ukuthathela kwizakhono ezininzi zelitheresi yolwimi lwaseKhaya. Singenza umzekelo wokuba, xa
abafundi befundiswe kakuhle ukubhala ngesandla kuLwimi lwaseKhaya, bangasebenzisa esi sakhono ekubhaleni
isiXhosa. Ukuba bafunda izandi (ifoniksi) kulwimi lwaseKhaya, akukho mfuneko yokuba bafunde kwakhona
unxulumano lwezandi nopelo esiXhoseni. Kufuneka kuphela basebenzise ulwazi lwabo kwisiXhosa, baze bafunde
olo nxulumano lwezandi nopelo olwahlukileyo kwisiXhosa.

INkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola yoLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo isebenzisa amathuba amahle
ezakhono zelitherasi abafundi abazifumene kulwimi lwaseKhaya. Umzekelo, imisebenzi efana nokufunda
okuKhokelwayo ebiqaliswe kwi-Nkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola yoLwimi lwaseKhaya kwiBanga loku-1

 8 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
iqalisa ukufundiswa kwiNkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlolayoLwimi lokuQala oloNgozelelweyo kwiBanga
lesi-2. Le ke yile nto kuthiwa kukufundwa kolwimi lwesibili kusongezwa kolwasekhaya - ukuphuhlisa isiseko esiluqilima
selitherasi kuLwimi lwaseKhaya, kuze kwakhiwe ilitheresi yoLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo phezu kwayo.

2.3 Imixholo eyahlukileyo yokufundisa ulwimi

Ezikolweni apho abafundi baza kusebenzisa ulwimi oloNgezelelweyo, njengoLwimi lokuFunda nokuFundisa (LoLT)
ukususela kwiBanga lesi-4, kubalulekile ukuba umyinge obonakalayo wexesha unikezelwa ekufundeni olo lwimi
kwisiGaba esiseSeko. Noko kunjalo, kwizikolo ezine-LoLT efanayo kumabanga onke, akwenzeki oku. Kwezi
zikolo, abafundi abaninzi abafunda isiXhosa okanye olunye ulwimi njengoLwimi lwaseKhaya, abazithethi ezi lwimi
njengoLwimi lwaseKhaya, kwaye ke ixesha elininzi elinokufunyanwa malinikelwe kulo msebenzi.

2.4 Ukwabiwa kwexesha

Ukwabiwa kwamaxesha okufundisa iilwimi okucwangciswe ngezantsi apha kuya kuqalisa ngowama-2012. KwisiGaba
esisisiSeko ixesha lokufundisa uLwimi liya kuxhomekeka kwimeko yeelwimi yesikolo. Izikolo zinokubona ngokwazo
ukuba ziwongeze okanye ziwunciphise umyinge wexesha ojongene noLwimi lwaseKhaya neeLwimi ezoNgezelelweyo
zokuQala, ngokulawulwa ziimfuno zabafundi bazo. Ubuncinane bexesha elabelwe uLwimi lwaseKhaya neeLwimi
ezoNgezelelweyo zokuQala liboniswe phakathi kwezibiyeli, kwesi sicwangicso silandelayo:

 ULwimi lwaseKhaya Ulwimi OloNgezelelweyo
 IBanga loku-1 Iiyure ezisi-8 (7) Iiyure ezi-3 (2)
 IBanga lesi-2 Iiyure ezisi-8 (7) Iiyure ezi-3 (2)
 IBanga lesi- 3 Iiyure ezisi-8 (7) Iiyure ezi- 4 (3)

ISebe alikwazi ukugunyazisa ngokwalo indlela yokucakaca ubuncinane bexesha elabelwa izifundo ezahlukileyo,
kodwa kunjalo, esi sicwangciso singezantsi singangumzekelo onokulandelwa kwisigaba ngasinye.

CAPS 9
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
2.5 Ukuhlola

INkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola ibonelela ngeengcebiso kumsebenzi ngamnye kwemisebenzi
yokuHlola okusesikweni kwiiLwimi ezikwisiGaba sesiSeko. Umsebenzi ngamnye unamacandelo athile ajongene
nemiba eyahlukileyo yoLwimi.

 IKOTA IKOTA IKOTA IKOTA
 IBANGA ISIFUNDO ZIZONKE
 YOKU-1 YESI-2 RESI-3 YESI-4
 1 Ulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo 1 1 1 1 4
 2 Ulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo 1 1 2 1 5
 3 Ulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo 1 2 2 1 6

KwiKota yoku-1 mnye kuphela umSebenzi wokuHlola okuseSikweni kwiBanga loku-1 ukuya kwelesi-3, (msebenzi
lowo oqulethe izinto ezichaphazela imiba ethile yoLwimi).. Ukongeza, kunikwa iingcebiso ngokuhlola okungekho
sesikweni, neziya kuzisa ulwazi kwinkqubo yemihla ngemihla yokufunda nokufundisa, kodwa kungarekhodishwa
ngokusesikweni.

2.6 Ukwaziswa koLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo

Xa utitshala eqalisa ukufundisa ulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo kwiBanga loku-1, kufuneka asebenzise indlela elula
yokufaka abafundi bakhe abancinci ‘kuLwimi oloNgezelelweyo’. Indlela elungileyo yokwenza oko kukusebenzisa
iphaphethi, enokunikwa igama lolu lwimi longezelelweyo, umz. uSipho iPhaphethi. Kwisifundo sokuqala, utitshala
wazisa le phaphethi aze axelele abafundi ukuba uSipho akakwazi kuluthetha ulwimi lwabo lwasekhaya; uthetha
isiXhosa kuphela, ngoko ke kuya kufuneka bathethe isiXhosa noSipho. USipho uya kuba luphawu olusisigxina
lweklasi yoLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo. Utitshala unokwazisa iphaphethi yesibini (umz. uNana iPhaphethi)
ukwenzela ukubonisa indlela olusetyenziswa ngayo ulwimi oloNgezelelweyo (umz. ukubulisa).

2.7 Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha

Ukufunda ulwimi oloNgezelelweyo kuphantse kufane nokufunda ulwimi lwasekhaya, umahluko kukuba kona akwenzeki
kwasekuqaleni kubomi babafundi. Kunyaka wabo wokuqala, abafundi beva kakhulu ulwimi olulula lusetyenziswa
emxholweni, nto leyo ibenza bafumane indlela olusetyenziswa ngayo ulwimi nesigama solwimi lwabo lwasekhaya.
Emva kwethuba elimalunga nonyaka, abafundi bayaqala ukuthetha ulwimi lwabo lwasekhaya kodwa bengathethi
ngezivakalisi ezigcweleyo. Baqala ngokubiza igama elinye okanye amabini, abathi bawasebenzise ukubiza uluhlu
lweentsingiselo neenjongo. Bayakwazi ukuqonda ulwimi olunzima kakhulu kunolo bakwaziyo ukuluthetha.

Kubalulekile ukuba ootitshala bayiqonde le nto xa abafundi befunda ulwimi oloNgezelelweyo. KwiBanga -1 abafundi
kufuneka balusebenzise kakhulu ulwimi oluthethwayo ngohlobo lwamabali nemiyalelo yaseklasini. Ukuphulaphula
amabali abaliswayo yeyona ndlela iphambili abalufunda ngayo abafundi ulwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo. Kufuneka
utitshala:

• akhethe ibali elinesakhiwo esilula, esiphindaphindayo, nesivumela ukuchongwa kwesigama neukusetyenziswa
 kolwimi eya kuphinda isetyenziswe (umz. iNtsomi kaDyakalashe neHobe)

• agcine ulwimi lulula kakhulu, ethetha ngokucothayo kodwa ngendlela eyiyo

• asebenzise izijekulo (iintshukumo zomzimba), imifanekiso nezinto zoqobo ukuxhasa ukuqondwa kwebali

 10 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
• alibalise amaxesha amaninzi ibali, esiya ebabandakanya ngokubabandakanya abafundi, umzekelo ngokungenelela
 kwezi ndawo ziphindaphindwayo (umz. “Hobe, Hobe, unabani na apho?” “Ndinabafundi bam.” “Bangaphi?”
 “Khawuthi omnye ndimbone.” “Hayi uzomtya.” “Ndakhwela, ndakhwela.” Lamjula.)

Enye indlela yokwenza abafundi basebenzise ulwimi oloNgezelelweyo kungokuphulaphula amabali (okanye iitekisi
ezingaqanjwanga) afundwa ngutitshala. Utitshala ufunda kwiNcwadi yamaBali, incwadi enkulu enemizobo nemibhalo
emikhulu apho bonke abafundi bakwaziyo ukubona ngeli xa afundayo. Le nto ibizwa ngokuba ‘kukuFunda noTitshala’.
Enye yezinto ezilungileyo ngokuFunda noTitshala kukuba ngaphandle kokuba ingumsebenzi wokuphulaphula
osebenza kakhulu, kukwakhulisa ulwazi lokufunda (ilitheresi) olusakhulayo lwabafundi. Umzekelo, abafundi bafunda
imiba yencwadi (umz. ukuba siqala ukufunda ngaphambili encwadini siye kuphela ngasemva; nokuba sifunda
sisuka ngasekhohlo sisiya ngasekunene yaye siqala phezulu sisiya ezantsi ephepheni), yaye bayaqala ukuqaphela
amagama ambalwa abhalwe ngolwimi oloNgezelelweyo (umz. mna, wena). Abafundi kufuneka bawuqhele umsebenzi
wokuFunda noTitshala nanjengoko beya kube bewenza kwizifundo zabo zoLwimi lwaseKhaya.

Enye indlela esebenza kakhulu yokuqhelisa abafundi ulwimi oloNgezelelweyo kukubanika imiyalelo elula ekufuneka
beyilandele ngokuyenza; umzekelo, athi utitshala, ‘Yiza apha, Thabo,’ elandelisa nentshukumo efanelekileyo, aze
umfundi enze njalo. Le yindlela, ebizwa ngokuba kuKwenza imiYalelo ngokuPheleleyo, inceda ekubeni utitshala
uyakwazi ukubona ngoko nangoko ukuba uThabo uyaqonda na okanye hayi aze amnike ingxelo - nokuba uthi,
“Usebenzile, Thabo,’ okanye angawuphinda umyalelo ethetha ngokucothayo yaye intshukumo eyibonisa nangakumbi.
Ulwimi lwaseklasini (umz. Yiza ngaphambili eklasini uhlale emethini) lunika amathuba amaninzi okungeniswa
eKwenzeni imiYalelo ngokuPheleleyo ngokweendlela ezisetyenziswayo. Izicengcelezo ezinentshukumo, nazo,
yindlela esebenza kakhulu ekudibaniseni ulwimi nentshukumo ngendlela exhasa ukuqonda kunye nokukhumbula
ulwimi.

Ukulunga kwezi ndlela zintathu zicaciswe apha ngentla (ukuphulaphula amabali, ukuFunda noTitshala noKwenza
imiYalelo ngokuPheleleyo) kukuba zonke zijonge ekufundeni ulwimi ngokuphulaphulela ukuqonda. Le nto isusa
uxinzelelo kwaba bafundi bancinci ekufuneka bethethile, inciphise ixhala ikwabavumela ukuba bajonge ekuqondeni
ulwimi. Kodwa ke, ukuze babe ngabasebenzisi abapheleleyo bolu lwimi, abafundi kufuneka baziqhelise nokuthetha.

Ekuqaleni, ulwimi lwabafundi luya kulandela indlela ethile - iingoma ezinkqayiweyo, izicengcelezo ezinentshukumo
kunye nemibongo, kananjalo nolunye ulwimi olulandela indlela ethile olufundwa luzizicuku, umzekelo, ‘Molo,
unjani?’ ‘Ndiphilile, unjani wena?’ Kodwa ngokuhamba kwexesha, ngokuya abafundi beqala ukuluqonda ulwimi
oloNgezelelweyo, kufuneka baqale ukuthetha, beqala ngokuthetha igama elinye okanye amabini. Umzekelo, xa
bephendula umbuzo katitshala othi, ‘Ulithandile ibali?’ umfundi uphendula athi ‘Ewe’ okanye ‘Hayi.’

Ekuqaleni, ulwimi olukhulayo lwabafundi kufuneka luqale kancinci ngokulula luye lusenziwa nzima (okt. luboniswe
benikwa inkxaso). Umzekelo, abafundi bangaqala ngokulinganisa amabali athile abawabaliselwe okanye
abawafundelwe ngutitshala, bethetha incoko ethile. Ngoncedo lukatitshala, abafundi bangalibalisa kwakhona ibali.
Kufuneka utitshala aqiniseke ukuba bonke abafundi bafumane amathuba okuthetha isiXhosa. Kuba abafundi beya
kuqhubela phambili ngamathuba ohlukeneyo, kufuneka utitshala alungiselele amathuba okuthetha (umz. imibuzo
ayibuzayo) afanele inqanaba lomfundi ngamnye. Nanjengoko abafundi bedlula kula mabanga, kufuneka utitshala
alindele abafundi ukuba bathethe kakhulu yaye izinto abazithethayo ziye zisiba nde.

Ngokuba abafundi beqhubela phambili nokufunda isiXhosa, kufuneka bangeniswe kwezinye iintlobo zeetekisi.
KwiBanga -1, baya kudibana namabali amaninzi baze baqale ukuqonda isakhiwo neempawu zetekisi ebaliswayo
(okt. kwaziswa abalinganiswa, kucaciswa indawo nexesha, kuvela ingxaki ize isonjululwe; ibali elibaliswayo lidla
ngokubaliswa ngexesha eladlulayo). KwiNkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlolayeBanga lesi-2 kulwimi
lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, kwaziswa amabali abaliswa ngomlomo (oral recounts) (umz. Izolo besiye edolophini.

CAPS 11
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
Siqale ngokuya kuthenga ukutya evenkileni yokutya. Emva koko, siye kwithala leencwadi (elayibrari), njl.), kuze
kwiBanga lesi-3 kuqukwe namabali abaliswa ngokubhaliweyo. Ibali elibaliswayo luhlobo olubalulekileyo kuba lidala
ibhulorho phakathi kolwimi oluthethwayo nolubhalwayo. Sidla ngokusebenzisa amabali abaliswayo (umz. ukuxelela
abantu izinto esizenzileyo), kodwa siyawabhala naphantsi. KwiBanga lesi-3, abafundi bakwaziswa ngomlomo
kwiitekisi ezilandela imiyalelo (procedural texts) (okt. imiyalelo efana neeresiphi) neengxelo zeenkcukacha (umz.
Iindlovu zizilwanyana ezikhulu. Zihlala ngokwemihlambi, umz.). Imizekelo yezi ntlobo zeetekisi inikiwe kwiCandelo
lesi-4 ekupheleni kolu xwebhu, ngokunjalo nengcaciso yesakhiwo neempawu.

Imisebenzi yokuthetha/ukufunda okungqalileyo yemihla ngemihla neyakanye/kabini ngeveki eyakhelwe
kwimixholo ethile

Ixesha elininzi kufuneka lichithwe ekuPhulaphuleni nasekuThetheni kwiBanga loku-1.Oku kuyehla kumaBanga esi-2
nelesi-3 xa kufakwa ukufunda nokubhala okuninzi kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo. Kufuneka kugxilwe kakhulu
ekuPhulaphuleni nasekuThetheni kwisiGaba esisiSiseko gabalala.

KwiNkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlolayoLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo, ukuphulaphula nokuthetha
kulungiselelwe ngolu hlobo:

• uluhlu lwemisebenzi eya kwenziwa imihla ngemihla ngethuba leveki. Ukukhethwa nenani lemisebenzi eya
 kwenziwa ngosuku ngalunye kuya kuxhomekeka kutitshala nexesha analo; le nto iya kwahluka ngokokuba
 usebenzisa elona xesha lincinane okanye elona lininzi kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo.

• umsebenzi ojolise ‘ekuphulaphuleni amabali abaliswayo nafundwayo’, ofundiswa kanye okanye kabini ngeveki,
 ngokuxhomekeke kwiBanga kunye nexesha elifumanekayo.

Le misebenzi ibekwa ngokwemixholo. Utitshala ucetyiswa ukuba akhethe imixholo emine ngekota, mhlawumbi
ingambalwa kunoko xa esebenzisa elona xesha lincinane loLwimi oloNgezelelweyo lokuQala. Kufuneka utitshala
akhethe imixholo encedisa ekufundiseni ulwimi oloNgezelelweyo. Le mixholo kufuneka ibe yeqhelekileyo kubafundi,
kungangcono xa inokuba yileyo sele ifundisiwe kuLwimi lweNkobe/ lwaseKhaya, yaye inike amathuba amaninzi
okufundisa ulwimi ngokusemxholweni (umz. kufuneka banike amathuba okubonisa nokusebenzisa izinto ezikhoyo
apho eklasini). Eminye imixholo inikwa njengemizekelo, kodwa ziingcebiso nje ezi, akufunekanga ithathwe
njengemiselweyo.

Izizathu zokusebenzisa imixholo kukwenza ukuba isigama nesakhiwo solwimi sikwazi ukubekelwa ukumana
sisetyenziswa kwimixholo enentsingiselo. Umzekelo, amagama anxulumene nomzimba (ubuso, amehlo, iindlebe,
impumlo, umlomo, iingalo, imilenze, iinyawo) nezakhiwo abekwe kuzo (Khomba ______ yakho./ Yi______ yam le./
Zii _____ zam ezi.) afuna ukuqala aviwe esetyenziswa emxholweni amaxesha ngamaxesha; emva koko abafundi
bafuna amathuba okuwasebenzisa. Ngokuya abafundi besiya kwiBanga lesi-2 nelesi-3, baya kufuna namathuba
okuwafunda bawabhale. Abafundi baya kukwazi ukusikhumbula basisebenzise isigama nezakhiwo xa zimana
zisetyenziswa.

2.8 Ukufunda nokubhala

Ngenxa yezizathu ezinikwe apha ngentla, kujoliswe kakhulu ekuphuhliseni ulwimi oluthethwayo kwiBanga-1, apho
abafundi baya kuba befunda ukufunda nokubhala ngoLwimi lwabo lwaseKhaya. Kodwa ke, kwiBanga lesi-2 nelesi-3
kufuneka kugxininiswe ekuphuhliseni ulwazi (ilitheresi) ngoLwimi oloNgezelelweyo lokuQala.

Ukufunda nokubhala kukwayinxalenye yokuphuhliswa kolwimi lomfundi ngesiXhosa. Ukufunda kunika abafundi
ithuba elongezelelweyo lokusebenzisa isiXhosa. Uphando lusifundise ukuba uphuhliso lwesigama sabafundi

 12 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
luxhomekeke kakhulu ekubeni bafunda kangakanani. Ukubhala kubalulekile kuba kunyanzela abafundi ukuba
bacinge ngeukusetyenziswa kolwimi nopelo. Le nto ikhuthaza abafundi ukuba balunqhele ulwimi, ikhawulezisa
ukukwazi kwabo ukuluqonda ulwimi ikwanyusa izinga lokulusebenzisa kwabo ngokufanelekielyo.

Ngoko ke kuchithwa ixesha elininzi kwimisebenzi yokufunda nokubhala kwiNkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam
nokuHlolayoLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo kwiBanga lesi-2 nelesi-3. Imisebenzi yokuFunda nokuBhala yile
ilandelayo:

2.8.1 Ukuboniswa kwezinto ezibhaliweyo ezisingqongileyo (environmental print)

Ukususela kwiminyaka yabo yokuqala, abafundi baseMzantsi Afrika badibana nezinto ezininzi ezibhalwe
ngesiXhosa kokusingqongileyo, umsekelo imibhalo exhonyiweyo (signage) (imithetho yendlela, imibhalo yeevenkile,
njl.) kunye naleyo ikumaqweqwe okanye amaphepha asongele iimveliso (packaging). Ootitshala bangasebenzisa
oku njengesiqalo solwazi (ilitheresi) olukhulayo lwabafundi kulwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo, umzekelo, ngokuza
neepakethe eziqhelekileyo okanye neentengiso eklasini babone ukuba abafundi bangawaqaphela na amagama
eemveliso. Ukususela kwikota yesi-3 yeBanga loku-1, xa abafundi sele benolwazi (ilitheresi) oluthile abalufunde
kuLwimi lwabo / lwaseKhaya, utitshala angaqala ukubeka iileyibheli kwizinto eziseklasini ngoLwimi / lwaseKhaya
nesiNgesi. Le misebenzi ixhasa ukufunda okuzenzekelayo (incidental learning); ayijonganga kwimisebenzi yelitheresi
yaye kufuneka inganikwa ixesha elininzi kakhulu.

2.8.2 UkuFunda noTitshala

UkuFunda noTitshala kuqaliswa kwiBanga 1 kuze kuqhube kwisiGaba esisiSiseko xa sisonke. Lo msebenzi ubalulekile
ujolise kuphuhliso lolwimi nelitheresi. Injongo yokuFunda noTitshala kwiBanga-1 kukwenza ukuba abafundi bakwazi
ukusebenzisa ulwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo ngendlela enentsingiselo nenenkxaso. Kukwakhulisa ilitheresi ekhulayo
yabafundi kulwimi lwabo olongezeleleyo. Bavelisa imiba yencwadi baqale ukuqonda amagama ambalwa olwimi
oloNgezelelweyo. Kweli nqanaba, kufuneka utitshala:

• Akhethe itekisi elula eyandisiweyo (umz. iNcwadi eNkulu) enamagama ambalwa nemizobo ecacileyo. Ibali
 kufuneka libe nesakhiwo esicacileyo nesilula (umz Kuluncedo ukuba ulwimi luyaphindaphinda yaye kulula
 ukuqikelela okuza kwenzeka (umz. Luph’ ulwandle? LuseThekwini. Lwenzan’ apho? Luyadlala. Kufuneka buye
 bukhula ubunzima beetekisi ngokuye iminyaka iqhuba.

• Ncokola nabafundi malunga nemifanekiso ukwenzela ukuba baqonde isigama. Buza imibuzo ngolwimi lwabo
 lwenkobe/ lwasekhaya. Bancedise ekudibaniseni ibali noko kwenzeka ebomini babo.

• Itekisi yifundeni amatyeli ngamatyeli nisebenzisa umnwe okanye ‘isikhombi’ ukwenzela ukuba umfundi ayilandele
 kakuhle itekisi.

• Buza imibuzo emalunga nebali.

• Yiya ubabandakanya ngokubabandakanya abafundi ‘ekufundeni’ ibali

Ngokuya abafundi besiya kwiBanga lesi-2 nelesi-3 maziye zisiba ngokuba nzima ngokuba nzima iitekisi. Utitshala
ubonisa indlela efanelekileyo yokufunda asebenzise itekisi ukuphuhlisa isigama, ukuqonda, izakhono zokucazulula,
ukuqondwa kwesakhiwo setekisi, iukusetyenziswa kolwimi nokuphawulwa kwentetho (punctuation).

2.8.3 UkuFunda ngokwaMaqela okuKhokelwayo

KwiBanga lesi-2 abafundi baqala umsebenzi omtsha ngolwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo: UkuFunda ngokwaMaqela
okuKhokelwayo. Kodwa ke, kuya kubakho ukufana komsebenzi nanjengoko beya kuba bebehleli beyenza kulwimi

CAPS 13
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
lwabo / lwasekhaya ukususela ekuqaleni kweBanga loku-1. Kulo msebenzi, utitshala ufuna uluhlu lweencwadi
zokufunda ezibekwa ngokomgangatho wobunzima bazo. Utitshala kufuneka ababeke abafundi ngokwamaqela
abo okukwazi ukwenza anabafundi aba-6 - 10 yaye kufuneka abakhethele incwadi yokufunda efanele umgangatho
wabo. Utitshala usebenza neqela ngalinye imizuzu eli-15 kanye ngeveki ngelixa amanye amaqela ebandakanywa
ekuFundeni ngeziBini okanye ngokuZimela okanye esenza imisebenzi enxulumene netekisi, umzekele, imisebenzi
elula yokubhala efana nokugqibezela izivakalisi okanye ukubeka izivakalisi ngokokulandelelana kwazo. Injongo
yokuFunda okuKhokelwayo kukuba utitshala anike abafundi uhoyo lomfundi ngamnye ukwenzela ukuphuhlisa
ukuqonda kwabo nezakhono zokujongana namagama (work attack) kulwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo.

 Imiyalelo yokuseka amaqela enziwa ngokokukwazi kwawo ukwenza

 Kuya kufuneka abafundi ubaqwalasele xa befunda kwiincwadi zokufunda zeklasi, ukwenzela ukubabeka ngokwamaqela.
 Khetha incwadi yokufunda ocinga ukuba umfundi uya kukwazi ukuyifunda, kodwa ingabiyiyo leyo ilula kakhulu - kufuneka
 kubekho amagama ambalwa awafumana enzima umfundi. Ukuba umfundi uyakwazi ukuyifunda ngokufanelekileyo,
 ebonakalisa uvakalelo olululo, loo tekisi ikwinqanaba lakhe lokufunda. Ukuba umfundi uyasokola, khetha elula kunaleyo de
 ufumane eyomgangatho ofanelekileyo. Wakuba ubahlole bonke abafundi, ungababeka ngokwamaqela ngokokukwazi kwabo
 ukufunda.

 Amanqanaba eSifundo esikukuFunda kwamaQela okuKhokelwayo

 I. Khetha itekisi efanelekileyo:

 Iincwadi zokufunda ezibekwe ngokwamabanga (graded readers) ziya kusetyenziswa kakhulu ekufundeni ngokwamaqela.
 Kufuneka zibe kwinqanaba elingezantsi kuneetekisi ezisetyenziswa ekuFundeni noTitshala. Qala uyifunde le tekisi
 ujonge nasiphi isigama okanye iukusetyenziswa kolwimi enokuba nobunzima ebantwaneni. Oku kunganika utitshala icala
 emakubhekise kulo ukufundisa kwakhe (teaching focus)

 II. Intshayelelo:

 Yazisa olu hlobo lwencwadi (umz. yeqwetyiweyo okanye yeyenyani) kunye nesihloko. Nceda abafundi banxulumanise
 isihloko namava abo obomi. Yijonge le ‘ntetho’ ingaphumi ecaleni yaye yanele nje ukuba abafundi bafunde ngempumelelo
 (imizuzu emi-2 - 3).

 III. Ncokolani ngemifanekiso

 Sebenzisa imifanekiso ukungenisa abafundi kumxholo nize nincokole ngaso nasiphi isigama esitsha. Kwakhona, zama
 ukuwugcina lo msebenzi ungaphumanga ecaleni yaye umfutshane. (imizuzu emi- 2 - 3)

 IV. UkuFunda kokuQala:

 Abafundi bazifundela ngabanye itekisi. Utitshala uqwalasela indlela abaziphatha ngayo abafundi xa befunda yaye
 usenokukhetha nawuphi omnye umxholo (focus) wokufundisa owongezelelweyo esekela kolu qwalaselo. Utitshala
 uhambela umfundi nomfundi eve ngamnye kubo efunda indawana yetekisi ekhwaza. Utitshala uyabakhokela abafundi
 kweli nqanaba ngokuthi, umzekelo:

 • Ulindele ukufunda ntoni kule ncwadi?

 • Ngaba loo nto iyevakala kuwe?

 • Usebenzile! Uzilungisile. Iyevakala loo nto.

 • Yintoni engavakala kakuhle kwesi sivakalisi?

 • Jonga emfanekisweni.

 • Usenokuthi, khawuphinde ujonge unobumba wokuqala kwakhona.

 Ekuqaleni usenokuqala ngoyibuza ngolwimi / lwasekhaya lomfundi le mibuzo. Kodwa ke, qala kwangoko kangangoko
 ukubuza imibuzo ngesiXhosa. Abafundi bafanele ukuba sele beyazi kakhulu le mibuzo kuba bebehleli bewenza lo msebenzi
 kulwimi lwabo / lwasekhaya ukususela ekuqaleni kweBanga loku-1.

 14 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
 V. Ukuqonda

 Buza abafundi imibuzo malunga netekisi ukwenzela ukuqinisekisa ukuba bayaqonda.

 VI. Ukufunda kwesibini nokulandelayo

 Kwiintsuku ezilandelayo abafundi baphinda ukufunda itekisi ngababini okanye bebodwa. Eyona nto kujoliswe kuyo
 apha kukukhulisa ukuqiniseka ngokufunda nokunika amathuba okusebenzisa itekisi ukwenzela ukukhulisa isigama,
 iukusetyenziswa kolwimi nokuqondwa okunzulu kwetekisi. Ukuphindaphindwa kokufunda kuxhasa ukukhuliswa kokukwazi
 ukufunda kulwimi oloNgezelelweyo.

Ootitshala basenokungakuqheli ukusebenzisa ukuFunda okuKhokelwayo ngakumbi kwiklasi yoLwimi lokuQala
oloNgezelelweyo. Ngoko ke, le ndlela ingaya ifakwa ngokufakwa. Bakuzithemba ngokuyisebenzisa kuLwimi /
lwaseKhaya, bangaqala ukuyisebenzisa kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo. Okwangoku, ootitshala bangenza
ukufunda kweklasi yonke, apho bonke abafundi banekopi efanayo yetekisi yaye abafundi betshintshisana ukufunda.
Ootitshala kufuneka bangayeku ukuqwalasela indlela aziphatha ngayo xa efunda umfundi ngamnye babancede
ekukhuliseni ukuqonda nezakhono zokujongana namagama (word attack skills).

Kwakhona, akukho xesha lininzi lokuFunda okuKhokelwayo kwiNkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlolayoLwimi
lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo, nanjengoko likhona kwiNkcazelo yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlolayoLwimi / lwaseKhaya.
Ootitshala abasebenzisa elona xesha lininzi kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo baya kukwazi ukusebenza neqela
ngalinye elincinane imizuzu eli-15 kanye ngeveki. Noko kunjalo, abo basebenzisa elona xesha lincinane kulwimi
lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo abayi kukwazi ukwenza njalo. Kuya kufuneka benze ukufunda kweklasi yonke endaweni
yoKufunda noTitshala.

2.8.4 UkuFunda ngeziBini nokuZifundela

UkuFunda ngeziBini nokuziFundela kulungiselela indlela yokunika abafundi ithuba lokuziqhelisa ukufunda
nokukhuthaza ukufundela ukuzonwabisa. Ekufundeni ngezibini, abafundi ababini bafunda kunye okanye
bayatshintshisana ekufundeni.

Abafundi kufuneka basebenzise eli xesha ekwenzeni izinto ezimbini: 1) ukumana bephinda ukufunda incwadi
yokufunda esetyenziselwa iThuba lokuFunda kweQela okuKhokelwayo de babe nokuyifunda ngokupheleleyo 2)
ukufundela ukuzonwabisa kwiincwadi ezikwikona yokufunda / ilayibrari yeklasi. Itekisi kufuneka ibe kumgangatho
ongezantsana kunowaleyo isetyenziselwa ukuFunda noTitshala nokweQela okuKhokelwayo.

Ukunika abafundi amathuba okuzifundela iincwadi kukwakhulisa ukufunda ngokufanelekileyo, ngokuxhomekeke
ekubeni iincwadi zilula ngokwaneleyo ukuba abafundi bangazifundela ngaphandle koncedo. Iincwadi ezimfutshane
ezilula ezineetekisi eziqikelelekayo nemifanekiso enombala zezona zifanelekileyo. Abanye ootitshala bathanda
ukunika abafundi umsebenzi wokuzifundela (individual reading) ukuba bakwenze ekhaya - baphinde bafunde incwadi
ebifundwa liqela okanye bafunde iincwadi ezilula ‘ezimnandi’. Oku kuziqhelisa ukufunda, xa kusenziwa rhoqo yonke
imihla, kudlala indima ebalulekileyo ekufundeni ukufunda.

2.8.5 Izandi

Inqanaba lokuqala lokucazulula ulwimi lolokuthetha - ukufunda ukukhupha izandi ezahlukeneyo zolwimi (ulwazi
lwezandi). Kufuneka ke ngoku umfundi anxulumanise izandi noonobumba abazimeleyo (umz ‘m’, ‘a’, ‘l’ okanye
‘sh’) aze emva koko azidibanise ukwenza amagama (umz. ‘imali’, ‘shushu’) (izandi). Kufuneka umfundi awaqonde
amagama (ukuqonda) aqhelane nawo ebhaliwe kangangokuba kuzenzekele ukuwabona kwakhe (ukuzenzekela).
Okokugqibela, umfundi makakwazi ukufunda amagama kwizivakalisi ngokukhawuleza nangokuwaqonda
(ukuqhabalaka). Noko kunjalo, le miba yokufunda ukufunda ayenzeki ngokulandelelana kwamanqanaba. Umzekelo,

CAPS 15
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
abafundi bafunda ukuqaphela nokuqonda amagama apheleleyo afumaneka kwinto ezibhaliweyo ezisingqongileyo
(environmental print) noKufunda noTitshala xa besebancinci. Noko kunjalo, inkqubo elandelelanayo yezandi
ibalulekile ekufundeni ukufunda ngolwimi lwakhe / lwasekhaya, kusetyenziswa nokufunda, ukubhala nokuphulaphula
amabali afundwayo.

Xa abafundi beqala ukufunda nokubhala ngolwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo, sele beyazi indlela yokucazulula
ulwimi lwabo / lwasekhaya. Sele beyiqonda imiba yezinto ezibhaliweyo yaye banolwazi lwangaphambili oluninzi
lokunxulumana kwesandi nopelo. Into abayifunayo kwiklasi yabo yezandi zoLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo
kukusebenzisa olu lwazi ekufundeni ukucazulula iitekisi ngesiXhosa (umz. ukudibanisa izandi ezaziwayo ukwenza
amagama - umz. ‘i’, ‘m’, ‘a’, ‘dl’, ‘n’ > ‘imadlana’). Abafundi bakwafuna ukufunda ukuba unxulumano phakathi
kwesandi nopelo lwahluka njani phakathi kolwimi / lwasekhaya nolwimi oloNgezelelweyo. Umzekelo, ‘th’ esiNgesini
umele izandi ezibini ezahlukeneyo, ezahlukileyo kwizandi eziku’th’ wesiXhosa (umz. thank, that, thatha). Amaqabane
esiXhosa anobunzima obubodwa kwizithethi zesiNgesi, yaye le nto yenziwa nzima nangakumbi yindlela abhalwa
ngayo (umz. ‘dl’ - ukudlala, ‘hl’ - ukuhleka, ‘ngq’ - ‘ingqayi’).

Kubalulekile ukuba kwiBanga loku-1 abafundi bakhulise isiseko esiqinileyo sokuthetha ngolwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo.
Kungenjalo, abayi kuwaqonda amagama abawacazululayo kwisiNgesi kwiBanga lesi-2 yaye umsebenzi abawenza
kwizandi uya kuphelela ‘ekubizeni okubhaliweyo’. Abafundi baya kufumana lukhulu nasekufundeni ukuchonga
izandi zesiXhosa (ulwazi lwezandi) kwiBanga loku-1. Le nto ifumaneka nangakumbi ngeengoma nezicengcelezo
ezibanceda ekwahluleni izandi (umz. Langa, langa yiza ngapha; Thunzi, thunzi bhek’ eBhayi).

Kubalulekile ukuba utitshala awuqonde umsebenzi wakhe ukuba kukwakha ulwazi lonxulumano phakathi kwesandi
nopelo ngokokuhamba kwamaxesha kulwimi oloNgezelelweyo, hayi ukubaqhelisa ukuqiniseka ngexesha elinye.
Izandi kufuneka zibe ngohlobo lwemisebenzi emifutshane eyenziwa rhoqo kwisiGaba esisiSiseko xa sisonke.

Imisebenzi yezandi yemihla ngemihla / yeveki

Kufuneka kujongwe ngokukodwa izandi kwisiGaba esisiSiseko xa sisonke. Inkqubo ilungiselelwe kwiNkcazelo
yePolisi yeKharityhulam nokuHlola yoLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo. KwiBanga loku-1 kufuneka kujongwe
kakhulu ekukhuliseni ulwazi lwezandi. KwiBanga lesi-2 nelesi-3 kunikwe inkqubo yezandi eyakhela phezu koko
sele bekwenzile abafundi kulwimi lwabo / lwasekhaya. Nanjengoko lilincinane ixesha elibekelwe ukufundisa izandi,
ootitshala bakhuthazwa ukuba badibanise ukufundiswa kwezandi kwimisebenzi yokuFunda, ukuThetha nokuFunda
noTitshala.

2.8.6 Ukuqatshelwa kwamagama

Abafundi bafunda ukuqaphela amagama ‘abonwayo’ (sight words) (okanye amagama ‘okujonga uxele’) ngokuwabona
amaxesha ngamaxesha. Amagama abonakala rhoqo kwiitekisi (amagama asetyenziswa kakhulu) angafundwa ngale
ndlela. Ngokuya abafundi befunda ngolwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo, kukhona baya befumana amagama abonwayo.

2.8.7 Ukuqonda

Abafundi badla ngokukwazi ukucazulula ulwimi oloNgezelelweyo, kodwa abakwazi ukuyiqonda into abayifundayo. Le
nto iphelela ekubeni abantu bayibize ngokuba ‘kukubiza okubhaliweyo’ (‘barking at print’). Isizathu esiphambili sokuba
abafundi bangakwazi ukuqonda iitekisi kukuba izakhono zabo zolwimi zibuthathaka. Abanasigama nakusetyenziswa
kolwimi kwaneleyo yokuqonda oko bakufundayo. Ngoko ke, ootitshala kufuneka bakhulise isigama nakusetyenziswa
kolwimi kwabo ngokubenza basebenzise isiXhosa esininzi kwinqanaba elifanelekileyo. Iindlela ezifana nokwakha
‘udonga lwamagama’ (word wall) eklasini nokukhuthaza abafundi ukuba bagcine izichazi-magama (okanye iincwadi
zesigama) zabo nazo ziluncedo. Ukwenza abafundi bafunde kakhulu ngolwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo mhlawumbi
yeyona ndlela ingcono yokuphucula isigama sabo. Noko kunjalo, le ndlela iya kusebenza kuphela xa iitekisi
zikwinqanaba elifanelekileyo lokuzifundela (ungancediswa).

 16 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
Enye indlela yokukhulisa ukuqonda kokufunda kwabafundi kukubabuza imibuzo eyenza ukuba abafundi bayijongisise
itekisi. Utitshala kufuneka aqale ngemibuzo elula, umz. ‘Ngubani ...?’ (umz. Ngubani owatya abafundi bakaHobe?)
‘Yintoni ...?’ (umz. Watya ntoni uDyakalashe?) kunye nothi ‘Phi ...?’ (umz. Wayehlala phi uHobe?) Ngokuya abafundi
beqhelana neendlela zemibuzo bekhulisa ulwimi olufunekayo ukuze bayiphendule, kungabuzwa imibuzo enzinyana.
Ngethuba abafundi bekwiBanga lesi-3 kufuneka bakwazi ukuphendula imibuzo ethi ‘Kutheni ...?’ (umz. ‘Kwakutheni
ukuze uHobe ajulele uDyakalashe abafundi bakhe?).

 Ngolwazi oluthe vetshe malunga nendlela yokufundisa ukuFunda noTitshala (Shared Reading), ukuFunda okuKhokelwayo
 (Guided Reading), ukuFunda kweziBini nokuziFundela (Paired and Independent Reading), iZandi (Phonics), ukuqatshelwa
 nokuqondwa kwamagama, jonga kwincwadana yeSebe leMfundo esisiSeko ethi, ‘Teaching Reading in Early Grades’ (2008),
 enokukhutshelwa kwa: www.education.gov.za

2.8.8 Ukubhala

Abafundi bafunda izakhono zokwenziwa koonobumba nokubhala ngoLwimi lwabo / lwaseKhaya. Olu lwazi
bangalusebenzisa xa beqala ukubhala uLwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo lokuQala kwikota yesithathu yeBanga loku-1.
Imisebenzi yokubhala yeBanga loku-1 ilula kakhulu nanjengoko abafundi kufuneka bajonge ekubhaleni ngoLwimi
lwabo / lwaseKhaya.

KwiBanga lesi-2, ukubhala ngoLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo kugxininiswa kakhulu. Ukubhala kuyakhokelwa;
umz, abafundi babhala besebenzisa izakhelo zezivakalisi (sentence frames) ezifana nokuthi, ‘Ndithanda ____./
Andithandi ______.’ KwiBanga lesi-3 ukubhala kuya kuba nzima kakhulu. Abafundi balindeleke ukuba babhale uluhlu
lwemiyalelo namabali abo abawabalisayo, befumana inkxaso. Babhala ibali elilula bekunye notitshala (ukuBhala
noTitshala).

Izakhono ezininzi zithathwa kuLwimi / lwaseKhaya. Umzekelo, abafundi baqala ngokufunda indlela yokubhala itekisi
efana nokubalisa ibali kuLwimi lwabo / lwaseKhaya, baze basebenzise olu lwazi xa, emva kwethuba, befunda ukubhala
ibali elibaliswayo ngoLwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo lokuQala. Ngokufanayo, abafundi bafunda ukudrafta, ukubhala,
ukuhlela nokupapasha umsebenzi wabo (inkqubo yokubhala) kuLwimi lwabo / lwaseKhaya, baze basebenzise ezi
zakhono xa bebhala ngoLwimi oloNgezelelweyo lokuQala.

2.8.9 Izakhi nokusetyenziswa kolwimi

Ulwazi olufanelekileyo lwesigama nokusetyenziswa kolwimi lusisiseko sokuphuhliswa kwezakhono (ukuphulaphula,
ukuthetha, ukufunda nokubhala) kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo. KwiBanga loku-1 isigama neukusetyenziswa
kolwimi zifundwa ngokuzenzekelayo (incidentally) ngokuba bamanyeliswe ulwimi oluthethwayo. KwiBanga lesi-2
nelesi-3, abafundi bakwafumana isigama neukusetyenziswa kolwimi ngokufunda isiXhosa. KwiBanga lesi-3 kukho
imisebenzi eyodwa ejonge ekuSetyenzisweni koLwimi.

Ujoliso lwesigama lubekelwe ibanga ngalinye kunye noluhlu lwamagama asetyenziswa kakhulu esiXhoseni lunikiwe
kwiCandelo lesi-3 lolu xwebhu. Kubalulekile ukuba abafundi bafikelele kolu joliso. Ootitshala bafuna iindlela
zokukhulisa isigama sabafundi, umzekelo:

• Amadonga neeleyibheli zamagama eklasini

• imidlalo yesigama, umz. imibuzo yamagama (quizzes)

• ukuzifundela

• ukuzigcinela izichazi-magama (iincwadi zesigama)

• ukusebenzisa izichazi-magama ezinemizobo zabafundi (ezinolwimi olunye nezineelwimi ezimbini)

 IIMFUNO ZEKOTA NGANYE
 IKOTA 1
UKUPHULAPHULA NOKUTHETHA (EZOMLOMO) IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDISA

 1 iyure nemizuzu engama-30 ngeveki
UMXHOLO / IINGQIQO / IZAKHONO

Iiveki 1-5

Ootitshala mabakhethe izihloko ezibini eziyakubanceda ukwazisa nokubeka isigama esiyakubane sisetyenziswa nokuquka
imisebenzi edweliswe ngezantsi.

Qaphela ukuba izihloko ezicetyiswayo ziingcebiso nje. Ootitshala bangakhetha ezabo ezifanelekileyo ngokwemeko
nezixhobo abanazo. Mabaqiniseke ukuba banazo iincwadi ezinkulu eziyimfuneko/ iipowusta, izicengcelezo, iingoma,
imidlalo nezinto eziphathekayo ngesihloko abasikhethileyo.

Mabazame ukuquka yonke imisebenzi kangangoko ukuba banako bayenze ngaphezulu kwesinye . MabaHlole umsebenzi
waabafundi Besebenzisa imisebenzi yokuHlola okungekho sesikweni okucetyiswayo kwiiveki 1 - 5.
Iiveki 6-10

Ootitshala mabakhethe izihloko ezibini ezitsha ezizakubavumela ukuphuhlisa esele bekwenzile kwiiveki ezintlanu
ezidlulileyo. Izihloko mazivumele utitshala ukuba aqalise isigama esitsha ngokubethelela / ngokugxininisa esifundisiweyo.

Izihloko mazimvumele utitshala aphinde asebenzise ukusetyenziswa kolwimi / izakhi zolwimi ezilula naxa ethetha nabafundi
ngoLwimi OloNgezelelweyo lokuQala.

Ootitshala mabazame ukuquka yonke imisebenzi bade bayiphinde kangangoko banako. MabawuHlole umsebenzi wabafundi
besebenzisa imisebenzi yokuHlola okungekho seSikweni okucetyiselwe iiveki 6 - 10. Ootitshala mabaHlole umsebenzi
wabafundi ngokuseSikweni ekupheleni kweKota.

Imisebenzi yemihla-ngemihla (1 iyure ngeveki)
Makwenziwe omnye nangaphezulu umsebenzi kule ingezantsi yonke imihla:

• Ukuqalisa ukuvelisa isigama somlomo (ukuphulaphula nokuthetha) esebenzisa isihloko esinje ngesi “Iimpahla zam”

• Ukubulisa, nokuthi ndlelantle esebenzisa amabinzana anje ngala“Mholo” “Unjani?” “Ndiphilile”.

• Enze izicelo ezilula umzekelo, “Ndicela ukuphuma’.

• Ukukhomba izinto eklasini okanye emfanekisweni ngokuthunywa ngutitshala, umz. “Ndibonise intombi enxibe ilokhwe
 ebomvu”.

• Ukukhomba izinto eziseklasini okanye emfanekisweni xa enikwa ebuzwa ngutitshala enje ngale, umz. ‘Yintoni le?”
 “Ngumnqwazi”.

• Ukwenza izinto ngokomyalelo, umz. “Khulula ijezi”.

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula, umz.”Imbala unjani ijezi?”. “Ibomvu”.

• Ukuqonda nokuqalisa ukusebenzisa imigaqo yolwimi elula kumxholo, umz. izininzi zezibizo ezinokubalwa, umz. “ikawusi
 enye - iikawusi ezimbini”.

• Ukucula iingoma ezilula enze nezilinganiso ngokuncediswa, umz. “Le yindlela endinxiba ngayo ihempe, endinxiba ngayo
 ihempe, endinxiba ngayo ihempe”.

• Ukuthatha inxaxheba kwizicengcelezo neengoma esenza izilinganiso, umz. “Iphin’inja yam encinane, Iphin’inja yam
 iphi?

• Ukudlala imidlalo ngolwimi, umz. utitshala ufihla incwadi eklasini, emva koko ababuze imibuzo, umz. iphi incwadi?

 ingaphantsi, ingaphezulu, isecaleni kwetafile njalo-njalo.

 24 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

Imisebenzi egxininise kuphulaphulo nokuthetha (imizuzu eli-15 kabini ngeveki)

Ukuphulaphula amabali abaliswayo nafundwayo

Kabini ngeveki, utitshala ufunda okanye ubalisa ibali. Amabali afundiweyo angalinganiswa kusetyenziswe izijekulo
ukubonisa inkcazelo. Amabali afundiswayo mawabe kwiincwadi ezinkulu nakwipowusta apho bangabona khona
imifanekiso.

• Ukuphulaphula amabali amafutshane afundwa kwincwadi enkulu okanye kwipowusta, umz. UNomehlomancinane.

• Ukuqonda nokuphendula imibuzo elula, umz. Unjani umbala womnqwazi? nemiyalelo, umz. “ndibonise izembe
 likaNomehlomancinane”

• Ukuxela amagama ezinto ezimfanekisweni, ukuphendula imibuzo katitshala, umz. “Ngubani lo?” “ngu Nomehlomancinane”.

Ukuvelisa imixholo, isigama, nesakhiwo solwimi.

Ngokuthatha inxaxheba kule misebenzi ingentla:

• Ukuqhubeleka ngokwakha isigama somlomo kuquka isigama esibonisa ingqiqo kwizihloko ezithile umz. umbala-blowu,
 bomvu, luhlaza

• Ukuqalisa ukuvelisa ukuqonda nokukwazi ukusebenzisa izakhiwo zolwimi ezilula kwimeko yolwimi oluthethwayo
 olunentsingiselo, umz. iziyaleli ezinjenge “ijezi yam”; “khulula ijezi yakho”, ixesha langoku kwimo ende, umz. “ndiyanxiba´
 izimnini, umz. “ijezi yam”, izininzi zezibizo ezibalwayo, umz. ikawusi - iikawusi.
UKUHLOLA

Iingcebiso zomsebenzi wokuhlola okungekho sesikweni:

Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha: (ezomlomo kunye/okanye nezenziwayo)

Iiveki 1 - 5

• Ukwenza ngokwemiyalelo elula yomlomo

• Ukukhomba izinto eklasini okanye emfanekisweni ngokuthunywa ngutitshala

Iiveki 6 - 10

• Ukuxela izinto eziseklasini okanye emfanekisweni

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula

Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha (ezomlomo kunye/okanye nezenziwayo)

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula

• Ukubonisa ukuqonda isigama somlomo esilula ngokukhomba izinto eklasini okanye emfanekisweni ngokuthunywa
 ngutitshala, umz. “khomba ijezi ebomvu/eqanda/eblowu/eluhlaza njalo-njalo.

ILiteresi esakhulayo (imizuzu emi-5-10 kanye okanye kabini ngekota)

Utitshala uza nezixhobo, iiphowusta njalo-njalo eklasini ukuze kubekho okanye kusetyenziswe ezo zifumaneka
kuloo ngingqi yabo. Xa bekutyelelo apho engingqini yabo, uyazikhomba ezo zixhobo abafundise ngazo.

• Ukuqaphela amagama asetyenziswa yonke imihla apho engingqini yabo, umz. phuma, ngena, vula, vala

• Ukuvelisa iLiteresi esakhulayo, umz. izihloko ngokufunda notitshala

 26 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

Ukufunda notitshala (elona lincinane imizuzu engama-30 nelona likhulu iyure-1 nemizuzu eli-15 ngeveki)

Xa utitshala esebenzisa elona xesha likhulu kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, uqala incwadi enkulu entsha
(okanye iphowusta okanye nayiphina itekisi enamagama amakhulu) qho ngeveki aze enze umsebenzi yonke imihla.
Xa esebenzisa elona xesha lincinane kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, usebenzisa le tekisi inye iiveki ezimbini
nezintathu aze enze umsebenzi kanye okanye kabini ngeveki. Utitshala ufundela iklasi itekisi leyo, akhombe
amagama kuxoxwe nangemifanekiso ngoko kuqhubekayo ebalini. Uphinda alifunde ibali kulo veki, abakhuthaze
bangenelele xa efunda. Itekisi le isetyenziswa ukwazisa isigama esitsha.

• Ukuphulaphula ibali okanye itekisi belinganisa utitshala, bejonge nemifanekiso

• Ukuxoxa ngemifanekiso besebenzisa LwaseKhaya apho kuyimfuneko

• Ukuchonga izinto emfanekisweni umz. Ukuphendula imibuzo elula bencediswa yimifanekiso umz. Iphi inja, indoda,
 inkwenkwe?

• Ukufunda isigama somlomo umz. inja, indoda, inkwenkwe njalo-njalo

• Xa begqibile ukuphinda -phinda ukufunda, bangenelela kumculo weklasi, umz.chapha, chapha, chapha, imanzi ilokhwe
 yam.

• Ukudlala imidlalo ngebali besebenzisa ingxoxo yababini

• Ukuzoba umfanekiso ukubonisa oyena ndoqo webali
UKUHLOLA

Iingcebiso zomsebenzi wokuhlola okungekho sesikweni:

Ukunakana izandi nendlela ezibizwa ngayo: (ezomlomo kunye/okanye nezenziwayo)

Iiveki 1-5

• Ukuchonga amagama aphinda-phindwayo emabalini, kwiingoma nezicengcelezo, umz. iqaqa liyaziqika-qika.

• Ukuqalisa ukuchonga izandi ezahluka-hlukeneyo zokuqala emagameni, umz. “c” cula, “s” sela

• Ubiza, akhe, abhale amagama esebenzisa amaqabane onke ealfabhethi oononye

Ukufunda: (ezomlomo okanye nezenziwayo)

• Ukuchonga abantu, izilwanyana nezinto kwimizobo ekwincwadi ezinkulu (nakwezinye iitekisi ezinemibhalo emikhulu)

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula ngomfanekiso okanye ibali okanye itekisi enobunyani

• Ukuzoba umfanekiso ngoyena ndoqo webali okanye itekisi enobunyani
Umsebenzi wokuhlola okusesikweni 1

Ukufunda: (Okomlomo/ okwenziwayo)

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula ngomfanekiso okanye ibali okanye itekisi enobunyani

• Ukubonisa ulwazi ngesigama ebalini ngokukhomba izinto emifanekisweni belandela imiyalelo katitshala, umz. ijezi ebomu/
 eqanda/eblowu/eluhlaza
• Ubiza, akhe, abhale amagama esebenzisa amaqabane onke ealfabhethi oononye noonombini

 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
 IKOTA 1
UKUBHALA IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDISA

 Elona lincinane: Alikho

 Elona lininzi : imizuzu eli-15 ngeveki
UMXHOLO / IINGQIQO / IZAKHONO

Ukubhala notitshala (elona lininzi - kanye ngeveki)

Ukubhala okokuqala kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo maluncediswe ngutitshala

• Ngoncedo lukatitshala ubhala isihloko somzobo wakhe aze afunde oko akubhalileyo
UKUHLOLA

Iingcebiso zomsebenzi wokuhlola okungekho sesikweni:

Ukubhala: (okubhalwayo)

• Ukukhuphela isihloko somfanekiso awuzobileyo aze afunde oko akubhalileyo

 28 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

 IKOTA 2
 UKUPHULAPHULA NOKUTHETHA ( EZOMLOMO) IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO
 iyure e-1 nemizuzu engama-30 ngeveki
UMXHOLO / IINGQIQO / IZAKHONO

Iiveki 1-5

Ootitshala mabakhethe izihloko ezibini eziyakubanceda ukwazisa nokubeka isigama esiyakumane sisetyenziswa nokuquka
imisebenzi edweliswe ngezantsi.

Qaphela ukuba izihloko ezicetyiswayo ziingcebiso nje. Ootitshala mabakhethe izihloko zabo ezifanelekileyo
ngokwemeko nezixhobo abanazo. Mabaqiniseke ukuba banazo iincwadi ezinkulu eziyimfuneko/ iipowusta,
izicengcelezo, iingoma, imidlalo nezinto eziphathekayo ngesihloko abasikhethileyo.

Mabazame ukuquka yonke imisebenzi kangangoko. MababaHlole abafundi Besebenzisa imisebenzi yokuHlola okungekho
sesikweni okucetyiswayo kwiiveki 1 - 5.
Iiveki 6-10

Ootitshala mabakhethe izihloko ezibini ezitsha ezizakubavumela ukuphuhlisa esele bekwenzile kwiiveki ezintlanu
ezidlulileyo. Izihloko mazimvumele utitshala azise isigama esitsha ngokubethelela / ngokugxininisa esifundisiweyo.

Izihloko mazimvumele utitshala ngokusoloko ebeka isigama esilula esiyakubuya sisetyenziswe, kwisakhiwo solwimi xa
ethetha nabafundi ngoLwimi OloNgezelelweyo lokuQala.

Ootitshala mabazame ukuquka yonke imisebenzi kangangoko banako. MabaHlole umsebenzi wabafundi abafundi
besebenzisa imisebenzi yokuHlola okungekho seSikweni okucetyiswayo kwiiveki 6 - 10. Ootitshala mabaHlole abafundi
ngokuseSikweni ekupheleni kweKota.

Imisebenzi yemihla-ngemihla (1 iyure ngeveki)
Makwenziwe umsebenzi omnye nangaphezulu yonke imihla, kuxhomekeke kwixesha elikhoyo:

• Ukuqalisa ukuvelisa isigama somlomo (ukuphulaphula nokuthetha) esebenzisa isihloko esinje ngesi “Ukutya”

• Ukuphendula imibuliso elula nokuthi ndlelantle esebenzisa amabinzana anje ngala“Molo” “Unjani?” “Ndiphilile”.

• Enze izicelo ezilula umzekelo, “Ndicela iapile’.

• Ukukhomba izinto eklasini okanye emfanekisweni ngokuthunywa ngutitshala, umz. “Ndibonise iapile/iorenji/ibhanana”
 njalo-njalo

• Ukuxela izinto eziseklasini okanye emfanekisweni nokuphendula imibuzo katitshala enje ngale, umz. ‘Yintoni le?” “Liapile”.

• Ukwenza ngokomyalelo, umz. “thatha iapile, yibeke phezu kwetafile”.

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula, umz.”zingaphi iiorenji?”. “zimbini”.

• Ukuqonda nokuqalisa ukusebenzisa imigaqo yolwimi elula kumxholo, umz. ixesha langoku, umz. “ndiyazithanda iiapile -
 andizithandi iiapile”.

• Ukucula iingoma ezilula enze nezilinganiso ngokuncediswa, umz. “ndiyakuthanda ukutya, ukutya, ukutya,

 ndiyakuthanda ukutya iapile nebhanana”.

• Ukuthatha inxaxheba kwizicengcelezo neengoma esenza izilinganiso, umz. “Iphi inja yam encinane, Iphi inja yam
 iphi?”.

• Ukudlala imidlalo ngolwimi, umz. omnye uchola umfanekiso weapile, awufihle, emva koko ababuze imibuzo, umz. Yintoni
 le? Abanye baqikelele, ‘liapile” njalo-njalo.

CAPS 29
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
Imisebenzi egxininise kukuphulaphula nokuthetha (imizuzu eli-15 kabini ngeveki)

Ukuphulaphula amabali abaliswayo nafundwayo

Kabini ngeveki, utitshala ufunda okanye ubalisa ibali. Amabali afundiweyo angalinganiswa kusetyenziswe izijekulo
ukubonisa inkcazelo. Amabali afundiswayo mawabe kwiincwadi ezinkulu nakwiphowusta apho bangabona khona
imifanekiso.

• Ukuphulaphula ngomdla amabali amafutshane afundwa kwincwadi enkulu okanye kwiphowusta ndawonye nezinye iitekisi,
 bazebangenelele kwikhorasi, umz. UNomehlomancinane.

• Ukuqonda nokuphendula imibuzo elula, umz. Unjani umbala weapile? nemiyalelo, umz. “ndibonise iorenjalo ebomvu”

• Ukuxela izinto ezinye izinto emfanekisweni, ukuphendula imibuzo katitshala, umz. “yintoni le?” “yibhanana”.

Ukuvelisa imixholo, isigama, nesakhiwo solwimi.

Ngokuthatha inxaxheba kule misebenzi ingentla:

• Ukuqhubeleka ngokwakha isigama somlomo kuquka isigama esilandelelanisayo, umz. iintsuku zeveki.

• Ukuqalisa ukuvelisa ukuqonda nokukwazi ukusebenzisa izakhiwo zolwimi ezilula kwimeko yolwimi oluthethwayo
 olunentsingiselo, umz. ixesha langoku kwimo ende, umz. “ndiyazithanda iiapile”, imo elandulayo umz. “andizithandi
 iibhanana”.
UKUHLOLA

Iingcebiso zomsebenzi wokuhlola okungekho sesikweni:

Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha: (ezomlomo kunye/okanye nezenziwayo)

Iiveki 1 - 5

• Ukuxela amagama ezinto eklasini okanye emfanekisweni ngokwesihloko esifundiweyo

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula

Iiveki 6 - 10

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula

• Ukuchaza iimfuno zakhe esebenzisa amabinzana alula afundiswe kule kota.

Iingcebiso zomsebenzi wokuhlola okusesikweni:

Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha (ezomlomo kunye/okanye nezenziwayo)

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula ngeempendulo ezimfutshane. efana nokuba “Mangaphi amaapile alapho)

• Ukuchaza iimfuno zakhe esebenzisa amabinzana alula Umzekelo: Andizithandi iibhanana?

• Ukubonisa ukuqonda isigama somlomo esilula ngokukhomba izinto eklasini okanye emfanekisweni ngokuthunywa
 ngutitshala

 30 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

 IKOTA 2
UKUFUNDA NEZANDI IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDA
 Elona lincinane: imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki
 Elona lininzi: iyure e-1 nemizuzu eli-15 ngeveki
UMXHOLO / IINGQIQO / IZAKHONO

Ukufunda ngezandi namagama (imizuzu e-1-5 ngomsebenzi)

Le misebenzi mayibe mifutshane iqukwe kukuphulaphula nokuthetha, ndawonye nokufunda notitshala.

• Ukwahlula-hlula izivakalisi zomlomo zibe ngamagama azimeleyo ngokuqhwaba ngegama ngalinye, umz. izivakalisi
 ezisebalini

• Ukuqhwaba kumagama aqhelekileyo, umz. bha-na-na ukubonisa amalungu egama

• Ngoncedo lukatitshala, bachonga amagama aphinda-phindwayo emabalini, kwiingoma nezicengcelezo, umz. iqaqa
 liyaziqika-qika.

• Ukuqalisa ukuchonga izandi ezahluka-hlukeneyo zokuqala emagameni, umz. “m” mengo, “l ” lamuni

• Ukwakha izivakalisi ezilula kusetyenziswa izandi ezifundiweyo, umz. esebenzisa izandi ezakhiwe ngamaqabane amabini(
 oonombini). umz.

 bh dl

 bhabha dlala

 ibhatata

 ibhekile dlisa

 ibhola dloba
 imfama, imfene, imfuyo, ingoma, ingubo, ingalo, ingobozi, ihlathi, ihlobo, iphupha, idyasi, inja, igusha, njalo -njalo
• Qala ngamagama aqhelekileyo.

• Ukulandelelana kwezikhamiso emagameni, umz. kwizininzi nakwizikhuzo (iimfene, oosisi, sii! Yhoo! Aa!)

ILiteresi esakhulayo (imizuzu emi-5-10 kanye okanye kabini ngekota)

Utitshala uza nezixhobo, iiphowusta njalo-njalo eklasini ukuze kubekho okanye kusetyenziswe ezo zifumaneka
kuloo ngingqi yabo. Xa bekutyelelo apho engingqini yabo, uyazikhomba ezo zixhobo abafundise ngazo.

• Ukuqaphela amagama asetyenziswa yonke imihla apho engingqini yabo, umz. yima, isikolo.

• Ukuvelisa iLiteresi esakhulayo, umz. izihloko ngokufunda notitshala

• Ukwakha izivakalisi ezilula kusetyenziswa izandi ezifundiweyo

• Ukulandelelana kwezikhamiso emagameni, umz. kwizininzi nakwizikhuzo (iimfene, oosisi, sii! Yhoo! Aa!)

Ukufunda notitshala (elona lincinane imizuzu engama-30 nelona likhulu iyure-1 nemizuzu eli-15 ngeveki)

Xa utitshala esebenzisa elona xesha likhulu kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, uqala incwadi enkulu entsha

(okanye iphowusta okanye nayiphina itekisi enamagama amakhulu) iveki nganye aze enze umsebenzi yonke imihla.
Xa esebenzisa elona xesha lincinane kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, usebenzisa le tekisi inye iiveki ezimbini
nezintathu aze enze umsebenzi kanye okanye kabini ngeveki. Utitshala ufundela iklasi itekisi leyo, akhombe
amagama kuxoxwe nangemifanekiso ngoko kuqhubekayo ebalini. Uphinda alifunde ibali kulo veki, abakhuthaze
bangenelele xa efunda. Itekisi le isetyenziswa ukwazisa isigama esitsha.
• Ukuphulaphula ibali okanye itekisi belinganisa utitshala, bejonge nemifanekiso

• Ukuxoxa ngemifanekiso besebenzisa uLwimi LweNkobe/ LwaseKhaya apho kuyimfuneko

• Ukuchonga izinto emfanekisweni umz. imvula, ilanga, amafu.

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula bencediswa yimifanekiso umz. Injani imo yezulu namhlanje?

CAPS 31
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
• Ukufunda isigama somlomo umz. imvula, ilanga, amafu njalo-njalo

• Xa begqibile ukuphinda -phinda ukufunda, bangenelela kumculo weklasi, umz.chapha, chapha, chapha, imanzi ilokhwe
 yam.

• Ukulinganisa ibali besebenzisa ingxoxo yababini

• Ukuzoba umfanekiso ngoyena ndoqo webali
UKUHLOLA

Iingcebiso zomsebenzi wokuhlola okungekho sesikweni:

Ukufunda ngezandi namagama: (ezomlomo kunye/okanye nezenziwayo)

• Ukuqalisa ukuchonga izandi ezahluka-hlukeneyo zokuqala emagameni, umz. “m” mengo, “l ” lamuni

• Ukuqhwaba kumagama aqhelekileyo, umz. bha-na-na ukubonisa amalungu egama
• Ubiza, akhe, abhale amagama esebenzisa amaqabane onke ealfabhethi, oononye noonombini

Ukufunda: (ezomlomo okanye nezenziwayo)

• Ukuchonga abantu, izilwanyana nezinto kwimizobo ekwincwadi ezinkulu (nakwezinye iitekisi ezinemibhalo emikhulu)

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula ngomfanekiso okanye ibali okanye ngetekisi

• Ukuzoba umfanekiso ngoyena ndoqo webali

• Ukwakha izivakalisi ezilula kusetyenziswa izandi ezifundiweyo

• Ukulandelelana kwezikhamiso emagameni, umz. kwizininzi nakwizikhuzo (iimfene, oosisi, sii! Yhoo! Aa!)

Umsebenzi wokuhlola okusesikweni 2

Ukufunda: (Okomlomo/ okwenziwayo)

• Ukuqhwaba kumagama aqhelekileyo, umz. bha-na-na ukubonisa amalungu egama

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula ngomfanekiso okanye ibali

• Ukubonisa ulwazi ngesigama ebalini ngokukhomba izinto emifanekisweni belandela imiyalelo katitshala, umz. imvula,
 ilanga, amafu

• Ukulandelelana kwezikhamiso emagameni, umz. kwizininzi nakwizikhuzo (iimfene, oosisi, sii! Yhoo! Aa!)

UKUBHALA IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDA
 Elona lincinane: Alikho

Ukubhala notitshala (elona lininzi - kanye ngeveki)

Ukubhala okokuqala kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo maluncediswe ngutitshala

• Ngoncedo lukatitshala ubhala isihloko somzobo wakhe aze afunde oko akubhalileyo
UKUHLOLA

Iingcebiso zomsebenzi wokuhlola okungekho sesikweni:

Ukubhala: (okubhalwayo)

• Ukukhuphela isihloko somfanekiso awuzobileyo aze afunde oko akubhalileyo

CAPS 33
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

 IKOTA 3
UKUPHULAPHULA NOKUTHETHA (EZOMLOMO) IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDA
 Iyure e-1 nemizuzu engama-30 ngeveki

UMXHOLO / IINGQIQO / IZAKHONO

Iiveki 1 - 5

Ootitshala mabakhethe izihloko ezibini eziyakubanceda ukwazisa nokubeka isigama esiyakubane sisetyenziswa nokuquka
imisebenzi edweliswe ngezantsi. Qaphela ukuba izihloko ezicetyiswayo ziingcebiso nje. Ootitshala bangakhetha ezabo
ezifanelekileyo ngokwemeko nezixhobo abanazo. Mabaqiniseke ukuba banazo iincwadi ezinkulu eziyimfuneko/ iiphowusta,
izicengcelezo, iingoma, imidlalo nezinto eziphathekayo ngesihloko abasikhethileyo.

Mabazame ukuquka yonke imisebenzi kangangoko. MababaHlole abafundi Besebenzisa imisebenzi yokuHlola okungekho
sesikweni okucetyiswayo kwiiveki 1 - 5.
Iiveki 6 - 10

Ootitshala mabakhethe izihloko ezibini ezitsha ezizakubavumela ukuphuhlisa esele bekwenzile kwiiveki ezintlanu
ezidlulileyo. Izihloko mazimvumele utitshala azise isigama esitsha ngokubethelela / ngokugxininisa esifundisiweyo.

Izihloko mazimvumele utitshala ngokusoloko ebeka isigama esilula esiyakubuya sisetyenziswe, kwisakhiwo solwimi xa
ethetha nabafundi ngoLwimi OloNgezelelweyo lokuQala.

Ootitshala mabazame ukuquka yonke imisebenzi kangangoko banako. MababaHlole abafundi besebenzisa imisebenzi
yokuHlola okungekho seSikweni okucetyiswayo kwiiveki 6 - 10. Ootitshala mabaHlole abafundi ngokuseSikweni ekupheleni
kweKota.

Imisebenzi yemihla-ngemihla (1 iyure ngeveki)
Mnye nangaphezulu umsebenzi omawenziwe yonke imihla, kuxhomekeke kwixesha elikhoyo:

• Ukuqalisa ukuvelisa isigama somlomo (ukuphulaphula nokuthetha) esebenzisa isihloko esinje ngesi “izilwanyana”

• Ukuphendula imibuliso elula nokuthi ndlelantle esbenzisa amabinzana anje ngala“hamba kakuhle” “sobonana ngomso” .

• Enze izicelo ezilula umzekelo, “nceda undiboleke ipensile’.

• Ukukhomba izinto eklasini okanye emfanekisweni ngokuthunywa ngutitshala, umz. “Ndibonise inkomo, igusha,
 inkuku,ihagu, idada, ikati, ibhokhwe, inja” njalo-njalo

• Ukuxela izinto eziseklasini okanye emfanekisweni nokuphendula imibuzo katitshala enje ngale, umz. ‘Yintoni le?”
 “yigusha”.

• Ukwenza ngokomyalelo, umz. “zoba umfanekiso wekati ”.

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula, umz.”unjani umbala wekati?”. “imnyama ”.

• Ukuqonda nokuqalisa ukusebenzisa imigaqo yolwimi elula kumxholo, umz. izichazi, umz. “inkomo inkulu, ikati incinane”.

• Ukuchonga umntu, isilwanyana okanye into ngokunika inkcaza korayi-rayi okanye oqashi-qashi, umz. “Ndisisilwanyana
 esincinane. Ndinemilenze emine nomsila. Xa ndikhala ndithi nyawu-nyawu-nyawu. Ndiyintoni?”

 - Uyikati.

• Ukucula iingoma ezilula enze nezilinganiso ngokuncediswa, umz. “Iphin’inja yam encinane, Iphin’inja yam iphi?”.

• Ukuthatha inxaxheba kwizicengcelezo neengoma esenza izilinganiso, umz. “ndiyinkuku, bhabha bhabha, ndiyihagu,
 hoki, hoki.”

• Ukudlala imidlalo ngolwimi, umz. omnye ulinganisa ukukhala kwesilwanyana, emva koko ababuze imibuzo, umz. Yintoni le
 ikhalayo? Abanye baqikelele, “yigusha, yinkomo, yibhokhwe, yinja” njalo-njalo.

 34 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

Imisebenzi egxininise kuphulaphulo nokuthetha (imizuzu eli-15 kabini ngeveki)

Ukuphulaphula amabali abaliswayo nafundwayo

Kabini ngeveki, utitshala ufunda okanye ubalisa ibali. Amabali afundiweyo angalinganiswa kusetyenziswe izijekulo ukubonisa
inkcazelo. Amabali afundiswayo mawabe kwiincwadi ezinkulu nakwiphowusta apho bangabona khona imifanekiso.

• Ukuphulaphula ngomdla amabali amafutshane afundwa kwincwadi enkulu okanye kwiphowusta ndawonye nezinye iitekisi,
 bazebangenelele kwikhorasi, umz. Ibhola yenkosazana.

• Ukuqonda nokuphendula imibuzo elula, umz. ihlala phi igusha? nemiyalelo, umz. “ndibonise inkuku”

• Ukuxela izinto ezinye izinto emfanekisweni, ukuphendula imibuzo katitshala, umz. “yintoni le?” “yibhokhwe”.

Ukuvelisa imixholo, isigama, nesakhiwo solwimi.

Ngokuthatha inxaxheba kule misebenzi ingentla:

• Ukuqhubeleka ngokwakha isigama somlomo kuquka isigama esilandelelanisayo, umz. inani, nobungakanani.

• Ukuqalisa ukuvelisa ukuqonda nokukwazi ukusebenzisa izakhiwo zolwimi ezilula kwimeko yolwimi oluthethwayo
 olunentsingiselo, umz. izichazi, umz. “inkulu - incinane”, izalathisi, umz. “lo, lowo, lowa ” nezihlomelo zobunjani,umz.
 ngokukhawuleza, ngokucotha.

UKUHLOLA

Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha: (ezomlomo kunye/okanye nezenziwayo)

Iiveki 1 - 5

• Ukukhomba izinto eklasini okanye emfanekisweni ngokwesihloko esifundiweyo

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula

Iiveki 6 - 10

• Ukubeka intetho yakhe ngamabinzana alula

• Ukuchonga umntu, isilwanyana okanye into ngokunika inkcaza korayi-rayi okanye oqashi-qashi, umz. “Ndisisilwanyana
 esincinane. Ndinemilenze emine nomsila. Xa ndikhala ndithi nyawu-nyawu-nyawu. Ndiyintoni?”

 - Uyikati.

Ukufunda ngezandi namagama (imizuzu e-1 - 5 ngomsebenzi)

Le misebenzi mayibe mifutshane iqukwe kukuphulaphula nokuthetha, ndawonye nokufunda notitshala.

• Ukuqhwaba kumagama aqhelekileyo, umz. i-bho-khwe, i- ka- ti njalo-njalo ukubonisa amalungu egama

• Ngoncedo lukatitshala, bachonga amagama aphinda-phindwayo emabalini, kwiingoma nezicengcelezo, umz. Idada lidada
 edamini

• Ukuqaphela isininzi, umz. iigusha, iibhokhwe, izinja njalo-njalo

• Ukwakha izivakalisi ezilula kusetyenziswa izandi ezifundiweyo, umz. esebenzisa izandi ezakhiwe ngamaqabane amabini(

 igusha

 isheleni

 ishishini

• Ukulandelelana kwezikhamiso emagameni, umz. kwizininzi nakwizikhuzo (iimfene, oosisi, sii! Yhoo! Aa!)

ILiteresi esakhulayo (imizuzu emi-5-10 kanye okanye kabini ngekota)

Utitshala uza nezixhobo, iiphowusta njalo-njalo eklasini ukuze kubekho okanye kusetyenziswe ezo zifumaneka
kuloo ngingqi yabo. Xa bekutyelelo apho engingqini yabo, uyazikhomba ezo zixhobo abafundise ngazo.

• Ukuqaphela amagama asetyenziswa yonke imihla apho engingqini yabo, umz. ivenkile, isitalato.

• Ukuvelisa iLiteresi esakhulayo, umz. izihloko ngokufunda notitshala
• Ubiza, akhe, abhale amagama esebenzisa amaqabane onke ealfabhethi, oononye noonombini

Ukufunda notitshala (elona lincinane imizuzu engama-25-30 ngeveki nelona likhulu iyure-1 nemizuzu eli-15 ngeveki)

Xa utitshala esebenzisa elona xesha likhulu kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, uqala incwadi enkulu entsha (okanye
iphowusta okanye nayiphina itekisi enamagama amakhulu) iveki nganye aze enze umsebenzi yonke imihla. Xa esebenzisa
elona xesha lincinane ulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, usebenzisa le tekisi inye iiveki ezimbini nezintathu aze enze
umsebenzi kanye okanye kabini ngeveki. Utitshala ufundela iklasi itekisi leyo, akhombe amagama kuxoxwe nangemifanekiso
ngoko kuqhubekayo ebalini. Uphinda alifunde ibali kulo veki, abakhuthaze bangenelele xa efunda. Itekisi le isetyenziswa
ukwazisa isigama esitsha.
• Ukuphulaphula ibali okanye itekisi belinganisa utitshala, bejonge nemifanekiso

• Ukuxoxa ngemifanekiso besebenzisa uLwimi LweNkobe/ LwaseKhaya apho kuyimfuneko

• Ukuchonga izinto emfanekisweni umz. umvundla, indlovu, ihobe, udyakalashe.

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula bencediswa yimifanekiso umz. Ingaba ihobe linawo umsila?

• Ukufunda isigama somlomo umz. ihashe, idonki, ukhetshe, udyakalashe njalo-njalo

• Xa begqibile ukuphinda -phinda ukufunda, bangenelela kumculo weklasi, umz.hobe, hobe, unabani na apho?

• Ukulinganisa ibali besebenzisa ingxoxo yababini

 Iyure e-1 nemizuzu engama -30 ngeveki
UMXHOLO / IINGQIQO / IZAKHONO

Iiveki 1 - 5

Ootitshala mabakhethe izihloko ezibini eziyakubanceda ukwazisa nokubeka isigama esiyakubane sisetyenziswa nokuquka
imisebenzi edweliswe ngezantsi. Qaphela ukuba izihloko ezicetyiswayo ziingcebiso nje. Ootitshala bangakhetha ezabo
ezifanelekileyo ngokwemeko nezixhobo abanazo. Mabaqiniseke ukuba banazo iincwadi ezinkulu eziyimfuneko/ iiphowusta,
izicengcelezo, iingoma, imidlalo nezinto eziphathekayo ngesihloko abasikhethileyo.

sesikweni okucetyiswayo kwiiveki 1 - 5.
Iiveki 6 - 10

Ootitshala mabakhethe izihloko ezibini ezitsha ezizakubavumela ukuphuhlisa esele bekwenzile kwiiveki ezintlanu
ezidlulileyo. Izihloko mazimvumele utitshala azise isigama esitsha ngokubethelela / ngokugxininisa esifundisiweyo.

Izihloko mazimvumele utitshala ngokusoloko ebeka isigama esilula esiyakubuya sisetyenziswe, kwisakhiwo solwimi xa
ethetha nabafundi ngoLwimi OloNgezelelweyo lokuQala.

Ootitshala mabazame ukuquka yonke imisebenzi kangangoko banako. MababaHlole abafundi besebenzisa imisebenzi
yokuHlola okungekho seSikweni okucetyiswayo kwiiveki 6 - 10. Ootitshala mabaHlole abafundi ngokuseSikweni ekupheleni
kweKota.

Imisebenzi yemihla-ngemihla (1 iyure ngeveki)
Mnye nangaphezulu umsebenzi omawenziwe yonke imihla, kuxhomekeke kwixesha elikhoyo:
• Ukuqalisa ukuvelisa isigama somlomo (ukuphulaphula nokuthetha) esebenzisa isihloko esinje ngesi “izinto endinokuzenza”
• Ukuphendula imibuliso elula nokuthi ndlelantle esbenzisa amabinzana anje ngala“hamba kakuhle” “sobonana ngomso.”
• Enze izicelo ezilula umzekelo, “nceda undiphe iglasi yamanzi”.
• Ukukhomba izinto eklasini okanye emfanekisweni ngokuthunywa ngutitshala, umz. “Ndibonise intaka, ikiwu, inkuku, idada,
 inja” njalo-njalo
• Ukuxela izinto eziseklasini okanye emfanekisweni nokuphendula imibuzo katitshala enje ngale, umz. ‘Yintoni le?” “likiwu”.
• Ukwenza ngokomyalelo, umz. “zoba umfanekiso wentaka”.
• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula, eyilinganisa umz.” tsiba, qhwaba, khaba?” njalo-njalo.
• Ukuqonda nokuqalisa ukusebenzisa imigaqo yolwimi elula kumxholo, umz. “ndingatsiba, ndingabaleka, ndingaqhwaba.”
• Ukuchonga umntu, isilwanyana okanye into ngokunika inkcaza korayi-rayi okanye oqashi-qashi, umz. “Ndinemilenze
 emibini, amaphiko amabini neentsiba, ndingabhabha. Ndiyintoni?”

 - Uyintaka.
• Ukucula iingoma ezilula enze nezilinganiso ngokuncediswa, umz. “Ndimbonil’uthekwane, Ebezibuk’emanzini.
• Ukuthatha inxaxheba kwizicengcelezo neengoma esenza izilinganiso, umz. “amahotyazana mahlanu emthini,
 kwabhabha lalinye kwasala amane”.
• Ukudlala imidlalo ngolwimi, umz. omnye ulinganisa ukukhala kwentaka, emva koko ababuze imibuzo, umz. Yintaka ni le
 ikhalayo? Abanye baqikelele, “yinkuku, lidada, likiwu, ngukhetshe” njalo-njalo.

 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
Imisebenzi egxininise kuphulaphulo nokuthetha (imizuzu eli-15 kabini ngeveki)

Ukuphulaphula amabali abaliswayo nafundwayo

Kabini ngeveki, utitshala ufunda okanye ubalisa ibali. Amabali afundiweyo angalinganiswa kusetyenziswe izijekulo
ukubonisa inkcazelo. Amabali afundiswayo mawabe kwiincwadi ezinkulu nakwiphowusta apho bangabona khona
imifanekiso.
• Ukuphulaphula ngomdla amabali amafutshane afundwa kwincwadi enkulu okanye kwiphowusta ndawonye nezinye iitekisi,
 bazebangenelele kwikhorasi, umz. Isikhukukazi namantshontsho aso.
• Ukuqonda nokuphendula imibuzo elula, nemiyalelo umz. ndibonise ibhabhathane” “ndibonise inkuku”
• Ukuxela izinto ezinye izinto emfanekisweni, ukuphendula imibuzo katitshala, umz. “yintoni le?” “libhabhathane”.

Ukuvelisa imixholo, isigama, nesakhiwo solwimi.

Ngokuthatha inxaxheba kule misebenzi ingentla:
• Ukuqhubeleka ngokwakha isigama somlomo kuquka isigama esinengqiqo, umz. ukwazi ukwenza-amabhabhathane
 ayabhabha.
• Ukuqalisa ukuvelisa ukuqonda nokukwazi ukusebenzisa izakhiwo zolwimi ezilula kwimeko yolwimi oluthethwayo
 olunentsingiselo, umz. izichazi, umz. “inkulu - incinane”, izalathisi, umz. “lo, lowo, lowa” nezihlomelo zobunjani,umz.
 ndingabaleka ngokukhawuleza, andinakubhabha.
UKUHLOLA

Iingcebiso zomsebenzi wokuhlola okungekho sesikweni:

Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha: (ezomlomo kunye/okanye nezenziwayo)

Iiveki 1 - 5
• Ukuqonda nokuphendula imibuzo elula, nemiyalelo umz. ndibonise ibhabhathane” “ndibonise inkuku”
• Ukubeka intetho yakhe ngamabinzana alula, umz. ndingatsiba.

Iiveki 6 - 10
• Ukubeka intetho yakhe ngamabinzana alula, umz. ndingataka ndixhuma-xhume
• Ukuchonga umntu, isilwanyana okanye intaka ngokunika inkcaza koorayi-rayi okanye ooqashi-qashi, umz.
 “Ndisisinambuzane esincinane, ndimnyama, ndisela igazi, ndiyabhabha ebusuku. Ndingubani?”
 - Uyingcongconi.

Iingcebiso zomsebenzi wokuhlola okusesikweni: 4

Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha (ezomlomo kunye/okanye nezenziwayo)
• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula, nemiyalelo, umz. bala uyekuma kwi-10
• Ukubeka intetho yakhe ngamabinzana alula, ndingalibhala igama lam.
• Ukubonisa ukuqonda isigama somlomo esilula ngokukhomba izinto eklasini okanye emfanekisweni ngokuthunywa
 ngutitshala
• Ukuqonda amagama angama-700 ubuncinane ngokomxholo kwimeko apho elona lincinane ixesha linikwa uLwimi
 lokuQala elongezelelwyo aze abeli- 1000 amagama apho elona xesha lininzi linikwa uLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo

 40 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

 IKOTA 3
 UKUFUNDA NEZANDI IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDA

 Elona lincinane: imizuzu engama-30 minutes ngeveki

 Elona lininzi: iyure e-1 nemizuzu eli-15 ngeveki
UMXHOLO / IINGQIQO / IZAKHONO

Ukufunda ngezandi namagama (imizuzu e-1 - 5 ngomsebenzi)

Le misebenzi mayibe mifutshane iqukwe kukuphulaphula nokuthetha, ndawonye nokufunda notitshala.
• Ukuqhwaba kumagama aqhelekileyo, umz. i-bho-khwe, i- ka- ti njalo-njalo ukubonisa amalungu egama
• Ngoncedo lukatitshala, bachonga amagama aphinda-phindwayo emabalini, kwiingoma nezicengcelezo, umz.
Idada lidada edamini
• Ukuqalisa ukuchonga izandi ezahluka-hlukeneyo zokuqala emagameni, umz. “d” idonki, “k” ikati
• Ukuqaphela isininzi, umz. iigusha, iibhokhwe, izinja njalo-njalo
• Ukwakha izivakalisi ezilula kusetyenziswa izandi ezifundiweyo, umz. esebenzisa izandi ezakhiwe ngamaqabane amabini(
 oonombini. umz sh

 igusha

 isheleni

 ishishini
• Ukwakha izivakalisi ezilula kusetyenziswa izandi ezifundiweyo
• Ukulandelelana kwezikhamiso emagameni, umz. kwizininzi nakwizikhuzo (iimfene, oosisi, sii! Yhoo! Aa!)

ILiteresi esakhulayo (imizuzu emi-5-10 kanye okanye kabini ngekota)

Utitshala uza nezixhobo, iiphowusta njalo-njalo eklasini ukuze kubekho okanye kusetyenziswe ezo zifumaneka
kuloo ngingqi yabo. Xa bekutyelelo apho engingqini yabo, uyazikhomba ezo zixhobo abafundise ngazo.
• Ukuqaphela amagama asetyenziswa yonke imihla apho engingqini yabo, umz. ivenkile, isitalato.
• Ukuvelisa iLiteresi esakhulayo, umz. izihloko ngokufunda notitshala
• Ubiza, akhe, abhale amagama esebenzisa amaqabane onke ealfabhethi, oononye noonombini

Ukufunda notitshala (elona lincinane imizuzu engama-25-30 ngeveki nelona likhulu iyure-1 nemizuzu eli-15 ngeveki)

Xa utitshala esebenzisa elona xesha likhulu kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, uqala incwadi enkulu entsha ( okanye
iphowusta okanye nayiphina itekisi enamagama amakhulu) iveki nganye aze enze umsebenzi yonke imihla. Xa esebenzisa
elona xesha lincinane ulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, usebenzisa le tekisi inye iiveki ezimbini nezintathu aze enze
umsebenzi kanye okanye kabini ngeveki. Utitshala ufundela iklasi itekisi leyo, akhombe amagama kuxoxwe nangemifanekiso
ngoko kuqhubekayo ebalini. Uphinda alifunde ibali kulo veki, abakhuthaze bangenelele xa efunda. Itekisi le isetyenziswa
ukwazisa isigama esitsha.
• Ukuphulaphula ibali okanye itekisi belinganisa utitshala, bejonge nemifanekiso
• Ukuxoxa ngemifanekiso besebenzisa uLwimi LweNkobe/ LwaseKhaya apho kuyimfuneko
• Ukuchonga izinto emfanekisweni umz. umvundla, indlovu, ihobe, udyakalashe.
• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula bencediswa yimifanekiso umz. Ingaba ihobe linawo umsila?
• Ukufunda isigama somlomo umz. ihashe, idonki, ukhetshe, udyakalashe njalo-njalo
• Xa begqibile ukuphinda -phinda ukufunda, bangenelela kumculo weklasi, umz.hobe, hobe, unabani na apho?
• Ukulinganisa ibali besebenzisa ingxoxo yababini
• Ukuzoba umfanekiso ngoyena ndoqo webali
