• Ukuqaphela isininzi, umz. iigusha, iibhokhwe, izinja njalo-njalo

Ukufunda: (ezomlomo okanye nezenziwayo)
• Ukuchonga abantu, izilwanyana nezinto kwimizobo ekwincwadi ezinkulu (nakwezinye iitekisi ezinemibhalo emikhulu)
• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula ngomfanekiso okanye ibali okanye ngetekisi
• Ukuzoba umfanekiso ngoyena ndoqo webali
Umsebenzi wokuhlola okusesikweni 3

Ukufunda: (Okomlomo/ okwenziwayo)
• Ukuqaphela isininzi, umz. iigusha, iibhokhwe, izinja njalo-njalo
• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula ngomfanekiso okanye ibali
• Ukubonisa ulwazi ngesigama ebalini ngokukhomba izinto emifanekisweni belandela imiyalelo katitshala
• Ubiza, akhe, abhale amagama esebenzisa amaqabane onke ealfabhethi, oononye noonombini
• Ukwakha izivakalisi ezilula kusetyenziswa izandi ezifundiweyo, umz. esebenzisa izandi ezakhiwe ngamaqabane amabini(
 oonombini)
• Ukwakha izivakalisi ezilula kusetyenziswa izandi ezifundiweyo
• Ukulandelelana kwezikhamiso emagameni, umz. kwizininzi nakwizikhuzo (iimfene, oosisi, sii! Yhoo! Aa!)

 42 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

 IKOTA 3
UKUBHALA IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDA

 Elona lincinane: Alikho

 Elona lininzi: imizuzu eli-15 ngeveki
UMXHOLO / IINGQIQO / IZAKHONO

Ukubhala notitshala (elona lininzi - kanye ngeveki)

Ukubhala okokuqala kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo maluncediswe ngutitshala
• Ngoncedo lukatitshala ubhala isihloko somzobo wakhe aze afunde oko akubhalileyo
• Ngoncedo lukatitshala ubhala uluhlu lwezinto, umz. izilwanyana: inja, ikati, imfene, indlulamthi njalo-njalo, iziqhamo:
 iapile, ibhanana, imengo, iorenjalo njalo-njalo.
UKUHLOLA

Iingcebiso zomsebenzi wokuhlola okungekho sesikweni:

Ukubhala: (okubhalwayo)
• Ukukhuphela isihloko somfanekiso awuzobileyo aze afunde oko akubhalileyo
• Ukubhala uluhlu lwezinto ngokwezihloko

CAPS 43
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

 IKOTA 4
UKUPHULAPHULA NOKUTHETHA ( EZOMLOMO) IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDA

 Iyure e-1 nemizuzu engama -30 ngeveki
UMXHOLO / IINGQIQO / IZAKHONO

Iiveki 1 - 5

Ootitshala mabakhethe izihloko ezibini eziyakubanceda ukwazisa nokubeka isigama esiyakubane sisetyenziswa nokuquka
imisebenzi edweliswe ngezantsi. Qaphela ukuba izihloko ezicetyiswayo ziingcebiso nje. Ootitshala bangakhetha ezabo
ezifanelekileyo ngokwemeko nezixhobo abanazo. Mabaqiniseke ukuba banazo iincwadi ezinkulu eziyimfuneko/ iiphowusta,
izicengcelezo, iingoma, imidlalo nezinto eziphathekayo ngesihloko abasikhethileyo.

Mabazame ukuquka yonke imisebenzi kangangoko. MababaHlole abafundi Besebenzisa imisebenzi yokuHlola okungekho
sesikweni okucetyiswayo kwiiveki 1 - 5.

Iiveki 6 - 10

Ootitshala mabakhethe izihloko ezibini ezitsha ezizakubavumela ukuphuhlisa esele bekwenzile kwiiveki ezintlanu
ezidlulileyo. Izihloko mazimvumele utitshala azise isigama esitsha ngokubethelela / ngokugxininisa esifundisiweyo.

Izihloko mazimvumele utitshala ngokusoloko ebeka isigama esilula esiyakubuya sisetyenziswe, kwisakhiwo solwimi xa
ethetha nabafundi ngoLwimi OloNgezelelweyo lokuQala.

Ootitshala mabazame ukuquka yonke imisebenzi kangangoko banako. MababaHlole abafundi besebenzisa imisebenzi
yokuHlola okungekho seSikweni okucetyiswayo kwiiveki 6 - 10. Ootitshala mabaHlole abafundi ngokuseSikweni ekupheleni
kweKota.

Imisebenzi yemihla-ngemihla (1 iyure ngeveki)

Mnye nangaphezulu umsebenzi omawenziwe yonke imihla, kuxhomekeke kwixesha elikhoyo:

• Ukuqalisa ukuvelisa isigama somlomo (ukuphulaphula nokuthetha) esebenzisa isihloko esinje ngesi “izinto endinokuzenza”

• Ukuphendula imibuliso elula nokuthi ndlelantle esbenzisa amabinzana anje ngala“hamba kakuhle” “sobonana ngomso.”

• Enze izicelo ezilula umzekelo, “nceda undiphe iglasi yamanzi”.

• Ukukhomba izinto eklasini okanye emfanekisweni ngokuthunywa ngutitshala, umz. “Ndibonise intaka, ikiwu, inkuku, idada, 
 inja” njalo-njalo

• Ukuxela izinto eziseklasini okanye emfanekisweni nokuphendula imibuzo katitshala enje ngale, umz. ‘Yintoni le?” “likiwu”.

• Ukwenza ngokomyalelo, umz. “zoba umfanekiso wentaka”.

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula, eyilinganisa umz.”tsiba, qhwaba, khaba?” njalo-njalo.

• Ukuqonda nokuqalisa ukusebenzisa imigaqo yolwimi elula kumxholo, umz. “ndingatsiba, ndingabaleka, ndingaqhwaba.”

• Ukuchonga umntu, isilwanyana okanye into ngokunika inkcaza korayi-rayi okanye oqashi-qashi, umz. “Ndinemilenze
 emibini, amaphiko amabini neentsiba, ndingabhabha. Ndiyintoni?”

 - Uyintaka.

• Ukucula iingoma ezilula enze nezilinganiso ngokuncediswa, umz. “Ndimbonil’uthekwane, Ebezibuk’emanzini.

• Ukuthatha inxaxheba kwizicengcelezo neengoma esenza izilinganiso, umz. “amahotyazana mahlanu emthini,
 kwabhabha lalinye kwasala amane”.

• Ukudlala imidlalo ngolwimi, umz. omnye ulinganisa ukukhala kwentaka, emva koko ababuze imibuzo, umz. Yintaka ni le
 ikhalayo? Abanye baqikelele, “yinkuku, lidada, likiwu, ngukhetshe” njalo-njalo.

Umsebenzi wokuhlola okusesikweni 4

Ukufunda: (Okomlomo/ okwenziwayo)

• Ukuqaphela isininzi, umz. iintaka, amabhabhathane, ookhetshe njalo-njalo

• Ukuphendula imibuzo elula ngomfanekiso okanye ibali

• Ukubonisa ulwazi ngesigama ebalini ngokukhomba izinto emifanekisweni belandela imiyalelo katitshala

• Ukwakha izivakalisi ezilula kusetyenziswa izandi ezifundiweyo, umz. esebenzisa izandi ezakhiwe ngamaqabane amabini(
 oonombini)

• Ukwakha izivakalisi ezilula kusetyenziswa izandi ezifundiweyo

Ukubhala okokuqala kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo maluncediswe ngutitshala

• Ngoncedo lukatitshala ubhala isihloko somzobo wakhe aze afunde oko akubhalileyo

• Ngoncedo lukatitshala ubhala uluhlu lwezinto, umz. izinto ezibhabhayo: intaka, ibhabhathane, inqwelo-ntaka, inyosi njalo-
 njalo,

Ukubhala: (okubhalwayo)

• Ukukhuphela isihloko somfanekiso awuzobileyo aze afunde oko akubhalileyo

• Ukubhala uluhlu olulula lwezinto ngokwezihloko

 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
IITEKISI EZINOKUSETYENZISWA / OOVIMBA BOLWAZI:

 UKUPHULAPHULA NOKUTHETHA
 • Imifanekiso neepowusta
 • Izinto ezinxulumene nezihloko nemixholo ethile, iiphaphethi, iimaskhi, njalo njalo
 • Imifanekiso eza kulandelelaniswa (pictures to sequence)
 • Iikhostyum ezenziweyo ukulungiselela ukulinganisa (role play) nokwenza (acting out) amabali
 • IiCD okanye iiteyiphu ezimanyelwayo ezinamabali (afundwayo okanye abaliswayo), imibongo, izicengcelezo neengoma,
 isidlali seeCD okanye isishicileli seteyiphu (tape recorder) umabonwakude neeteyiphu zevidiyo/iiDVD
 • Iincwadi zamabali namabali abaliswa ngomlomo
 • Imibongo, iingoma nezicengcelezo
 • Imidlalo yolwimi (eyenziwa ngomlomo)
 UKUFUNDA NEZANDI
 • Imifanekiso neepowusta
 • IiNcwadi eziNkulu - zamabali aqwetyiweyo nayinyani
 • Uluhlu lweencwadi ekucetyiswa ukuba zifundwe (amabali asi-7 -10)
 • Ezinye iitekisi ezandisiweyo (imibongo, iingoma, izicengcelezo, njalo njalo)
 • Iincwadi ezinemifanekiso eneengcaciso ezibhalwe phantsi kwazo (caption books)
 • Iincwadi ‘zokuzonwabisa’ ezimfutshane ezinesivakalisi esi-1 - 2 kwiphepha ngalinye, ezilungiselelwe ikona yokufundela
 • Oonotsheluza (flash card labels) bezinto eziseklasini, izinto ezibonisiweyo namagama abonwa rhoqo
 • Iitshathi zealfabhethi
 • Izinto zokukhomba eziza kusetyenziswa xa kufundwa iitekisi ezandisiweyo, amabali odonga (aseludongeni), izinto
 ezibonisiweyo
 • Izichazi-magama zabafundi (ezinolwimi olunye nezineelwimi ezimbini)
 UKUBHALA NOKUBHALA NGESANDLA
 • Izixhobo zokubhala ezifana neepensile, iipensile ezinemibala, iikhrayoni ezibukhandlelarha (wax crayons), iphepha
 elingabhalwanga elibubukhulu ngobukhulu (A3, A4, A5), irula, irabha, iincwadi zokubhalela ezingabhalwanga (blank jotters)
 • Iifliphutshathi neeKhokhi ezibhala ngqindilili

 IBANGA 2 ISIXHOSA ULWIMI LOKUQALA OloNgezelelweyo

 IIMFUNO ZEKOTA
 IKOTA 1
 UKUPHULAPHULA NOKUTHETHA (EZOMLOMOI) IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDA

 Elona lincinane: Imizuzu engama-45

 Elona lininzi: Iyure e-1 ngeveki
UMXHOLO/INGQIQO/IZAKHONO

Iiveki 1 - 5

Ootitshala bakhetha izihloko ezibini eziya kubavumela ukuba bazise yaye bamane besebenzisa isigama (recycle
vocabulary), yaye benze le misebenzi idweliswe apha ngezantsi. Qaphela ukuba ezi zihloko zicetyiswayo ziingcebiso nje.
Ootitshala mabazikhethele imixholo yabo efanelekileyo ngokuxhomekeke kwimeko nezixhobo ezikhoyo. Mabaqiniseke ukuba
baneeNcwadi eziNkulu/iipowusta, izicengcelezo, iingoma, imidlalo nezinto zenyani ezaneleyo zeso sihloko basikhethileyo.

Ootitshala mabazame ukwenza imisebenzi emininzi kangangoko ngexesha elikhoyo. Mabafake umsebenzi omtsha -
ukuphulaphula amabalana nokubalisa amabalana. Utitshala makaqale akubonise oku ngokubalisa ibalana (umz. lento
ayenze ngempela-veki). Emva koko kufuneka abaxhase abafundi ekubaliseni awabo amabali. Makanike amagama ayalelayo
ukuncedisa abafundi, umzekelo, ‘Kule veki iphelileyo bendi ….ndaza nda....emva koko… Emva koko ndiya nda ….’ Kufuneka
abancedise nangemo emfutshane yexesha elidlulileyo (umz. Ndihambe...). Abafundi baya kuziqhelisa ukuphulaphula
nokubalisa amabalana abo unyaka wonke. Le nto iya kubanika ithuba lokusebenzisa ixesha elidlulileyo nezihlanganisi
ezibonisa amaxesha (okukuqala, okulandelayo, emva koko) kwiimeko ezithile. Ngesinye isikhokelo jonga kwiCandelo lesi-4
ekupheleni kolu xwebhu.

Kufuneka utitshala ahlole abafundi esebenzisa imiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho sesikweni ecetyisiweyo yeeveki 1-5.
Iiveki 6 - 10

Ootitshala bakhetha izihloko ezitsha ezibini eziya kubenza bakwazi ukwakhela koko sele kwenziwe kwiiveki zokuqala
ezintlanu. Ezi zihloko mazenze ukuba afake isigama esitsha yaye agxininise kweso sigama sele sifundisiwe. Ezi zihloko
mazimvumele utitshala ukuba amane esebenzisa izakhiwo zolwimi ezilula nezisisiseko xa ethetha nabafundi ngoLwimi
oloNgezelelweyo lokuQala.

Ootitshala mabaqiniseke ukuba bayenza yonke imisebenzi, ibe ngaphezu komsebenzi omnye ukuba kuyenzeka. Mabahlole
abafundi besebenzisa imiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho sesikweni ecetyisiweyo yeeVeki 6 - 10. Ootitshala mabenzise
imiSebenzi yokuHlola okuseSikweni ekupheleni kwekota.

Imisebenzi yemihla ngemihla (imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki)

Le misebenzi iyahluka ngokobude. Utitshala usenokwenza umsebenzi omnye omdana kungenjalo imisebenzi emibini okanye
emithathu emifutshanana ngeveki.
• Uyaqhuba nokukhulisa isigama somlomo (ukuphulaphula nokuthetha) esebenzisa izihloko ezifana ‘neeMvakalelo’
• Ulandela uluhlu olufutshane lwemiyalelo, umzekelo. ‘Zoba ubuso obonwabileyo. Ngoku ke zoba ubuso obungonwabanga.’
• Unika imiyalelo emifutshane, umzekelo, ‘Zoba ubuso obonwabileyo’.
• Uqonda aphendule imibuzo elula efana nothi, ‘Yeyiphi ...?’ ‘Yekabani ...?’ Yekabani le ncwadi? Ufuna eliphi ibali?)
• Wenza izicelo neentetho ezilula, umzekelo, ‘Ndinxaniwe. Ndicela iglasi yamanzi.’
• Uchonga into ngokulandela ingcaciso yomlomo elula, umzekelo, ‘Intombazana ikhathazekile. Iyalila.’ Abafundi
 mabadibanise le ngcaciso nomfanekiso ochanekileyo.
• Uthetha ngezinto ezisemfanekisweni ngokomyalelo katitshala, umzekelo, ‘Ubona ntoni emfanekisweni?’ ‘Ndixelele into
 oyibona emfanekisweni.’
• Uphulaphula ibalana elilula, umzekelo, utitshala ubalisa ngento ayenze ngempelaveki edlulileyo
• Ubalisa ibalana elilula, encediswa ngutitshala, umzekelo, ubalisa ngento ayenze ngempelaveki edlulileyo
• Ugcina engqondweni enze imibongo elula, izicengcelezo ezinentshukumo neengoma, umzekelo, Sifikil’ ezibukweni (x2),
 Tancu, tancu siwelile, Masisele emanzini!
• Udlala imidlalo yolwimi, umzekelo uqikelela ukuba umntu uziva njani ngokujonga imbonakalo neentshukumo azenzayo

 50 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

Imisebenzi egxininise ekuphulaphuleni nasekuthetheni (ubuncinane imizuzu eli-15 ka-1 ngeveki, kodwa ingegqithi
kwimizuzu eli-15 ka-2 ngeveki)

Uphulaphula amabali abaliswayo nafundwayo (ukuFunda noTitshala)

Kanye okanye kabini ngeveki, ngokuxhomekeke kwixesha elifumanekayo, utitshala ubalisa okanye ufunda ibali (okanye
abalise ngezehlo ezehlileyo). Amabali abaliswayo angalinganiswa kusetyenziswa iintshukumo nezincedisi (props) ukuxhasa
intsingiselo. Amabali afundwayo makathathwe eNcwadini eNkulu okanye ipowusta enemizobo apho bonke abafundi
bayibonayo imifanekiso.
• Uphulaphula amabali amafutshane, amabalana abaliswayo okanye iitekisi eziyinyani ezibaliswayo okanye ezifundwa
 eNcwadini eNkulu okanye kwipowusta enemizobo, umzekelo, Intsomi kaDyakalashe noHobe, ewonwabela athathe
 inxaxheba kwiindawana eziphinda-phindwayo ngamaxesha afanelekileyo.
• Uva imiyalelo ayenze, umzekelo, ‘Khawundibonise uDyakalashe ophantsi komthi.’
• Uphendula, ngeempendulo ezimfutshane, imibuzo elula ecacileyo malunga nebali, umzekelo ‘Likhwele phi iHobe? Ufuna
 ukutya ntoni uDyakalashe?’
• Unika amagama ezinto ezisemfanekisweni ephendula imibuzo katitshala, umzekelo, ‘Yintoni le?’ ‘Yintlanzi.’
• Ulinganisa ibali, esebenzisa inxalenye yencoko yababini

Ukukhuliswa kwengqiqo, isigama nezakhiwo zolwimi

Ngokuthatha inxaxheba kule misebenzi ingentla:
• uyaqhuba nokwakha isigama somlomo, kuquka isigama esibonisa ingqiqo, umzekelo, ukuthetha ngeemvakalelo
• uqala ukwakha ukuqonda nokukwazi ukusebenzisa izakhiwo zolwimi ezilula ezisemxholweni wolwimi oluthethwayo
 olunentsingiselo, umzekelo, uyaqonda aqale ukusebenzisa ixesha elidlulileyo, umzekelo, ‘Bendiye’; izihlanganisi ezibonisa
 ixesha, umzekelo, ‘okokuqala’, ‘emva koko’, ‘kuze kulandele’
UKUHLOLA

Iingcebiso zemiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho seSikweni:

UkuPhulaphula nokuThetha (okuthethwayo kunye/okanye okwenziwayo)

Iiveki 1 - 5
• Uchonga umfanekiso ngokulandela ingcaciso yomlomo elula, umzekelo, ‘Inkwenkwe yonwabile, incumile.’
• Uphulaphula abalise amabalana alula aze aphendule imibuzo emalunga nawo, umzekelo ‘Waqala ngokwenza ntoni
 uMandla? Waza wenza ntoni emva koko?, njalo njalo.’

Iiveki 6 - 10
• Ulandela uluhlu lwemiyalelo, umzekelo. ‘Zoba ubuso obonwabileyo. Ngoku ke zoba ubuso obukhathazekileyo.
 Okokugqibela, zoba ubuso obucaphukayo.’
• Ubalisa ibalana elilula ngomlomo: NgeCawa, ndivuke ngentsimbi yesi-8. Ndahlamba. Emva koko ndiye ndanxiba iimpahla
 zam. Ndaza ndaya enkonzweni.

UMsebenzi woku-1 oCetyiswayo wokuHlola okuseSikweni:

UkuPhulaphula nokuThetha (okuthethwayo kunye/okanye okwenziwayo)
• Uphulaphula abalise amabalana alula aze aphendule imibuzo emalunga nawo, umzekelo ‘Waqala ngokwenza ntoni
 umakhulu? Waza wenza ntoni emva koko?, njalo njalo.’
• Ubonisa ukuqonda isigama esithile esisisiseko somlomo ngokukhomba abantu abasemfanekisweni okanye enze izenzo
 ayalelwa ukuba makazenze ngutitshala, umzekelo, Khawenze ngathi awonwabanga/wonwabile/uyacaphuka/udiniwe, njalo
 njalo. Khomba umfanekiso womntu ongonwabanga/owonwabileyo/onomsindo/odiniweyo, njalo njalo.

Ulwazi lwezandi namagama (imizuzu eli-15 ngeveki)

Ootitshala bahlaziya ulwazi loononye, beqala ngonxulumano oluphakathi kwesandi nonobumba olufanayo nolo loLwimi /
lwaseKhaya nezibalulekileyo esiXhoseni. Bakha amagama amafutshane aqhelekileyo besebenzisa izandi abafundi abasele
bezazi kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo. Le misebenzi mayibe mifutshane (imizuzu emi-5 - 10); isenokudityaniswa
kwimisebenzi yokuPhulaphula nokuThetha nokuFunda.
• Wahlula phakathi kwezandi ezidla ngokubhidaniswa ngokuziva ngokuziphulaphula (umz. ‘hl’ no’dl’ - ‘hlala’, ‘dlala’)
• Ukhetha unxulumano phakathi kwesandi nonobumba abazimeleyo (umz. a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h, i, l, m, n, o, p, q, r, s, t, v, w, y)
• Wakha acazulule amagama anoonobumba aba-3 esebenzisa izandi azifundileyo. (umz. m-n-a, m-na, mna; e-w-e, e-we,
 ewe)
• Ukuhlaziya izandi zoononye noonombini abafundwe kwibanga 1
• Ukwakha amagama esebenzisa izandi ezakhiwe ngamaqabane amathathu (oonontathu) umz.

 ngc ndl ntl

 ingca indlala intlanzi

 ingcongconi indlela intlaka

 ukungcola indlebe intloko

 indlovu

 indlu

 indlulamthi

 ingxolo, intsimbi, tshayela tshisa, ingqaka, ingqele, ingqondo, ingqolowa njalo-njalo

 Qala ngamagama aqhelekileyo. Upelo nobizelo luyinxalenye yoku.
• Ukwakha amagama esebenzisa izandi ezakhiwe ngamaqabane amane ( oonone) umz.

 ngcw

 ingcwaba

 ingcwele
• Wakha acazulule amagama anoonobumba aba-3 esebenzisa izandi azifundileyo. (umz. m-n-a, m-na, mna; e-w-e, e-we,
 ewe)
• Uqaphela ukuphela ngokufanayo kwamagama (umz. ‘am’ - ‘kwam’, ‘eni’-’esikolweni’)
• Ubeka amagama afanayo ngokwamaqela amagama (umz. yam, zam, bam)

UkuFunda noTitshala (ixesha lako libekwe phantsi kokuPhulaphula nokuThetha)

UkuFunda noTitshala ngumsebenzi wokufunda nokuphulaphula; kukwaquka ukuthetha kuba abafundi bathetha ngetekisi
notitshala wabo. KwiBanga lesi-2, ukuFunda noTitshala kuya kuqhuba, kodwa sele kuyinxalenye yokuPhulaphula
nokuThetha.

UkuFunda kwamaQela okuKhokelwayo (ubuncinane bemizuzu engama-30 nelona lininzi leyure nemizuzu eli-15 ngeveki)

Ootitshala mabazise ukuFunda kwamaQela okuKhokelwayo kule kota. Ootitshala abasebenzisa elona lininzi kulwimi
lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo mabohlule iklasi ibe ngamaqela okufunda ama-5 ohlulwe ngokokulingana kokukwazi ukufunda
baze basebenze neqela ngalinye imizuzu eli-15.

 52 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

Ngoku utitshala asasebenza neli qela amanye amaqela aya kufundisana eqeleni okanye abafundi bafundelane ngezibini,
besebenzisa iitekisi abaziqhelileyo okanye imisebenzi enxulumene netekisi. Ezi ncwadi mazibe lula kakhulu zinokuphinda-
phindwa kwezakhiwo nesigama kunye nemifanekiso exhasa itekisi. Abafundi sele bekwenzile ukuFunda okuKhokelwayo
kuLwimi lwabo / lwaseKhaya ekuqaleni kweBanga loku-1, ngoko ke bafanele ukuba sele beyiqhelile le ndlela.

Ootitshala abasebenzisa elona lincinane kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, apho banemizuzu engama-30 kuphela,
mabenze umsebenzi wokufunda okukhokelwayo kweklasi kabini ngeveki.
• Ufundela ngaphandle (ngokukhwazayo) encwadini yakhe ekufundeni kweqela okukhokelwayo efunda notitshala. Iqela
 lonke lifunda ibali elifanayo okanye itekisi eyinyani, lifunda kunye notitshala
• Usebenzisa iindlela zokufunda ezifundiswe kuLwimi / lwaseKhaya esenzela ukuqonda itekisi nokuzihlola xa efunda (izandi,
 izikhokelo zeemeko, uhlalutyo lwesakhiwo, amagama abonwa rhoqo)
• Ufunda ngokuqhabalaka okwandayo
• Ubonisa ukuqonda uphawulo lwentetho (punctuation) xa efundela ngaphandle
• Uqalisa ukuzakhela uluhlu lwamagama abonwa rhoqo ewathatha ekufundeni okukhokelwayo, ukufunda notitshala
 nokuzifundela yedwa

Ukuzifundela (ngexesha abangenzi nto ngalo abafundi esikolweni okanye ekhaya)

Abafundi mabakhuthazelwe ukuba bazifundele ngoLwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo lokuQala xa benexesha abangenzi nto
ngalo eklasini (umz. xa beqale bagqiba ukwenza umsebenzi) nasekhaya (umz. umsebenzi wasekhaya). Kubalulekile ukuba
onke amathuba afumanekayo eklasini asetyenziselwe ukuphuhlisa ukufunda kwabo.
• Ufunda into ayibhalileyo nebhalwe ngabanye
• Uzifundela ngoLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo iincwadi engancediswa ngethuba lokuFunda okuKhokelwayo afunde
 neencwadi ezilula ezicacisa imifanekiso (caption books) neencwadi zamabali abaliswa ngemifanekiso (picture story books)
 afumaneka kwikona yokufunda yaseklasini
• Usebenzisa izichazi-magama zabafundi ezinemifanekiso (zolwimi olunye nezimbini) ukukhangela intsingiselo yamagama
 angawaziyo
UKUHLOLA

Iingcebiso zemiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho seSikweni:

Izandi: (okuthethwayo kunye/okanye okwenziwayo)
• Wakha acazulule amagama anoonobumba aba-3 esebenzisa izandi azifundileyo. (umz. m-n-a, m-na, mna; e-w-e, e-we,
 ewe)

UMsebenzi oCetyiswayo wokuHlola okuseSikweni:

Izandi: (okuthethwayo kunye/okanye okwenziwayo)
• Ukhetha unxulumano phakathi kwesandi nonobumba abaninzi abazimeleyo (umz. a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h, i, l, m, n, o, p, q, r, s,
 t, v, w, y)
• Ukwakha amagama esebenzisa izandi ezakhiwe ngamaqabane angoononye, oonombini, oonontathu noonone

Iingcebiso zemiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho seSikweni:

• Ubonisa ukuqonda uphawulo lwentetho (punctuation) xa efundela ngaphandle

 utitshala

UMXHOLO/INGQIQO/IZAKHONO

Iiveki 1 - 5

Ootitshala bakhetha izihloko ezibini eziya kubavumela ukuba bazise yaye bamane besebenzisa isigama (recycle vocabulary),
yaye benze le misebenzi idweliswe apha ngezantsi. Qaphela ukuba ezi zihloko zicetyiswayo ziingcebiso nje. Ootitshala
mabazikhethele imixholo yabo efanelekileyo ngokuxhomekeke kwimeko nezixhobo ezikhoyo. Mabaqiniseke ukuba
baneeNcwadi eziNkulu/iipowusta, izicengcelezo, iingoma, imidlalo nezinto zenyani ezaneleyo zeso sihloko basikhethileyo.

Ootitshala mabazame ukwenza yonke imisebenzi, ibe ngaphezu komsebenzi omnye ukuba kuyenzeka. Mabahlole abafundi
besebenzisa imiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho sesikweni ecetyisiweyo yeeVeki 1-5.
Iiveki 6 - 10

Ootitshala bakhetha izihloko ezitsha ezibini eziya kubenza bakwazi ukwakhela koko sele kwenziwe kwiiveki zokuqala ezintlanu.
Ezi zihloko mazenze ukuba afake isigama esitsha yaye agxininise kweso sigama sele sifundisiwe. Ezi zihloko mazimvumele
utitshala ukuba amane esebenzisa izakhiwo zolwimi ezilula nezisisiseko xa ethetha nabafundi ngoLwimi oloNgezelelweyo
lokuQala.

Ootitshala mabaqiniseke ukuba bayenza yonke imisebenzi, ibe ngaphezu komsebenzi omnye ukuba kuyenzeka. Mabahlole
abafundi besebenzisa imiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho sesikweni ecetyisiweyo yeeVeki 6-10. Ootitshala mabenzise
imiSebenzi yokuHlola okuseSikweni ekupheleni kwekota.

Imisebenzi yemihla ngemihla (imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki)
Le misebenzi iyahluka ngokobude. Utitshala usenokwenza umsebenzi omnye omdana kungenjalo imisebenzi emibini okanye
emithathu emifutshanana ngeveki.
• Uyaqhuba nokukhulisa isigama somlomo (ukuphulaphula nokuthetha) esebenzisa izihloko ezifana ‘neeMvakalelo’
• Ulandela uluhlu olufutshane lwemiyalelo, umzekelo. ‘Phakama. Yiya emnyango uvule ucango.’
• Unika imiyalelo emifutshane, umzekelo, ‘Vula ucango’. ‘Vala ucango.’
• Uqonda aphendule imibuzo elula efana nothi, ‘Yeyiphi ...?’ ‘Yekabani ...?’ (Sesikabani esi sihlangu?)
• Wenza izicelo neentetho ezilula, umzekelo, ‘Kuyabanda. Ndicela ukuthatha ijezi.’
• Uchonga into ngokulandela ingcaciso yomlomo elula, umzekelo, ‘Kusibekele.’ Abafundi mabadibanise le ngcaciso
 nomfanekiso ochanekileyo.)
• Uthetha ngezinto ezisemfanekisweni ngokomyalelo katitshala, umzekelo, ‘Ubona ntoni emfanekisweni?’ ‘Ndixelele into
 oyibona emfanekisweni.’
• Uphulaphula ibalana elilula, umzekelo, utitshala ebalisa ngotyelelo elwandle ngosuku olutshisayo.
• Ubalisa ibalana elilula, encediswa ngutitshala, umzekelo, ubalisa ngohambo lwakhe.
• Ugcina engqondweni enze imibongo elula, izicengcelezo ezinentshukumo neengoma, umzekelo, Mntakwethu (x2), Ulele
 na? (x2) Kubethwa intsimbi (x2) Khelekenkce! (x2)
• Udlala imidlalo yolwimi, umzekelo, abafundi basebenza ngokwamaqela - mabacinge ngamagama eempahla abazinxiba xa
 kutshisa/kubanda. Iqela elinamagama amaninzi liyaphumelela.

Imisebenzi egxininise ekuphulaphuleni nasekuthetheni (ubuncinane imizuzu eli-15 ka-1 ngeveki, kodwa ingegqithi
kwimizuzu eli-15 ka-2 ngeveki)

Uphulaphula amabali abaliswayo nafundwayo (ukuFunda noTitshala)

Kanye okanye kabini ngeveki, ngokuxhomekeke kwixesha elifumanekayo, utitshala ubalisa okanye ufunda ibali (okanye
abalise ngezehlo ezehlileyo). Amabali abaliswayo angalinganiswa kusetyenziswa iintshukumo nezincedisi (props) ukuxhasa
intsingiselo. Amabali afundwayo makathathwe eNcwadini eNkulu okanye ipowusta enemizobo apho bonke abafundi bayibonayo
imifanekiso.

CAPS 55
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
• Uphulaphula amabali amafutshane, amabalana abaliswayo okanye iitekisi eziyinyani ezibaliswayo okanye ezifundwa
 eNcwadini eNkulu okanye kwipowusta enemizobo, umzekelo, Intsomi kaDyakalashe noHobe, ewonwabela athathe
 inxaxheba kwiindawana eziphinda-phindwayo ngamaxesha afanelekileyo. [Utitshala makazikhethele ibali elifanelekileyo
 asebenzise lona.]
• Uva imiyalelo ayenze, umzekelo, ‘Khawundibonise uDyakalashe ophantsi komthi.’ Ndibonise umthi. Bala abafundi beHobe.
 Bangaphi?’
• Uphendula, ngeempendulo ezimfutshane, imibuzo elula ecacileyo malunga nebali, umzekelo ‘Kutheni uDyakalashe
 engakwazi ukuzithathela ngokwakhe abafundi beHobe?’
• Unika amagama ezinto ezisemfanekisweni ephendula imibuzo katitshala, umzekelo, ‘Yintoni le?’ ‘Ngumthi.’
• Ulinganisa amabali alula esebenzisa inxalenye yencoko yababini
• Ubalisa ibali kwakhona encediswa ngutitshala

Ukukhuliswa kwengqiqo, isigama nezakhiwo zolwimi

Ngokuthatha inxaxheba kule misebenzi ingentla:
• uyaqhuba nokwakha isigama somlomo, kuquka isigama esibonisa ingqiqo, umzekelo, ukuthetha ngemozulu
• uqala ukwakha ukuqonda nokukwazi ukusebenzisa izakhiwo zolwimi ezilula ezisemxholweni wolwimi oluthethwayo
 olunentsingiselo, umzekelo, uyaqonda aqale ukusebenzisa intsiza-senzi ‘Kuyabanda’; uluhlu olubanzana lwezibizo
 ezinentsiza-senzi: kuyagqutha, liyana, uyafika, bayahamba)
UKUHLOLA

Iingcebiso zemiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho sesikweni:

UkuPhulaphula nokuThetha (okuthethwayo kunye/okanye okwenziwayo)

Iiveki 1 - 5
• Uchonga umfanekiso ngokulandela ingcaciso yomlomo elula, umzekelo, ‘Liyana.’
• Uphulaphula abalise amabalana alula aze aphendule imibuzo emalunga nawo, umzekelo ‘Waqala ngokwenza ntoni
 uMandla? Waza wenza ntoni emva koko?, njalo njalo.’

Iiveki 6 - 10
• Uphendula imibuzo elula, umzekelo, ‘Injani imozulu namhlanje?’
• Ubalisa ibalana elilula lomlomo, umzekelo, ‘NgeCawa, ndivuke ngentsimbi yesi-8. Ndahlamba. Emva koko ndiye ndanxiba
 iimpahla zam. Ndaza ndaya enkonzweni.’

UMsebenzi oCetyiswayo wokuHlola okuseSikweni: 2

UkuPhulaphula nokuThetha (okuthethwayo kunye/okanye okwenziwayo)
• Uphulaphula abalise amabalana alula aze aphendule imibuzo emalunga nawo, umzekelo ‘Uvuke nini uSipho? Ibinjani
 imozulu? Uqale ngokwenza ntoni? Waza wenza ntoni emva koko? Wagqibela ngokwenza ntoni?
• Ubonakalisa ukuqonda isigama somlomo esisisiseko ngokukhomba izinto eziseklasini okanye emfanekisweni okanye enze
 iintshukumo utitshala amyalele ukuba azenze, umzekelo, ‘Khawundibonise isambuleli. Unjani umbala wesambuleli?, njalo
 njalo.’

Ulwazi lwezandi namagama (imizuzu eli-15 ngeveki)

Le misebenzi mayibe mifutshane (imizuzu emi-5 - 10) yaye yahlula-hlulelwe iveki. Isenokudityaniswa kwimisebenzi
yokuPhulaphula nokuThetha, neyokuFunda.
• Uchonga unxulumano lwezandi nonobumba kubo bonke oonobumba abazimeleyo (abanye)
• Uqaphela ukuphela ngokufanayo kwamagama (umz. ‘am’, ‘eni’ - ‘kwam’, ‘esikolweni’)
• Wakha acazulule amagama alula aqala ngeqabane elinye ngokwesiqalo selungu (onset) nokulandelayo kwelungu (rime)
 (umz. f-a(ka), s-i(ka), s-e(la))
• Wahlula phakathi kwamagama anezandi ezide nezifutshane (umz. wahleka (kudala), wahleka (ngoku))
• Ukuhlaziya izandi zoononye noonombini abafundwe kwikota 1
• Ukwakha amagama esebenzisa izandi ezakhiwe ngamaqabane amathathu (oonontathu) umz.

 ngc ndl ntl

 ingca indlala intlanzi

 ingcongconi indlela intlaka

 ukungcola indlebe intloko

 indlovu

 indlu

 indlulamthi

 ingxolo, intsimbi, tshayela tshisa, ingqaka, ingqele, ingqondo, ingqolowa njalo-njalo

 Qala ngamagama aqhelekileyo. Upelo nobizelo luyinxalenye yoku.
• Ukwakha amagama esebenzisa izandi ezakhiwe ngamaqabane amane ( oonone) umz.

 ngcw

 ngcwaba

 ingcwele
• Wakha acazulule amagama anoonobumba aba-3 esebenzisa izandi azifundileyo. (umz. m-n-a, m-na, mna; e-w-e, e-we,
 ewe)
• Uqaphela ukuphela ngokufanayo kwamagama (umz. ‘am’ - ‘kwam’, ‘eni’-’esikolweni’)
• Ubeka amagama afanayo ngokwamaqela amagama (umz. yam, zam, bam)

UkuFunda noTitshala (ixesha lako libekwe phantsi kokuPhulaphula nokuThetha)

UkuFunda noTitshala ngumsebenzi wokufunda nokuphulaphula; kukwaquka ukuthetha kuba abafundi bathetha ngetekisi
notitshala wabo. KwiBanga lesi-2, ukuFunda noTitshala kuya kuqhuba, kodwa sele kuyinxalenye yokuPhulaphula nokuThetha.

UkuFunda kwamaQela okuKhokelwayo (ubuncinane bemizuzu engama-30 nelona lininzi leyure nemizuzu eli-15
ngeveki)

CAPS 57
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
Ootitshala abasebenzisa elona lininzi kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo mabohlule iklasi ibe ngamaqela okufunda ama-5
ohlulwe ngokokulingana kokukwazi ukufunda baze basebenze neqela ngalinye imizuzu eli-15. Ngoku utitshala asasebenza
neli qela amanye amaqela aya kufundisana eqeleni okanye abafundi bafundelane ngezibini, besebenzisa iitekisi abaziqhelileyo
okanye imisebenzi enxulumene netekisi. Ezi ncwadi mazibe lula kakhulu zinokuphinda-phindwa kwezakhiwo nesigama kunye
nemifanekiso exhasa itekisi.

Ootitshala abasebenzisa elona lincinane kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, apho banemizuzu engama-30 kuphela, mabenze
umsebenzi wokufunda okukhokelwayo kweklasi kabini ngeveki.
• Ufundela ngaphandle (ngokukhwazayo) encwadini yakhe ekufundeni kweqela okukhokelwayo efunda notitshala. Iqela
 lonke lifunda ibali elifanayo okanye itekisi eyinyani, lifunda kunye notitshala
• Usebenzisa iindlela zokufunda ezifundiswe kuLwimi / lwaseKhaya esenzela ukuqonda itekisi nokuzihlola xa efunda (izandi,
 izikhokelo zeemeko, uhlalutyo lwesakhiwo, amagama abonwa rhoqo)
• Ufunda ngokuqhabalaka okwandayo
• Ubonisa ukuqonda uphawulo lwentetho (punctuation) xa efundela ngaphandle
• Uqalisa ukuzakhela uluhlu lwamagama abonwa rhoqo ewathatha ekufundeni okukhokelwayo, ukufunda notitshala
 nokuzifundela yedwa

Ukuzifundela (ngexesha abangenzi nto ngalo abafundi esikolweni okanye ekhaya)

Abafundi mabakhuthazelwe ukuba bazifundele ngoLwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo lokuQala xa benexesha abangenzi nto ngalo
eklasini xa beqale bagqiba ukwenza umsebenzi nasekhaya njengomsebenzi wasekhaya. Kubalulekile ukuba onke amathuba
afumanekayo eklasini asetyenziselwe ukuphuhlisa ukufunda kwabo.
• Ufunda into ayibhalileyo nebhalwe ngabanye
• Uzifundela ngoLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo iincwadi engancediswa ngethuba lokuFunda okuKhokelwayo afunde
 neencwadi ezilula ezicacisa imifanekiso (caption books) neencwadi zamabali abaliswa ngemifanekiso (picture story books)
 afumaneka kwikona yokufunda yaseklasini
• Usebenzisa izichazi-magama zabafundi ezinemifanekiso (zolwimi olunye nezimbini) ukukhangela intsingiselo yamagama
 angawaziyo

 esebenzisa imizobo ukuncedisa ukuqonda
• Ubonakalisa ukuqonda ibali elifutshane elibhaliweyo, umzekelo, udibanisa ingcaciso yomfanekiso/isivakalisi esicacisa
 umfanekiso esidibanisa nomfanekiso okanye aphendule imibuzo

Imisebenzi yokuBhala (ka-2 okanye ka-3 ngeveki)

Ootitshala mabakhethe kwezi zinto zilandelayo; mabazame ukwenza le misebenzi ibe mininzi kangangoko kwixesha abanalo:
• Usebenzisa izakhono zokubhala ngesandla esele zifundiwe kuLwimi / lwaseKhaya
• Ukhetha akhuphele ingcaciso (caption) ecacisa imifanekiso
• Ubhala ingcaciso yomfanekiso, encediswa
• Ugqibezela izivakalisi ngokufakela amagama ashiyelelweyo.
• Ubhala izivakalisi esebenzisa amagama anezandi namagama abonwayo aqhelekileyo (common sight words) asele
 efundiwe
• Usebenzisa uphawulo-ntetho (punctuation) asele elufundisiwe kuLwimi / lwaseKhaya (oonobumba abakhulu nezingxi)
• Uzakhela ibhanki yesigama nesichazi-magama sakhe
UKUHLOLA

Ukubhala: (okubhaliweyo)

• Ugqibezela izivakalisi ezi-3 ngokufakela amagama ashiyelelweyo

Iiveki 6 - 10
• Ubhala izivakalisi esebenzisa amagama anezandi namagama abonwayo aqhelekileyo (common sight words) asele
 efundiwe
• Usebenzisa uphawulo-ntetho asele elufundisiwe (oonobumba abakhulu nezingxi)

Imisebenzi yemihla ngemihla (imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki)

Le misebenzi iyahluka ngokobude. Utitshala usenokwenza umsebenzi omnye omdana kungenjalo imisebenzi emibini okanye

• Ulandela imiyalelo emifutshane elandelelanayo, umzekelo, ‘Thatha itshokhwe ubhale igama lakho ebhodini. Ngoku ke
 thatha idasta wosule ibhodi.’

• Unika imiyalelo emifutshane, umzekelo, ‘Khaba ibhola’.

• Uqonda aphendule imibuzo elula efana nothi, ‘Yeyiphi ...?’ ‘Yekabani ...?’ (Ngowuphi umdlalo owuthandayo? Yibhola
 ekhatywayo)

• Wenza izicelo neentetho ezilula, umzekelo, ‘Ndicela ukuya kudlala phandle.’

• Uchonga into ngokulandela ingcaciso yomlomo elula, umzekelo, ‘Inkwenkwe ikhaba ibhola.’ Abafundi mabadibanise le
 ngcaciso nomfanekiso ochanekileyo.)

• Uthetha ngezinto ezisemfanekisweni ngokomyalelo katitshala, umzekelo, ‘Ubona ntoni emfanekisweni?’ ‘Ndixelele into
 oyibona emfanekisweni.’

• Uphulaphula ibalana elilula, umzekelo, utitshala ubalisa ngomdlalo webhola ekhatywayo

• Ubalisa ibalana elilula, encediswa ngutitshala, umzekelo, ubalisa ngomdlalo webhola ekhatywayo abewubukele.

• Ugcina engqondweni enze imibongo elula, izicengcelezo ezinentshukumo neengoma, umzekelo, Umzi watsha (x2),
 Khangela phaya (x2), Umlilo! (x2) Galel’ amanzi! (x2)
• Udlala imidlalo yolwimi phandle, umzekelo, uNamhla [kungasetyenziswa nokuba liliphi igama] uthi: UNamhla uthi
 khawuleza. UNamhla uthi cotha. Khawuleza! UNamhla uthi jonga ngasekhohlo. Jonga ngasekunene, njalo njalo.’

kwimizuzu eli-15 ka-2 ngeveki)

 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

• Uphulaphula amabali amafutshane, amabalana abaliswayo okanye iitekisi eziyinyani ezibaliswayo okanye ezifundwa
 eNcwadini eNkulu okanye kwiphowusta enemizobo, (umz. Ibhola ekhatywayo), ewonwabela athathe inxaxheba
 kwiindawana eziphinda-phindwayo ngexesha elifanelekileyo.

• Uva imiyalelo ayenze, umzekelo, ‘Khawundibonise ibala lomdlalo. Ndibonise inqaku elifakiweyo. Ndibonise unozinti. Bala
 abadlali. Bangaphi?’

• Uphendula, ngeempendulo ezimfutshane, imibuzo elula ecacileyo malunga nebali, umzekelo ‘Lifakwe ngubani inqaku?’

• Unika amagama ezinto ezisemfanekisweni ephendula imibuzo katitshala, umzekelo, ‘Yintoni le?’ ‘Linqaku elifakiweyo.’

• Ukuba libali, uyalidlala/uyalilinganisa esebenzisa incoko ethile

• Ubalisa ngokutsha ibali okanye anike isishwakathelo esilula setekisi, encediswa ngutitshala

Ukukhuliswa kwengqiqo, isigama nezakhiwo zolwimi

Ngokuthatha inxaxheba kule misebenzi ingentla:

• uyaqhuba nokwakha isigama somlomo, kuquka isigama esibonisa ingqiqo, umzekelo, ukuthetha ngeemvakalelo

• uqala ukwakha ukuqonda nokukwazi ukusebenzisa izakhiwo zolwimi ezilula ezisemxholweni wolwimi oluthethwayo
 olunentsingiselo, umzekelo, imo ende yexesha langoku: ‘Uyayikhaba ibhola’; uluhlu lolubanzana lwezihlomelo:
 ‘ngokukhawuleza,’ ‘ngokucotha’
UKUHLOLA

Iingcebiso zemiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho sesikweni:

UkuPhulaphula nokuThetha (okuthethwayo kunye/okanye okwenziwayo)

Iiveki 1 - 5

• Uphendula imibuzo elula ecacileyo malunga nebali /itekisi

• Ubalisa ngokutsha ibali/ ushwankathela itekisi ngomlomo (ngezivakalisi ezithathu okanye ezine)

Iiveki 6 - 10

• Uphendula imibuzo elula, umzekelo, ‘Nguwuphi owona mdlalo uwuthandayo?’

• Uchonga imifanekiso emi-3 esebenzisa iingcaciso ezilula zomlomo, umzekeko, Indoda idlala ibhola ekhatywayo/ Indoda
 idlala intenetya/ Indoda idlala iqakamba

UMsebenzi oCetyiswayo wokuHlola okuseSikweni: 3

UkuPhulaphula nokuThetha (okuthethwayo kunye/okanye okwenziwayo)

• Uphulaphula abalise amabalana alula aze aphendule imibuzo emalunga nawo, umzekelo ‘Liliphi iqela elifake inqaku
 lokuqala? Liliphi iqela elifake inqaku lesibini? Liliphi iqela elifake inqaku lesithathu? Liliphi iqela eliphumeleleyo?’

• Ubonakalisa ukuqonda isigama somlomo esisisiseko ngokukhomba izinto eziseklasini okanye emfanekisweni okanye enze
 iintshukumo utitshala amyalele ukuba azenze, umzekelo, ‘Khawundibonise irakethi lentenetya/ iphini leqakamba/ ibhola
 ekhatywayo, njalo njalo.’
UMsebenzi oCetyiswayo wokuHlola okuseSikweni: 4

UkuPhulaphula nokuThetha (okuthethwayo kunye/okanye okwenziwayo)

• Ubalisa ibalana elilula, umzekelo, ingxelo yokwenzeke emdlalweni webhola ekhatywayo

• Ubonakalisa ukuqonda isigama somlomo esisisiseko ngokukhomba izinto eziseklasini okanye emfanekisweni okanye enze
 iintshukumo utitshala amyalele ukuba azenze, umzekelo, ‘Khawundibonise ukhapteni weqela.’ ‘Ndibonise unozinti.’

UKUFUNDA NEZANDI IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDA

Ulwazi lwezandi namagama (imizuzu eli-15 ngeveki)

Ootitshala baqhuba nokwakha amagama alula, aqhelekileyo. Bafaka oonombini abathile abaqhelekileyo, baqale ekuqaleni
kwamagama baphinde babe sekupheleni kwamagama. Kufuneka bazame ukusebenzisa amagama asele esaziwa
ngabafundi aze asetyenziswe kwizivakalisi ezilula. Le misebenzi mayibe mifutshane (imizuzu emi-5 - 10) yaye yahlula-
hlulelwe iveki. Isenokudityaniswa kwimisebenzi yokuPhulaphula nokuThetha, neyokuFunda.
• Wakha acazulule amagama alula aqala ngeqabane elinye ngokwesiqalo selungu (onset) nokulandelayo kwelungu (rime)
 (umz. f-a(ka), s-i(ka), s-e(la))
• Ubeka amagama afanayo ngokwamaqela amagama (umz. yam, zam, bam)
• Uqaphela ukuphela ngokufanayo kwamagama (umz. ‘am’, ‘eni’ - ‘kwam’, ‘esikolweni’)
• Uqaphela oonombini bamaqabane abafana ekuqaleni nasekupheleni kwamagama njengo’th’ (thabatha)
• Ukuhlaziya izandi zoononye noonombini abafundwe kwikota 1
• Ukwakha amagama esebenzisa izandi ezakhiwe ngamaqabane amathathu (oonontathu) umz.

 ngc ndl ntl

 ingca indlala intlanzi

 ingcongconi indlela intlaka

 ukungcola indlebe intloko

 indlovu

 indlu

 indlulamthi

 ingxolo, intsimbi, tshayela tshisa, ingqaka, ingqele, ingqondo, ingqolowa njalo-njalo

 Qala ngamagama aqhelekileyo. Upelo nobizelo luyinxalenye yoku.
• Ukwakha amagama esebenzisa izandi ezakhiwe ngamaqabane amane ( oonone) umz.

 ngcw

 ingcwaba

 ingcwele
• Wakha acazulule amagama anoonobumba aba-3 esebenzisa izandi azifundileyo. (umz. m-n-a, m-na, mna; e-w-e, e-we,
 ewe)
• Uqaphela ukuphela ngokufanayo kwamagama (umz. ‘am’ - ‘kwam’, ‘eni’-’esikolweni’)
• Ubeka amagama afanayo ngokwamaqela amagama (umz. yam, zam, bam)

UkuFunda noTitshala (ixesha lako libekwe phantsi kokuPhulaphula nokuThetha)

UkuFunda noTitshala ngumsebenzi wokufunda nokuphulaphula; kukwaquka ukuthetha kuba abafundi bathetha
ngetekisi notitshala wabo. KwiBanga lesi-2, ukuFunda noTitshala kuya kuqhuba, kodwa sele kuyinxalenye
yokuPhulaphula nokuThetha.

UkuFunda kwamaQela okuKhokelwayo (ubuncinane bemizuzu engama-30 nelona lininzi leyure nemizuzu eli-15
ngeveki)

 62 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

Ootitshala abasebenzisa elona lininzi kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo mabohlule iklasi ibe ngamaqela okufunda
ama-5 ohlulwe ngokokulingana kokukwazi ukufunda baze basebenze neqela ngalinye imizuzu eli-15. Ngoku utitshala
asasebenza neli qela amanye amaqela aya kufundisana eqeleni okanye abafundi bafundelane ngezibini, besebenzisa iitekisi
abaziqhelileyo okanye imisebenzi enxulumene netekisi. Ezi ncwadi mazibe lula kakhulu zinokuphinda-phindwa kwezakhiwo
nesigama kunye nemifanekiso exhasa itekisi.

Ootitshala abasebenzisa elona lincinane kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, apho banemizuzu engama-30 kuphela,
mabenze umsebenzi wokufunda okukhokelwayo kweklasi kabini ngeveki.
• Ufundela ngaphandle (ngokukhwazayo) encwadini yakhe ekufundeni kweqela okukhokelwayo efunda notitshala. Le nto
 ithetha ukuba iqela lonke lifunda ibali elifanayo okanye itekisi eyinyani, lifunda kunye notitshala
• Usebenzisa iindlela zokufunda ezifundiswe kuLwimi / lwaseKhaya esenzela ukuqonda itekisi nokuzihlola xa efunda (izandi,
 izikhokelo zeemeko, uhlalutyo lwesakhiwo, amagama abonwa rhoqo)
• Ufunda ngokuqhabalaka okwandayo
• Ubonisa ukuqonda uphawulo lwentetho (punctuation) xa efundela ngaphandle
• Uqalisa ukuzakhela uluhlu lwamagama abonwa rhoqo ewathatha ekufundeni okukhokelwayo, ukufunda notitshala
 nokuzifundela yedwa

Ukuzifundela (ngexesha abangenzi nto ngalo abafundi esikolweni okanye ekhaya)

Abafundi mabakhuthazelwe ukuba bazifundele ngoLwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo lokuQala xa benexesha abangenzi nto
ngalo eklasini (umz. xa beqale bagqiba ukwenza umsebenzi) nasekhaya (umz. umsebenzi wasekhaya). Kubalulekile ukuba
onke amathuba afumanekayo eklasini asetyenziselwe ukuphuhlisa ukufunda kwabo.
• Ufunda into ayibhalileyo nebhalwe ngabanye
• Uzifundela ngoLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo iincwadi engancediswa ngethuba lokuFunda okuKhokelwayo afunde
 neencwadi ezilula ezicacisa imifanekiso (caption books) neencwadi zamabali abaliswa ngemifanekiso (picture story books)
 afumaneka kwikona yokufunda yaseklasini
• Usebenzisa izichazi-magama zabafundi ezinemifanekiso (zolwimi olunye nezimbini) ukukhangela intsingiselo yamagama
 angawaziyo

UKUBHALA IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDA

 Imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki
UMXHOLO/INGQIQO/IZAKHONO

Imisebenzi yokuBhala (ka-2 okanye ka-3 ngeveki)

Ootitshala mabazame ukwenza imisebenzi emininzi kangangoko ngexesha elikhoyo.
• Usebenzisa izakhono zokubhala ngesandla esele zifundiwe
• Ubhala ingcaciso yomfanekiso
• Ugqibezela izivakalisi ngokufakela amagama ashiyelelweyo.
• Ubhala izivakalisi esebenzisa amagama anezandi namagama abonwayo aqhelekileyo asele efundiwe
• Ubhala izivakalisi esebenzisa isakhelo (frame) (umz. Ndithanda _____. Andithandi ____________.
• Ubhala amagama nezivakalisi eziqhelekileyo kwezo azibizelwayo (dictation)
• Ubhala iitekisi ezimfutshane ezilula asele ezifundile, umz. umyalezo wekhadi lokunqwenelela ukuphila
• Usebenzisa izibizo nezimelabizo (mna, wena, yena, yona) ekubhaleni
• Upela amagama ngokuchanekileyo engawajonganga, umzekelo, ewe, hamba, nika, okanye esebenzisa ulwazi lwamaga,
 umzekelo, umama, utata, usisi
• Usebenzisa isichazi-magama sabafundi xa kuyimfuneko
• Usebenzisa uphawulo-ntetho asele elufundisiwe (oonobumba abakhulu nezingxi)
• Uzakhela ibhanki yesigama nesichazi-magama sakhe
UKUHLOLA

UMsebenzi oCetyiswayo wokuHlola okuseSikweni: 3

Ukubhala: (okubhaliweyo)
• Ubhala ingcaciso yomfanekiso, umzekelo, Inkomo itya ingca.
• Ubhala izivakalisi esebenzisa isakhelo, umz. Ndithanda _____. Andithandi ____________.

UMsebenzi oCetyiswayo wokuHlola okuseSikweni: 4

Ukubhala: (okubhaliweyo)
• Ubhala isivakalisi asibizelwayo; aphawule isivakalisi
• Ubhala azobe ikhadi lokunqwenelela ukuphila

 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

 IKOTA 4
UKUPHULAPHULA NOKUTHETHA (EZOMLOMO) IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDA
 Elona lincinane: Imizuzu engama-45 ngeveki
 Elona lininzi: Iyure e-1 ngeveki
UMXHOLO/INGQIQO/IZAKHONO

Iiveki 1 - 5

Ootitshala bakhetha izihloko ezibini eziya kubavumela ukuba bazise yaye bamane besebenzisa isigama (recycle vocabulary),
yaye benze le misebenzi idweliswe apha ngezantsi. Qaphela ukuba ezi zihloko zicetyiswayo ziingcebiso nje. Ootitshala
mabazikhethele imixholo yabo efanelekileyo ngokuxhomekeke kwimeko nezixhobo ezikhoyo. Mabaqiniseke ukuba
baneeNcwadi eziNkulu/iipowusta, izicengcelezo, iingoma, imidlalo nezinto zenyani ezaneleyo zeso sihloko basikhethileyo.
Mabazame ukwenza yonke imisebenzi, ibe ngaphezu komsebenzi omnye ukuba kuyenzeka.

Kufuneka utitshala ahlole abafundi esebenzisa imiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho sesikweni ecetyisiweyo yeeVeki 1-5.
Iiveki 6 - 10

Ootitshala bakhetha izihloko ezitsha ezibini eziya kubenza bakwazi ukwakhela koko sele kwenziwe kwiiveki zokuqala ezintlanu.
Ezi zihloko mazenze ukuba afake isigama esitsha yaye agxininise kweso sigama sele sifundisiwe. Ezi zihloko mazimvumele
utitshala ukuba amane esebenzisa izakhiwo zolwimi ezilula nezisisiseko xa ethetha nabafundi ngoLwimi oloNgezelelweyo
lokuQala.

Ootitshala mabazame ukwenza yonke imisebenzi, ibe ngaphezu komsebenzi omnye ukuba kuyenzeka. Mabahlole abafundi
besebenzisa imiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho sesikweni ecetyisiweyo yeeVeki 6-10. Ootitshala mabenzise imiSebenzi
yokuHlola okuseSikweni ekupheleni kwekota.

Imisebenzi yemihla ngemihla (imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki)

Le misebenzi iyahluka ngokobude. Utitshala usenokwenza umsebenzi omnye omdana kungenjalo imisebenzi emibini okanye
emithathu emifutshanana ngeveki.

• Uyaqhuba nokukhulisa isigama somlomo (ukuphulaphula nokuthetha) esebenzisa izihloko ezifana ‘namaXesha onyaka’

• Ulandela imiyalelo emifutshane elandelelanayo, umzekelo, ‘Yiza uhlale apha ngaphambili eklasini.

• Unika imiyalelo emifutshane, umzekelo, ‘Qhwaba izandla.’

• Uqonda aphendule imibuzo elula efana nothi, ‘Yeyiphi ...?’ ‘Yekabani ...?’ (Leliphi ixesha lonyaka olithanda kakhulu?
 Lihlobo.)

• Wenza izicelo neentetho ezilula, umzekelo, ‘Ndicela ukupakisha iincwadi zam.’

• Uchonga into ngokulandela ingcaciso yomlomo elula, umzekelo, ‘Kusehlotyeni, Ilanga liyatshisa. Iintyatyambo ziyadubula.
 Abafundi banxibe izikhindi (iibhulukhwe ezimfutshane) nezihlangu ezivulekileyo.’ Abafundi mabadibanise le ngcaciso
 nomfanekiso ochanekileyo.

• Uthetha ngezinto ezisemfanekisweni ngokomyalelo katitshala, umzekelo, ‘Ubona ntoni emfanekisweni?’ ‘Ndixelele into
 oyibona emfanekisweni.’)

• Uphulaphula ibalana elilula, umzekelo, utitshala ubalisa ngezinto azenzileyo xa ebeye kuthenga edolophini

• Ubalisa ibalana elilula, encediswa ngutitshala, umzekelo, abafundi baxelela utitshala malunga nezinto abazenzileyo

• Ugcina engqondweni enze imibongo elula, izicengcelezo ezinentshukumo neengoma, umzekelo, Langa, langa yiza
 ngapha, Thunzi, thunzi bhek’ eBhayi!

• Udlala imidlalo yolwimi, umzekelo, Iliso lam libona into eqala ngo …...

Imisebenzi egxininise ekuphulaphuleni nasekuthetheni (ubuncinane imizuzu eli-15 ka-1 ngeveki, kodwa ingegqithi
kwimizuzu eli-15 ka-2 ngeveki)

Uphulaphula amabali abaliswayo nafundwayo (ukuFunda noTitshala)

 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
Kanye okanye kabini ngeveki, ngokuxhomekeke kwixesha elifumanekayo, utitshala ubalisa okanye ufunda ibali (okanye
abalise ngezehlo ezehlileyo). Amabali abaliswayo angalinganiswa kusetyenziswa iintshukumo nezincedisi (props) ukuxhasa
intsingiselo. Amabali afundwayo makathathwe eNcwadini eNkulu okanye ipowusta enemizobo apho bonke abafundi bayibonayo
imifanekiso.

• Uphulaphula amabali amafutshane, amabalana abaliswayo okanye iitekisi eziyinyani ezibaliswayo okanye ezifundwa
 eNcwadini eNkulu okanye kwipowusta enemizobo, umzekelo, UNomehlomancinane, [Sebenzisa ibali elinento yokwenza
 namaxesha onyaka, ngakumbi ubusika.] ewonwabela athathe inxaxheba kwiindawana eziphinda-phindwayo ngamaxesha
 afanelekileyo.

• Uva imiyalelo ayenze, umzekelo, ‘Khawundibonise imithi. Ngaba inamawo amagqabi? Leliphi ixesha lonyaka eli?’

• Uphendula, ngeempendulo ezimfutshane, imibuzo elula ecacileyo malunga nebali, umzekelo ‘Kwenzeka ntoni emithini
 ebusika?’

• Unika amagama ezinto ezisemfanekisweni ephendula imibuzo katitshala, umzekelo, ‘Yintoni le?’ ‘Likhephu.’

• Ukuba libali, uyalidlala/uyalilinganisa esebenzisa incoko ethile

• Ubalisa ngokutsha ibali okanye anike isishwakathelo esilula setekisi, encediswa ngutitshala

Ukukhuliswa kwengqiqo, isigama nezakhiwo zolwimi

Ngokuthatha inxaxheba kule misebenzi ingentla:

• uyaqhuba nokwakha isigama somlomo, kuquka isigama esibonisa ingqiqo, umzekelo, utshintsho/imijikelo - amaxesha
 onyaka

• uqala ukwakha ukuqonda nokukwazi ukusebenzisa izakhiwo zolwimi ezilula ezisemxholweni wolwimi oluthethwayo
 olunentsingiselo, umzekelo, uyaqonda aqale ukusebenzisa intsiza-senzi: Kuyatshisa’; uluhlu olubanzana lwezibaluli:
 ebandayo, eyomileyo, nezihlomelo: ngokuthambileyo, kuqinile

Iingcebiso zemiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho sesikweni:
UkuPhulaphula nokuThetha (okuthethwayo kunye/okanye okwenziwayo)

Iiveki 6 - 10

 ayinamagqabi. /Kusentwasahlobo. Intaka yakha indlwana yayo.’

UkuPhulaphula nokuThetha (okuthethwayo kunye/okanye okwenziwayo)
• Uphulaphula amabalana alula, umzekelo, Iiholide zam zehlobo, aze aphendule imibuzo emalunga nalo, umzekelo, Ubeye
 phi uThandi ngeeholide zakhe zehlobo? Ufike wenza ntoni akufika apho? Waza wenza ntoni emva koko?, njalo njalo.’
• Unika ibalana elilula elinezivakalisi ezi-3 okanye ezi-4, esebenzisa isakhelo (frame)
• Ubonakalisa ukuqonda isigama somlomo esisisiseko ngokukhomba izinto eziseklasini okanye emfanekisweni okanye enze
 iintshukumo utitshala amyalele ukuba azenze, umzekelo, ‘Khawundibonise irakethi lentenetya/ iphini leqakamba/ ibhola
 ekhatywayo, njalo njalo.’
• Uqonda amagama amalunga mama-500 ukuba kusetyenziswa elona lincinane ukufundisa ulwimi lokuqala
 oloNgezelelweyo, aze aye kutsho kwi-1,000 ukuba kusetyenziswa elona lininzi ukufundisa ulwimi lokuqala

Ulwazi lwezandi namagama (imizuzu eli-15 ngeveki)

Ootitshala bazisa izandi ezenziwa ngokudibanisa amaqabane ohlukeneyo (consonant blends) noonombini bezikhamiso,
besebenzisa amagama asele esaziwa ngabafundi okanye amagama asetyenziswa kakhulu. Xa besazisa amagama amatsha
kufuneka baqiniseke ukuba abafundi bayayiqonda intsingiselo yaye bawasebenzise kwizivakalisi. Le misebenzi mayibe
mifutshane (imizuzu emi-5 - 10) yaye yahlula-hlulelwe iveki. Isenokudityaniswa kwimisebenzi yokuPhulaphula nokuThetha,
neyokuFunda.
• Ubeka amagama aqhelekileyo ngokwamaqela amagama (umzekelo, ilitha, ukutha, isitya, ukutya, sela, xela)
• Wakha acazulule amagama alula aqhelekileyo aqala ngokudityansiwa kwezandi ezenziwa ngokudibanisa amaqabane
 ohlukeneyo (consonant blends) (umz. hl-ala, dl-ala, bh-ala)
• Uqaphela oonombini bezikhamiso nezikhuzo (umz. ‘oo’ - ooSipho, ‘ii’ - iibhasi, ‘Aa!’ Mhlekazi!)
• Wahlula phakathi kwezandi ezidla ngokubhidaniswa ngokuziva ngokuziphula-phula (umz. ‘hl’ no’dl’ - ‘hlala’, ‘dlala’)
• Ukuhlaziya izandi zoononye noonombini abafundiweyo
• Ukwakha amagama esebenzisa izandi ezakhiwe ngamaqabane amathathu (oonontathu) umz.

 ingcongconi indlela intlaka

 ukungcola indlebe intloko

 indlulamthi

 ingxolo, intsimbi, tshayela tshisa, ingqaka, ingqele, ingqondo, ingqolowa njalo-njalo

 Qala ngamagama aqhelekileyo. Upelo nobizelo luyinxalenye yoku.
• Ukwakha amagama esebenzisa izandi ezakhiwe ngamaqabane amane ( oonone) umz.

 ngcw

 ingcwaba

 ingcwele
• Wakha acazulule amagama anoonobumba aba-3 esebenzisa izandi azifundileyo. (umz. m-n-a, m-na, mna; e-w-e, e-we,
 ewe)
• Uqaphela ukuphela ngokufanayo kwamagama (umz. ‘am’ - ‘kwam’, ‘eni’-’esikolweni’)
• Ubeka amagama afanayo ngokwamaqela amagama (umz. yam, zam, bam)

UkuFunda noTitshala (ixesha lako libekwe phantsi kokuPhulaphula nokuThetha)

UkuFunda noTitshala ngumsebenzi wokufunda nokuphulaphula; kukwaquka ukuthetha kuba abafundi bathetha ngetekisi
notitshala wabo. KwiBanga lesi-2, ukuFunda noTitshala kuya kuqhuba, kodwa sele kuyinxalenye yokuPhulaphula
nokuThetha.

UkuFunda kwamaQela okuKhokelwayo (ubuncinane bemizuzu engama-30 nelona lininzi leyure nemizuzu eli-15
ngeveki)

CAPS 67
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
Ootitshala abasebenzisa elona lininzi kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo mabohlule iklasi ibe ngamaqela okufunda
ama-5 ohlulwe ngokokulingana kokukwazi ukufunda baze basebenze neqela ngalinye imizuzu eli-15. Ngoku utitshala
asasebenza neli qela amanye amaqela aya kufundisana eqeleni okanye abafundi bafundelane ngezibini, besebenzisa iitekisi
abaziqhelileyo okanye imisebenzi enxulumene netekisi. Ezi ncwadi mazibe lula kakhulu zinokuphinda-phindwa kwezakhiwo
nesigama kunye nemifanekiso exhasa itekisi.

Ootitshala abasebenzisa elona lincinane kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, apho banemizuzu engama-30 kuphela,
mabenze umsebenzi wokufunda okukhokelwayo kweklasi kabini ngeveki.
• Ufundela ngaphandle (ngokukhwazayo) encwadini yakhe ekufundeni kweqela okukhokelwayo efunda notitshala. Le nto
 ithetha ukuba iqela lonke lifunda ibali elifanayo okanye itekisi eyinyani, lifunda kunye notitshala
• Usebenzisa iindlela zokufunda ezifundiswe kuLwimi / lwaseKhaya esenzela ukuqonda itekisi nokuzihlola xa efunda (izandi,
 izikhokelo zeemeko, uhlalutyo lwesakhiwo, amagama abonwa rhoqo)
• Ufunda ngokuqhabalaka okwandayo
• Ubonisa ukuqonda uphawulo lwentetho (punctuation) xa efundela ngaphandle
• Uqalisa ukuzakhela uluhlu lwamagama abonwa rhoqo ewathatha ekufundeni okukhokelwayo, ukufunda notitshala
 nokuzifundela yedwa

Ukuzifundela (ngexesha abangenzi nto ngalo abafundi esikolweni okanye ekhaya)

Abafundi mabakhuthazelwe ukuba bazifundele ngoLwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo lokuQala xa benexesha abangenzi nto
ngalo eklasini xa beqale bagqiba ukwenza umsebenzi nasekhaya njengomsebenzi wasekhaya. Kubalulekile ukuba onke
amathuba afumanekayo eklasini asetyenziselwe ukuphuhlisa ukufunda kwabo.
• Ufunda into ayibhalileyo nebhalwe ngabanye
• Uzifundela ngoLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo iincwadi engancediswa ngethuba lokuFunda okuKhokelwayo afunde
 neencwadi ezilula ezicacisa imifanekiso (caption books) neencwadi zamabali abaliswa ngemifanekiso (picture story books)
 afumaneka kwikona yokufunda yaseklasini
• Usebenzisa izichazi-magama zabafundi ezinemifanekiso (zolwimi olunye nezimbini) ukukhangela intsingiselo yamagama
 angawaziyo

• Wakha acazulule amagama alula aqhelekileyo aqala ngokudityansiwa kwezandi ezenziwa ngokudibanisa amaqabane
 ohlukeneyo (umz. hl-ala, dl-ala, bh-ala)

 yomfanekiso nomfanekiso

 UKUBHALA IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDA

Imisebenzi yokuBhala (ka-2 okanye ka-3 ngeveki)

Ootitshala mabazame ukwenza imisebenzi emininzi kangangoko ngexesha elikhoyo.
• Usebenzisa izakhono zokubhala ngesandla esele zifundiwe kuLwimi / lwaseKhaya
• Ubhala izivakalisi esebenzisa amagama anezandi namagama abonwayo aqhelekileyo (common sight words) asele
 efundiwe
• Ubhala amagama nezivakalisi eziqhelekileyo kwezo azibizelwayo (dictation)
• Ulandelelanisa kakuhle izivakalisi ezibhidanisiweyo (jumbled sentences) esenzela ukwenza umhlathi aze awukhuphele
• Ubhala umhlathi owenziwa bubuncinane bezivakalisi ezi-3 ngesihloko asaziyo
• Usebenzisa izakhono azifundiswe kuLwimi / lwaseKhaya ukulandelelanisa iinkcukacha ngendlela elula (umz. itshathi
 okanye ukulandelelana kweziganeko ngokwamaxesha (time line)
• Usebenzisa izibizo nezimelabizo (Mna, wena, yena, yona, njl.) ekubhaleni
• Usebenzisa imo emfutshane, imo ende yexesha langoku nexesha elidlulileyo xa ebhala
• Usebenzisa isininzi samagama aqhelekileyo xa ebhala
• Upela amagama ngokuchanekileyo engawajonganga, umzekelo, ewe, hamba, nika, okanye esebenzisa ulwazi lwamaga,
 umzekelo, umama, utata, usisi
• Usebenzisa isichazi-magama sabafundi xa kuyimfuneko
• Usebenzisa uphawulo-ntetho (punctuation) asele elufundisiwe kuLwimi / lwaseKhaya (oonobumba abakhulu nezingxi)
• Uzakhela ibhanki yesigama nesichazi-magama sakhe
UKUHLOLA

UMsebenzi oCetyiswayo wokuHlola okuseSikweni:

Ukubhala: (okubhaliweyo)
• Ubhala izivakalisi ezihlanu azibizelwayo esebenzisa uphawulo-ntetho (punctuates) olufanelekileyo.
• Ulandelelanisa kakuhle izivakalisi ezibhidanisiweyo (jumbled sentences) esenzela ukwenza umhlathi aze awukhuphele

UMsebenzi oCetyiswayo wokuHlola okuseSikweni: 5

Ukubhala: (okubhaliweyo)
• Ubhala umhlathi owenziwa bubuncinane bezivakalisi ezithathu ngesihloko asaziyo
• Ulungelelanisa iinkcukacha kwindlela elula yokuzoba, umzekelo, itshathi okanye ukulandelelana kweziganeko
 ngokwamaxesha (time line)

 70 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

 IBANGA 3 ULWIMI LOKUQALA OloNgezelelweyo ISIXHOSA

 IIMFUNO ZEKOTA NGANYE
 IKOTA 1
 UKUPHULAPHULA NOKUTHETHA (EZOMLOMO) IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDISA

 Iyure e-1 ngeveki
UMXHOLO/IINGQIQO/IZAKHONO

Iiveki ukususela kweyoku-1 ukuya kweye-5

Ootitshala bakhetha imixholo ibe mibini eya kubavumela ukuba baqalise ukufundisa baphinda-phinde isigama, baze baquke
nemisebenzi edweliswe ngezantsi apha. Phawula ukuba imixholo okanye izihloko ozinikiweyo ziingcebiso nje kuphela.
Ootitshala mabazikhethele imixholo efanele bona ngokuxhomekeke kwiimeko abakuzo nezixhobo abanazo. Kufuneka
baqinisekise ukuba banazo izinto ezifunekayo ezinjengeeNcwadi eziNkulu neephowusta, izicengcelezo, iingoma, imidlalo
nezinto ezingoondoqo kumxholo abawukhethileyo. Kufuneka bazame kangangoko banakho ukuquka imisebenzi emininzi
abanokuyenza ngexesha abalifumanayo.

Utitshala makahlole abafundi esebenzisa imiSebenzi yokuHlola engekho seSikweni, ecetyiswe ukuba yenziwe kwiveki yoku-
1 ukuya kweyesi-5.
Iiveki: ukusuka kweyesi-6 ukuya kweye-10

Ootitshala bakhetha imixholo emitsha emibini eya kubavumela bakhele phezu koko bebekwenze kwiiveki ezintlanu zokuqala.
Imixholo mayivumele utitshala ukuba ahlaziye, avuselele izakhiwo zolwimi nesigama esilula nesisiseko, njengokuba
esabelana ngolwazi nabafundi besebenzisa uLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo .

Ootitshala mabaqinisekise ukuba bafikelela kuyo yonke imisebenzi, nangaphezu kwexesha elinye ukuba kuyavumeleka.
Bamelwe kukuba bahlole abafundi besebenzisa imiSebenzi yokuHlola engekho seSikweni ecetyiswa ukuba yenziwe kwiveki
yesi-6 ukuya kweye-10. Ootitshala mabaqalise ngomSebenzi wokuHlola oseSikweni ekupheleni kwekota.

Imisebenzi yemihla ngemihla (imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki)
 Imisebenzi yahlukile ngobude. Utitshala angenza umsebenzi omnye omdana, okanye ibe mibini noba mithathu emifutshane
ngeveki.
• Aqhube aphuhlise isigama somlomo (ukuphulaphula nokuthetha) esebenzisa imixholo okanye izihloko, njenge-
 ‘Izilwanyana Zasekhaya’.
• Alandele imiyalelo, umzekelo, ‘Khuphani iincwadi zenu zomsebenzi’, ‘Bhalani umhla kwikona ephezulu ngasekunene’.
• Anike imiyalelo elula, umzekelo, ‘Bamba isandla sam’.
• Aqonde, akwazi ukuphendula imibuzo elula njengaxa kubuzwa, ‘Nini....?’ ‘Njani...? ‘Phi...? umzekelo, ‘Wayibona phi
 izebra?’ ‘Wayibona phi?’ ‘Ehlathini, ‘Kulo nyaka uphelileyo.’
• Abuze ecela ingcaciso, umzekelo, ‘Andiqondi kakuhle. Ndicela uphinde kwakhona’.
• Aphendule, enze izicelo, umzekelo, ‘Ndingasisebenzisa isichazi-magama?’
• Athathe inxaxheba kwincoko emfutshane kwisihloko esiqhelekileyo, umzekelo, ‘Imozulu’, ‘Usapho lwam’.
• Achonge izinto ezilula ephulaphule inkcazelo yomlomo, umzekelo, ‘Ndisisilwanyana esikhulu, Ndingwevu. Ndinesikhumba
 esirhabaxa. Ndinamabamba abukhali.nomboko omde. Ndingubani?’ (ooqashi-qashi)
• Athethe ngezinto ezisemfanekisweni okanye ifoto elandela imiyalelo katitshala, umzekelo, ‘Ungabona ntoni efotweni?’
 ‘Ndixelele into oyibonayo efotweni’.
• Aphulaphule amabali alula, umzekelo, utitshala ebalisa ngotyelelo kumzi ogcina izilwanyana.
• Ngoncedo lukatitshala, ubalisa ibali elilula, umzekelo, umfundi ubalisela utitshala ngento athe wayenza.
• Aqonde akwazi ukusebenzisa izakhiwo zolwimi kwii meko ezifaneleyo, umzekelo, ixesha elaldulayo, ‘Ndabona indlulamthi’.
• Afunde ngengqondo enze imidlalo yemibongo, izicengcelezo neengoma, umzekelo, ‘Izilwanyana zihamba ngambini’.
• Adlale imidlalo ngoLwimi, umzekelo, ‘Qashi, qashi, ndingubani?’

CAPS 71
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
Imisebenzi yokuphulaphula nokuthetha egxininiswayo (imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki)

Aphulaphule amabali abaliswayo nafundwayo (Ukufunda notitshala)

Utitshala ubalisa okanye afunde ibali kabini ngeveki (okanye ibali elibalisiweyo). Amabali abaliswayo anokudlalwa
kusetyenziswa izijekulo nezixhobo ezincedisa ekwenzeni umdlalo, ukuxhasa nokuphuhlisa intsingiselo. Amabali afundwayo
mawathathelwe kwiNcwadi eNkulu okanye iphowusta enomfanekiso apho bonke abafundi banokuyibona imifanekiso.
• Aphulaphule amabali amafutshane, amabali abalisiweyo okanye iitekisi eziyinyani, umzekelo, amabali angenkcukacha
 ethile, imiyalelo, iingxelo ezingolwazi, ezibaliswa okanye zifundwa kwiNcwadi eNkulu okanye iphowusta enemifanekiso,
 bezonwabisa.
• Aphulaphule amabali, umzekelo, ‘Yayifumana njani indlulamthi intamo ende’, kunye namabali abaliswa ngabo ngokwabo
 nokuphendula imibuzo yengqiqo njengokuba, ‘Yayi fumana njani indlulamthi intamo ende?’
• Aqikelele into elandelayo eya kwenzeka ebalini, okanye amabali abantu, ‘Yintoni ocinga ukuba iza kwenzeka kwindlulamthi
 ngoku?’
• Achaze iimvakalelo zakhe ngebali, umzekelo, ‘Ulithandile na ibali? Yintoni ebangela ukuba ulithande? Yintoni ebangela
 ukuba ungalithandi?’
• Abalise ibali eliphinda
• Aphulaphule itekisi eyinyani njengebali elingenkcukacha ethile okanye ingxelo engolwazi ngeendlovu, neempendulo
 kwimibuzo engengqiqo, umzekelo, ‘Itya ntoni indlovu? Iwusebenzisa entwenini umboko ?
• Ngoncedo lukatitshala, unika isishwankathelo esilula setekisi eyinyani.

Ukuphuhliswa kwemixholo, isigama nezakhiwo zolwimi

Ngokuthatha inxaxheba kule misebenzi ingentla:
• Aqhube esakha isigama somlomo, kuqukwa isigama sengqiqo, umzekelo, izilwanyana; ubuqu, iindawo ezihlala kuzo,
 njalo.
• Aqalise ukuphuhlisa ukuqonda nokwazi ukusebenzisa izakhiwo zolwimi ezilula kwiimeko zokuvelisa intsingiselo ngolwimi
 oluthethwayo, umzekelo, izibizo ezibhekisa kwizinto ezibalwayo nezo zibhekisa kwezingabalwayo njengezi: ‘iindlovu’,
 ‘amanzi’; ixesha langoku, ‘Nditya isonka’, ixesha eladlulayo, ‘Ndabona indlovu’.
UKUHLOLA

Iingcebiso ngemiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho seSikweni:

Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha: (ezomlomo kunye/okanye ezenziwayo)

Iiveki: ukusuka kweyoku-1 ukuya kweyesi-5
• Aqonde aphendule imibuzo elula njengemibuzo, ehamba ngolu hlobo: Nini...? Njani...? Kutheni...?
• Achonge into ngokuphulaphula inkcazo yomlomo elula nengantsokothanga, umzekelo, ‘Ndisisilwanyana esikhulu kakhulu.
 Ndinentamo ende. Ndingafikelela nditye awona aphezulu emthini amagqabi. Ndingubani?’(qashi-qashi)

Iiveki: ukusuka kweyesi-6 ukuya kweye-10
• Abalise ibali ngomlomo eliphinda
• Athathe inxaxheba engxoxweni engesihloko esiqhelekileyo

UmSebenzi wokuHlola oseSikweni ocetyiswayo woku-1:

Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha (ezomlomo kunye/okanye ezenziwayo)
• Aphulaphule itekisi eyinyaniso, njengebali elingenkcukacha okanye ingxelo engolwazi nokuphendula imibuzo yengqiqo
 ngomlomo
• Anike isishwankathelo esilula somlomo setekisi eyinyani esiyimigca emi-3 okanye emi-4
• Abonakalise ukusiqonda isigama somlomo ngokukhomba izinto eziseklasini okanye emfanekisweni, ephendula imibuzo
 okanye esabela imiyalelo katitshala, umzekelo, ‘Yintoni leya?’ ‘Khomba amabamba endlovu.

UMXHOLO/IINGQIGO/IZAKHONO
Izandi (imizuzu eli-15 ngeveki)
Utitshala wenza uhlaziyo loonobumba abancinci noonombini abathile aqhelekileyo kusetyenziswa amagama abasele bewazi
abafundi okanye amagama aqhelekileyo. Xa utitshala aza okanye afundisa igama elitsha, uqinisekisa ukuba abafundi
bayayiqonda intsingiselo nokulisebenzisa kwisivakalisi. Ubenzisa imisebenzi ephulaphulwayo efunisa ukuba abafundi
bachonge izandi zamagama oLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo abawaziyo Le misebenzi mayibe mifutshane (ithathe imizuzu
emi-5 ukuya kweli-10), zichakwe zigqibe iveki yonke. Zinokubandakanywa kwimisebenzi yokuPhulaphula nokuThetha,
nokuFunda.
Iiveki: ukusuka kweyoku-1 ukuya kweyesi-5
• Achonge unxulumano phakathi konobumba nesandi koonobumba abahamba bodwa, bekuqaphela ukwahluka ekubizweni
 kwezandi phakathi koLwimi lwaseKhaya noLwimi oloNgezelelweyo.
• Abaqaphele oonombini (‘ch’, ‘th’, ‘bh’) ekuqaleni kwegama, umz. Ch - Chuma, Th - Thabo, bh-ibhola
• Ahlule phakathi kwezandi zezikhamiso xa eziva, umz. ch - cha che chi cho chu
• Abize ngokuvakalayo amagama amafutshane anoonobumba aba-3 nawanoonobumba aba-4 esebenzisa izandi
 azifundileyo.
• Ukwakha amagama esebenzisa izandi ezakhiwe ngamaqabane amathathu (oonontathu) noonone
Iiveki: ukusuka kweyesi-6 ukuya kweye-10
• Aqaphele oonombini ( ‘ph’, ‘th’) ekupheleni kwegama, umz. thetha, chopha
• Aqaphele izikhamiso ezingoonombini, umz. ‘oo’ “ii” oomama, iigusha, iindlulamthi
• Abize amagama amafutshane akhiwe ngoonobumba aba 3 naba-4 esebenzisa izandi azifundileyo
Ukufunda notitshala (ixesha lithe labekelwa bucala phantsi kweziFundo zokuPhulaphula nokuThetha)
Ukufunda notitshala ngumsebenzi oquka ukufunda nokuphulaphula; uthi uchaphazele kwanokuthetha kuba abafundi
bathetha ngetekisi kunye notishala wabo. KwiBanga lesi-3 ukuFunda noTitshala luza kuqhuba, kodwa njengenxalenye
yokuPhulaphula nokuThetha
• Afunde itekisi emfutshane ebhaliweyo, umzekelo kwiNcwadi eNkulu okanye itekisi eyandisiweyo, kunye notishala,
 besebenzisa isihloko ukuqikelela ukuba ingantoni na
• Aphendule imibuzo elula nengqalileyo ngebali, umzekelo, ‘Yenza ntoni inkwenkwe esikolweni?’’
• Achaze ukuba ibali limenze waziva njani na, esebenzisa neelwimi ezininzi ngaxeshanye ngokuzitshintsha-tshintsha, ukuba
 kuyimfuneko.
UkuFunda ngamaQela noTitshala (elona xesha lincinane yimizuzu engama-45, elona lininzi yiyure e-1 nemizuzu eli-15)
Xa ootitshala besebenzisa elona xesha lininzi kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, mabayahlule iklasi ibe ngamaqela ama-5
abafundi bantanganye ngokumgangatho. Mabasebenzise iinkqubo ngokufunda okuhleliweyo, baze baqinisekise ukuba iqela
ngalinye lisebenza ngetekisi ezisemgangathweni ekufundiswa kuwo, umyinge wokuwaqonda ngokuchanekileyo amagama
ube phakathi kwamamshumi alithoba nalithoba elinesihlanu ekhulwini (90-95%). Utitshala makasebenze neqela ngalinye
yonke imihla imizuzu eli-15. Eli xa asebenza neqela elithile, abanye abafundi baya kube befunda ngababini okanye umntu
ezifundela, okanye besenza imisebenzi eyayame apha kwiyetekisi efundwayo .
Apho ootitshala basebenzisa khona elona xesha lincinane kuLwimi oloNgezelelweo lokuQala, mabakhokele iklasi yonke
kwizifundo ezikhokelweyo, kathathu ngeveki.
• A funde ngokuvalayo kwincwadi yakhe kwiqela elifunda notitshala. Oku kuthetha ukuba iqela lonke lifunda ibali elinye
 okanye itekisi eyinyani efana notitshala
• Asebenzise iindlela ezingamaqhinga okufunda azifundiswe kuLwimi lwaseKhaya ukuze izinto ziyondelelane engqondweni,
 akwazi naye ukuzilungisa xa efunda (izandi, imixholo zikhokelo, ukuhlahlela ukwakhiwa, amagama aqhelekileyo)
• Afunde ngokuqhabalaka okukhulu eyiva into ayifundayo

• Abonise ukuziqonda iziphumlisi xa afunda ngokuvakalayo

Ukufunda eyedwa (ngexesha abafundi bengenzi nto esikolweni nasekhaya)
Abafundi bamelwe ukuba bakhuthazwe bazifundele bebodwa kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo xa bengenzi nto eklasini,
umzekelo, ukuba utitshala usebenze neqela elifunda likhokelwa, okanye xa bewugqibe umsebenzi ngaphambi kwexesha
Kubalulekile ukuba lonke ithuba elivelayo eklasini lisetyenziselwe ukuphuhlisa ukufunda. Abafundi mabasoloko bekhuthazwa
ukuba bafunde ekhaya njengomsebenzi wasekhaya.
• Afunde into ayibhalileyo naleyo ibhalwe ngabanye
• Azifundele eyedwa iincwadi ezifundwe kwiindibano zoFundo oluKhokelweyo neencwadi ezinezihloko ezilula, neencwadi
 zamabali ayimifanekiso kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo kwikona yokufunda yaseklasini
• Afunde imibongo nezicengcelezo eziqhelekileyo
• Asebenzise izichazi magama zabafundi ezinemifanekiso, umzekelo, ezilwiminye okanye zimalwimi axandileyo, bafumane
 intsingiselo yamagama abangawaziyo

UKUBHALA IXESHA LOKUFUNDISA ELICETYISWAYO
 Ukubhala:
 Elona xesha lincinane: imizuzu angama-30 ngeveki
 Elona xesha lininzi: iyure e-1 ngeveki
 Ukusetyenziswa kolwimi: imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki
UMXHOLO/IINGQIQO/IZAKHONO
Imisebenzi yokubhala (imizuzu engma-30 ubuncinci, iyure e-1elona xesha lininzi ngeveki)
Umsebenzi wokubhala ophambili ube mnye okanye ibe mibini apho kusetyenziswa elona xesha lininzi, uze ube mnye
xa kusetyenziswa elona lincinane
• Asebenzise izakhono zokubhala ngesandla ezifundiswe kuLwimi lwaseKhaya
• Abhale eziya ziba nzima izintlu ezinezihloko, umz. Izilwanyana zasendle: iindlovu,iindlulamthi, iingonyama, amaqwarhashe
• Abhale itekisi elula, umzekelo, ikhadi leminqweno emihle yemini yokuzalwa
• Elandela isikhokelo, abhale uthotho lwemiyalelo elula, umzekelo, iresipi,
• Abhale umhlathi omigca mi-4 ukuya esi-6 ngesihloko esiqhelekileyo
• Asebenzise inkqubo elandelwayo xa kubhalwa (ukuyila, ukubhala, ukuhlela nokupapashsa)
• Asebenzise iziphumlisi esezifundisiwe kuLwimi lwaseKhaya (izingxi, iikoma, iimpawu zombuzo, iimpawu zokukhuza
 neempawu zokucaphula)
• Apele amagama aqhelekileyo ngokuchanekileyo, aze alinge nalawo angaqhelekanga, esebenzisa ulwazi lwezandi
• Azakhele uvimba wesigama nesichazi-magama sakhe
• Asebenzise isischazi-magama sabafundi (esilwimi lunye nesineelwimi ezimbini)
Imisebenzi yokusetyenziswa kolwimi (imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki)
IKule misebenzi abafundi baya kuqalisa ukugxininisa kwiukusetyenziswa kolwimi esetyenziswayo. Kufuneka
iukusetyenziswa kolwimi ifundiswe ngendlela enika intsingiselo.
• Aqonde, asebenzise ixesha langoku elifutshane
• Aqonde asebenzise izivakalisi ezilula, umzekelo, ‘Incwadi iphezu kwetafile’.
• Aqonde asebenzise isimnini nezibizo, umzekelo, ‘Incwadi yam’
• Ahlaziye/azikhumbuze eminye imigaqo yeukusetyenziswa kolwimi efundwe ngokungekho sesikweni kwiBanga labaQalayo
 (R) ukuya kwelesi-2

CAPS 75
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

 IKOTA 2
UKUPHULAPHULA NOKUTHETHA (EZOMLOMO) IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDA

 Iyure e-1 ngeveki
UMXHOLO/IINGQIQO/IZAKHONO

Iiveki: ukusuka kweyoku-1 ukuya kweyesi-5

Ootitshala bakhetha imixholo emibini ebavumela ukuba bafundise isigama abamane besihlaziya besivusela, kwaye
bafikelele kwimisebenzi edweliswe ngezantsi apha. Qaphela ukuba imixholo okanye izihloko ezicetyiswayo ziingcebiso nje.
Ootitshala mabazikhethele imixholo efanele okanye elungele bona ngokuxhomekeke kwiimeko nezixhobo ezifumanekayo
kubo. Mabaqiniseke ukuba banazo izinto ezifunekayo ezinjengeeNcwadi eziNkulu okanye iiphowusta, izicengcelezo,
iingoma, imidlalo nezinto eziyinyani ezilungele imixholo abayikhethileyo.

Ootitshala mabazame ukufikelela imisebenzi emininzi kangangoko banakho, kwixesha elifumanekayo. Kufuneka bahlole
abafundi besenzisa imiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho seSikweni ecetyiswe ukuba yenziwe ukusuka kwiveki yoku-1 ukuya
kweyesi-5.
Iiveki ukusuka kweyesi-6 ukuya kweye-10

Ootitshala bakhethe imixholo emibini emitsha eya kubavumele ukuba bongezelele koko sebekwenzile kwiiveki ezintlanu
zokuqala. Imixholo mayibavumele ukuba baqalisee isigama esitsha nokugxininisa kubethelelwe isigama esesifundisiwe.
Imixholo mayivumele nokuba utitshala asoloko evuselela izakhiwo ezilula ezisiseko, eli xesha esabelana ngolwazi nabafundi
kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo.

Ootitshala mabazame ukufikelela kuyo ibe mininzi kangangoko imisebenzi kwixesha elifumanekayo. Mabahlole abafundi
besebenzisa imiSebenzi yokuHlola engekho seSikweni, nekucetyiswe ukuba yenziwe ukusuka kwineki yesi-6 ukuya
kweye-10. Kumelwe ukuba bathabathise ngemiSebenzi yokuHlola eseSikweni.

Imisebenzi yemihla ngemihla(imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki)
Imisebenzi iyahluka ngobude. Utitshala unokwenza umsebenzi omdana omnye okanye ibe mibini nemithathu emifutshane
ngeveki.
• Aqhube ephuhlisa isigama somlomo (ukuphulaphula nokuthetha), besebenzisa imixholo okanye izihloko ezinjengesi.
 ‘Growing Things’
• Alandele imiyalelo, umzekelo, ‘Zalisa imbiza ngomhlaba. Faka imbewu. Ugqubelele ngokuthi sa umhlaba. Okokugqibela,
 unkcenkceshele.
• Anike imiyalelo elula, umzekelo, ‘ Nkcenkceshela ngoku.’
• Aqonde aphendule imibuzo elula njengale: ‘Nini....?’ ‘Yintoni...’, umzekelo, ‘ Imbewu iya kukhula ibe njani?’’
• Acele ukucaciselwa, umzekelo, ‘Andiqondi kakuhle. Ndicela undichazele kwakhona.’
• Aphendule, enze izicelo, umzekelo, ‘Ndingasisebenzisa isichazi-magama?’
• Athathe inxaxheba kwincoko emfutshane kwisihloko esiqhelekileyo, umzekelo, incoko engabahlobo bakhe nezinto
 abazenzayo.
• Achonge into ngokuphulaphula inkcazelo emfutshane, umzekelo, ‘Ndimncinane ndilukhuni. Ukuba undinika umhlaba
 namanzi, ndikhula ndibe sisityalo. Ndingubani?’
• Athethe ngezinto ezisemfanekisweni, okanye ifoto ephendula, esabela imiyalelo katitshala, umzekelo, ‘Ubona ntoni
 efotweni? Ndixelele into ongayibonayo efotweni.’
• Aphulaphule amabal abaliswayo, umzekelo, utitshala abaxelele ngokukhulisa isityalo. ‘Ndiqala ndigalele umhlaba embizeni
 iphantse ukugcwala. Ndandule ukufaka imbewu ndiyigqubelele ngomhlaba, ndiyinkcenkceshele. Ndiyibukele ikhula.’
• Abalise ibali elifutshane ngomlomo, umzekelo, abalise ngokutyala imbewu nokukhula kwesityalo.
• Aqonde asebenzise izakhiwo zolwimi kwimeko ezifanelelyo, umzekelo, ixesha eladlulayo, ‘Ndankcenkceshela isityalo,
 njalo-njalo.
• Afunde ngentloko enze imibongo elula, izicengcelezo neengoma, umzekelo, ‘Siza kutyala iimbotyi esitiyeni.’
• Adlale umdlalo wolwimi, umzekelo, ‘Ndiqhwaba izandla.’

 76 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha okugxininiswayo (imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki)

Ukuphulaphul amabali abaliswayo nafundwayo (Ukufunda notitshala)

Ngokuxhomekeke kwixesha elifumanekayo, utitshala ubalisa okanye afunde ibali, (okanye ibali elingeziganeko),
kabini ngeveki. Amabali abaliswayo anokwenziwa imidlalo, kusetyenziswa izijekulo nezixhobo zokuphuhlisa
intsingiseli. Amabali afundwayo mawathathwe kwiNcwadi eNkulu okanye iphowusta enemifanekiso, apho abafundi
banokubona imifanekiso.
• Ukuphulaphula amabali amafutshane, amabali angeziganeko ezehlele umntu okanye amabali ayinyani, umzekelo,
 iziganeko eziyinyani, imiyalelo, iingxelo zolwazi, ezibaliswa okanye zifundwa kwiNcwadi eNkulu okanye iphowusta
 enemifanekiso, bezonwabisa
• Aphulaphule amabali namabali angeziganeko, aphendule imibuzo engengqiqo, umzekelo, ‘Wawuxhwitha njani umnqathe?’
• Aqikelele okuza kwenzeka kwibali elilandelayo, okanye ibali lomntu, umzekelo, ‘Ucinga ukuba kuza kwenzeka ntoni kwixa
 elizayo?’
• Achaze imvakalelo yakhe ngebali, ‘Ulithandile ibali? Yintoni oyithandileyo, iyintoni ongayithandanga? Nika izizathu.’
• Abalise ibali eliphinda.
• Abalise itekisi eyinyani, njengebali elingeziganeko okanye ingxelo yolwazi, umzekelo, ‘Ukukhulisa izityalo’, nokuphendula
 imibuzu engengqiqo, umzekelo, ‘Ziintoni omelwe kukuba nazo xa ulima izityalo?’
• Ngoncedo lukatitshala, anike isishwankanthelo setekisi eyinyani.

Ukuphuhlisa isigama sengqiqo, isigama nezakhiwo zolwimi

Ngokuthatha inxaxheba kule misebenzi ingentla:
• Aqhube esakha isigama somlomo, kuqukwa nesigama sengqiqo, umzekelo ukuchaza into ukuqhubeka kwento
 ngokwamanqanaba okwenzeka kwayo, umzekelo, ibali ngobomi
• Aqalise ukuphuhlisa ukuqonda nokukwazi ukusebenzisa izakhiwo zolwimi ezilula kwiimeko ezinentsingiselo yolwimi
 oluthethwayo, umzekelo, izibizo ezingezinto ezibalwayo nezingabalwayo, umzekelo, imbewu, amanzi, isitiya, njalo-njalo.
UKUHLOLA

Iingcebiso ngemiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho seSikweni:

Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha: (ezomlomo okanye ezenziwayo)

Iiveki: ukusuka kweyoku-1 ukuya kweyesi-5
• Athethe ngomfanekiso okanye ifoto
• Athathe inxaxheba kwincoko emfutshane ngesihloko esiqhelekileyo

Iiveki: ukusuka kweyesi-6 ukuya kweye-10
• Aphulaphule ibali lomlomo elilula elingeziganeko
• Abalise ibali lomlomo elilula elingeziganeko

UmSebenzi wokuHlola okuseSikweni wesi-2:

Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha (ezomlomo okanye ezenziwayo)
• Aphulaphule itekisi eyinyani, aze aphendule ngomlomo imibuzo engengqiqo
• Abonise ukusiqonda isigama somlomo ngokukhomba izinto eziseklasini okanye emfanekisweni, ephendula imibuzo
 okanye imiyaleleo katitshala, umzekelo, ‘Yintoni le?’ ‘yalatha iingcambu zesityalo?’’

Umsebenzi wokuHlola okuseSikweni wesi-3:

Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha ezomlomo okanye ezenziwayo)
• Aphulaphule ibali abuye alibalise
• Abonakalise ukuqonda isigama somlomo ngokwalatha izinto eziseklasini okanye umfanekiso ephendula imibuzo okanye
 imiyalelo katitshala, umzekelo, ‘Yintoni le?’ ‘Yalatha iingcambu zesityalo’.

 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

 IKOTA 2
UKUFUNDA NEZANDI IXESHA LOKUFUNDISA ELICETYISWAYO

 Elona xesha lincinane: iyure e-1 ngeveki

 Elona xesha lininzi: iyure e-1 imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki
UMXHOLO/IINGQIQO/IZAKHONO

Izandi (imizuzu eli-15 ngeveki)

Utitshala uqalisa ukufundisa izikhamiso ezingoonombini, nokuxubana/ukudityaniswa kweezandi (amaqabane), esebenzisa
amagama abafundi abasele bewazi okanye amagama aqhelelkileyo. Xa esiza negama elitsha, utitshala uyaqinisekisa ukuba
abafundi bayayiqonda intsinselo yalo, kwaye bangalisebenzisa kwisivakalisi. Ubonelela ngemisebenzi emanyelwayoefuna
ukuba abafundi anakane amagama azandi zifanayo. Le misebenzi mayibe mifutshane (imizuzu emi-5 ukuya kweli-10),
yahlulwa-hlulwe igqibe iveki. Inokudityaniswa kwimisebenzi yokuphulaphula nokuthetha, nemisebenzi yokufunda.

Iiveki ukusuka kweyoku-1 ukuya kweyesi- 5

Anakane ubuncinane oonombini bezikhamiso abatsha, umzekelo ‘oo’, ‘ii’ kula magama, loo,iinkomo.
• Afunde amagama nezivakalisi esebenzisa izandi esezifundiweyo, agxininise koonontathu noonone

Iiveki ukusuka kweyesi-6 ukuya kweye-10
• Asebenzise amaqabane axutyiweyo ukwakha nokuqhekeza amagama, i-dla-dla, i-hla-hla
• Anakane amagama aqhelekileyo anemfano-zandi, iqaqa
• Ahlule phakathikwezikhamiso ezide nezifutshane
• Afunde amagama nezivakalisi esebenzisa izandi ezifundiweyo

Ukufunda notitshala (Ixesha liye labiwa phantsi kwifundo zokuphulaphula nokuthetha)

Ukufunda notitshala kuquka imisebenzi yokufunda nokuphulaphula; ikwabandakanya ukuthetha kuba abafundi bayathetha
ngetekisi notitshala. KwiBanga lesi-3, ukuFunda notitshala kuyaqhuba, kodwa njengenxalenye yokuphulaphula nokuthetha
• Afunde itekisi emfutshane ebhaliweyo notitshala kwiNcwadi eNkulu okanye itekisi eyandisiweyo, asebenzisa isihloko
 ukuqikelela.
• Aphendule imibuzo elula ngebali
• Achaze ukuba ibali limenze wavakalelwa njani na, esebenzisa iilwimi ngokuzitshintsha-tshintsha, xa kuyimfuneko

Ukufunda ngamaqela bekhokelwa ( elona xesha yimizuzu engama-45, elona lininzi liyiyure e- enemizuzu eli-15
ngeveki)

Apho ootitshala basebenzisa elona xesha lininzi kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, mabayahlule iklasi ibe ngamaqela
amahlanu akumgangatho omnye wokufunda. Mabasebenzise inkqubo yokuhlela ngokufunda, baqinisekise ukuba iqela
ngalinye lisebenza ngeetekisi ekumgangatho wokufundisa, ukunakana amagama ngokuchanekileyo ngomyinge wamashumi
alithoba ukuya kumashumi alithoba anesihlanu (90% - 95%). Utitshala makasebenze neqela elinye yonke imihla imizuzu
eli-15. Eli xesha asebenza asebenza neli qela, abanye abafundi baya kufunda ngababini okanye eyedwa okanye benze
imisebenzi enxulumene netekisi efundwayo. Apho ootitshala basebenzisa elona lincinane ixesha kulwimi lokuqala
oloNgezelelweyo, mababenze izifundo ezikhokelwayo neklasi yonke kathathu ngeveki.
• Afunde incwadi yakhe ngokuvakalayo kufundo ngamaqela ekhokelwa, besebenza notitshala. Oku kuthetha ukuba iqela
 lonke lifunda ibali elifanayo okanye itekisi eyinyani kunye notitshala
• Asebenzise iindlela zokufunda ezifundiswe kulwimi lwaseKhaya, ukuze ivakale akwazi ukuzilungisa iziphoso xa efunda
 (izandi, iimeko ezikhokelayo,ukuhlahlela izakhiwo, amagama aqhelekileyo)
• Afunde ngokungathintithiyo nokuchebeleza okukhulayo nokuvakalelwa
• Abonise ukuziqonda iziphumlisi xa afunda ngokuvakalayo
• Aqhube esakha isigama samagama aqhelekileyo esivela kufundo olukhokelweyo, kufundo notitshala nayedwa

Abafundi mabakhuthazwe ukuba bazifundele bebodwa kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, xa bengenzi nto eklasini,
umzekelo, xa utitshala esebenza neqela elifunda likhokelwa, okanye xa bewugqibe phambi kwexesha umsebenzi.

bekhuthazwa ukuba bazifundele ekhaya njengomsebenzi wasekhaya.

 kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo kwiikona zokufunda zaseklasini

UKUPHULAPHULA NOKUTHETHA (EZOMLOMO) IXESHA LUKUFUNDISA ELICETYIWEYO
