Iiveki ukusuka yoku-1 ukuya kweye-5

Ootitshala bakhetha imixholo emibini eya kubavumela ukuba baqalise isigama basivuselele, baze bafikelele kwimisebenzi
edweliswe ngezantsi apha. Uphawule ukuba imixholo okanye izihloko ezicetyiswayo, eneneni ziingcebiso nje. Ootitshala
mabakhethe imixholo ebafaneleyo nebalungeleyo ngokuxhomekeke kwiimeko abakuzo nemithombo abanayo.
Mabaqinisekise ukuba banazo izinto eziyimfuneko njengeeNcwadi eziNkulu neephowusta, izicengcelezo, iingoma,
imidlalo nezinto ezizizo ukulungiselela umxholo abawukhethileyo. Mabazame kangangoko banakho ukufikelela kwemininzi
imisebenzi abanokuyenza ngexesha abalifumanayo.

Ootitshala mabahlole abafundi besebenzisa imisebenzi yokuHlola engekho seSikweni njengoko kucetyisiwe kwiveki yoku-1
ukuya kweyesi-5.

Iiveki yesi-6 ukuya kweue-10

Ootitshala bakhetha imixholo emibini emitsha eya kubavumela ukuba bakhele phezu koko bebekwenze kwiiveki
ezintlanu zokuqala. Le mixholo iya kubavumela ukuba baqalise isigama esitsha babethelele eso sebesifundisile. Imixholo
mayimvumele utitshala athi rhoqo ahlaziye evuselela izakhiwo zolwimi ezisisiseko nezilula, njengokuba esabelana nabafundi
kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo.

Ootitshala mabazame kangangoko banakho ukufikelela kwimisebenzi eminizi abanokuyenza ngexesha abanalo. Mabahlole
abafundi besebenzisa imisebenzi yokuHlola engekho seSikweni njengoko icetyisiwe kwiveki yoku-1 ukuya kwesi-5.
Mabaqalise ngemisebenzi yokuHlola okuseSikweni..

Imisebenzi yemihla ngemihla (imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki)

Aqhube ephuhlisa isigama somlomo (ukuphulaphula nokuthetha), esebenzisa imixholo okanye izihloko.
• Alandele imiyalelo, umzekelo, ‘Kwakufuneka ube nephepha, ikoki, isikere nomsonto wokubopha imaski. Qala uzobe
 ubume bobuso bakho, njalo-njalol.
• Anike imiyalelo elula, umzekelo,, ‘Yifake umbala omthubi.’
• Aqonde aphendule imibuzo elula enjengale, ‘Nini …?’ ‘Kutheni …?’ ‘njani…?’, umzekelo,Uyenza njani imaski
 eyoyikekayo?’
• Acele ukucaciselwa, umzekelo, ‘Andiqondi kakuhle. Khawundincede wethu.’
• Aphendule enze izicelo, umzekelo, ‘Ndingasisebenzisa isichazi-magama?’
• Athabathe inxaxheba kwincoko emfutshane kwisihloko esiqhelekileyo, umzekelo, ‘Izinto eyenzekayo ekuhlaleni’.
• Achonge into kwinkcazelo yomlomo emfutshane, umzekelo, abeke imifanekiso ngokulandelelana kwayo emva
 kokuphulaphula imiyalelo yokwenza imaski
• Athethe ngezinto ezisemfanekisweni okanye ifoto ephendula imiyalelo katitshala, umzekelo, ‘Yintoni onokuyibona
 efotweni? Ndixelele into onokuyibona efotweni.’
• Aphulaphule amabali angeziganeko, umzekelo, utitshala ubalisa ngendlela ayenze ngayo imaski eyoyikisayo: ‘Ndiqale
 ndazoba ukuma kobuso bam. Ndaza ndazoba amehlo, impumlo nomlomo, njalo-njalo.’
• Anike ibali elilula ngesiganeko elibalisa ngomlomo, umzekelo, ebalisa ngokwenza imaski
• Aqonde asebenzise izakhiwo zolwimi kwimeki ezichanaekileyo, umzekelo, izalathisi: ‘Nceda uchole elo phepha. Yeyamle
 ncwadi.’
• Afunde ngentloko enze imibongo emifutshane, izicengcelelzo neengoma
• Adlale imidlalo yolwimi

 eza kwenzeka?

 imaski, aphendule nemibuzo yengqiqo, umzekelo, ‘Ziintoni ezifunekayo xa usenza imaski?’
• Ngoncedo lukatitshala, anike isishwankathelo seetekisi eziyinyani

 IKOTA 3
UKUFUNDA NEZANDI IXESHA ELICETYISWAYO LOKUFUNDA

 Elona xesha lincinane: iyure e-1 ngeveki

 Elona xesha lininzi: iyure e-1 imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki
UMXHOLO/IINGQIQO/IZAKHONO

Izandi (imizuzu eli-15 ngeveki)

Utitshala uqalisa oonombini bezikhamiso namaqabane axutyiweyo amatsha, esebenzisa amagama abasele bewazi abafundi
okanye amagama aqhelekileyo. Xa eqalisa igama elitsha, utitshala uyaqinisekisa ukuba abafundi bayayiqonda intsingiselo
yalo, kwaye bakwazi ukulisebenzisa kwizivakalisi. Uza nemisebenzi emanyelwayo efuna abafundi baphawule umahluko
kunxulumano phakathi kwezandi nopelokulwimi lwasekhaya nolo longezelelweyo. Le misebenzi mayibe mifutshane (imizuzu
emi-5 ukuya kweli-10) yahlulwe inikane izithuba evekini apha. Basenokuyibandakanya nemisebenzi yokuPhulaphula
nokuThetha, naleyo yokufunda.

Iiveki yoku-1 ukuya kwesi-5
• Ahlule phakathi kwezandi z ezikhamiso ezahlukileyo ngokuziva zibizwa, umzekelo, ‘intaka’,
• Anakana umahluko othile kunxulumano phakathi kwesandi nopelo kulwimi lwasekhaya nolo longezelelweyo,
• Anakane ubuncinane oonombini bezikhamiso abatsha
• Afunde, abhale amagama nezivakalisi esebenzisa izandi ezifundiweyo

Iiveki eyesi-6 ukuya kweye-10
• Ahlule phakathi kwezandi zezikhamiso ezahlukileyo ngokuziphulaphula
• Anakane amaqabane angoonontathu ekuqaleni nasekupheleni kwegama, umz. nts, ngq, ngx njalo-njalo
• Awazi amagama nezivakalisi esebenzisa izandi ezifundiweyo

Ukufunda notitshala (Ixesha labiwe phantsi kokuPhulaphula nokuThetha)

Ufunda notitshala umsebenzi wokufunda nokuphulaphula; ukwachaphazela nokuthetha kuba abafundi bayathetha notitshala
ngetekisi. KwiBanga lesi-3, ukuFunda noTitshala kuya kuqhuba, kodwa njengenxalenye yokuPhulaphula nokuThetha
• Afunde itekisi emfutshane ebhaliweyo notitshala kwiNcwadi eNkulu okanye itekisi eyandisiweyo, esebenzisa isihloko
 ukuqikelela
• Aphendule imibuzo elula ngebali,
• Achaze ukuba ibali limenze waziva njani na, etshintsha-tshintsha iilwimi ukuba kuyimfuneko

Ukufunda beliQela noTitshala (Elona lincinane imizuzu engama- 45, elona lininzi iyure e-1 nemizuzum eli-15)

Apho ootitshala basebenzisa elona xesha lininzi kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, baya kuyahlula iklasi ibe ngamaqela
ama-5 alinganayo ngomgangatho wokufunda. Mabasebenzise inkqubo yokufunda ehleliweyo, baze baqinisekise ukuba
iqela ngalinye lisebenza ngetekisi ezisemgangathweni wokufunda, ube umyinge wokunakana amagama ngokuchanekileyo
uphakathi kwamashumi alithoba ukuya kwalithoba aneihlanu ekhulwini (90 - 95%). Utitshala makasebenze neqela elinye
usuku ngalunye imizuzu eli-15. Eli xesha asebenza neli qela, abanye abafundi baya kufunda ngababini okanye ngabanye
okanye benze imisebenzi enxulumene netekisi efundwayo.

Apho ootitshala basebenzisa elona xesha lincinane kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, mabaqhube ufundo notitshala
lweklasi yonke kathathu ngeveki.
• Afunde ngokuvakalayo incwadi kufundo olukhokelwayo notitshala. Loo nto ithethe ukuthi lonke iqela lifunda ibali elinye
 okanye itekisi enye notitshala
• Asebenzise iindlela ezingamaqhinga okufunda ezifundiswe kuLwimi lwaseKhaya ukuze kubekho into evakalayo azilungise
 neziphoso xa efunda (izandi iingqiqo zikhokelo, ukuhlalutywa kwesakhiwo, amagama aqhelekileyo
• Afunde ngokungathintilizi nokuvakalelwa
• Abonise ukuziqonda iziphumlisi xa efunda ngokuvakalyo
• Aqhube esakha isigama samagama aqhelekileyo kufundo olukhokelweyo, kunye notitshala nayedwa

Ukufunda uwedwa (ngexesha abafundi bengenzi nto esikolweni nasekhaya)

Abafundi mabakhuthazwe bazifundule ngokwabo kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, xa benexesha lokungenzi nto
eklasini, umzekelo, ukuba utitshala usebenza neqela elikhokelwa ekufundeni, okanye xa bethe bawugqibaumsebenzi
phambi kwexesha. Kubalulekile ukuba onke amathuba afumanekayo eklasi asetyenziswe ukuphuhlisa ukufunda. Abafundi
mabakhuthazwe ukuba bafunde ekhay njengomsebenzi wasekhaya
• Afunde akubhalileyo nokubhalwe ngabanye
• Azifundele iincwadi ezifundwa kwiindibano zofundo ngokhokelo, kwakunye neencwadi zezihloko ezilula neencwadi
 zamabali ezinemifanekiso kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo kwiikona zokufunda eklasini
• Afunde imibongo nezicengelezo eziqhelekileyo
• Asebenzise izichazi-magama nemifanekiso zabafundi (ezilwimi nye nezilwimi mbini), bafumane iintsingiselo zamagama
 abangawaziyo

Izandi: (ezomlomo okanye ezenziwayo okanye ezibhalwayo)
• Anakane umahluko othile kunxulumano phakathi kwesandi nopelo
• Afunde, abhale amagama nezivakalisi esebenzisa izandi ezifundiweyo

Umsebenzi wokuHlola okuseSikweni:

Izandi: (ezomlomo okanye ezenziwayo okanye ezibhalwayo)
• Ahlule phakathi kwezandi zezikhamiso ezahlukileyo, umzekelo
• Abazi ubuncinane oonombini bezikhamiso abatsha namaqabane

Umsebenzi wokuHlola osseSikwenu wesi-5:

Izandi: (ezomlomo okanye ezenziwayo okanye ezibhalwayo)
• Anakane amaqabane angoonontathu emagameni nakwizivakalisi
• Afunde, abhale amagama nezivakalisi esebenzisa izandi ezifundiweyo

• Abalise inxalenye yebali encediswa ngutitshala

Ukufunda beliqela notitshala
• Abonise ukuqonda nokuchebeleza xa efunda notitshala kumgangatho wakhe

Imisebenzi yokuHlola okuseSikweni eceetyiswayo yesi-4 nesi-5

mayifundiswe ngokomxholo.

UKUPHULAPHULA NOKUTHETHA (EZOMLOMO) IXESHA LOKUFUNDISA ELICETYIWEYO

 Iyure e-1 ngeveki
UMXHOLO/IINGQIQO/IZAKHONO

Iiveki eyoku-1 ukuya kweyesi-5

Ootitshala bakhetha imixholo emibini eya kubavumela ukuba bazise isigama basibethelele, basivuselele, baze bafikelele
kwimisebenzi edweliswe ngezantsi apha. Uphawule ukuba imixholo okanye izihloko ezicetyiswayo, eneneni ziingcebiso
nje. Ootitshala mabakhethe imixholo ebafaneleyo nebalungeleyo ngokuxhomekeke kwiimeko abakuzo nezixhobo abanazo.
Mabaqinisekise ukuba banazo izinto eziyimfuneko njengeeNcwadi eziNkulu neephowusta, izicengcelezo, iingoma, imidlalo
nezinto ezizizo ukulungiselela umxholo abawukhethileyo.

Ootitshala mabazame kangangoko banakho ukufikelela kwemininzi imisebenzi abanokuyenza ngexesha abalifumanayo.
Mabahlole abafundi besebenzisa imisebenzi yokuHlola engekho seSikweni njengoko kucetyisiwe kwiveki yoku-1 ukuya
kweyesi-5.
Iiveki yesi-6 ukuya kweye-10

Ootitshala bakhetha imixholo emibini emitsha eya kubavumela ukuba bakhele phezu koko bebekwenze kwiiveki
ezintlanu zokuqala. Le mixholo iya kubavumela ukuba baqalise isigama esitsha babethelele eso sebesifundisile. Imixholo
mayimvumele utitshala athi rhoqo ahlaziye evuselela izakhiwo zolwimi ezisisiseko nezilula, njengokuba esabelana nabafundi
kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo

Ootitshala mabazame kangangoko banakho ukufikelela kwimisebenzi eminizi abanokuyenza ngexesha abanalo. Mabahlole
abafundi besebenzisa Imisebenzi yokuHlola engekho seSikweni njengoko icetyisiwe kwiveki yoku-1 ukuya kwesi-5.
Mabathabathise ngemisebenzi yokuHlola okuseSikweni

Imisebenzi yemihla ngemihla (imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki)
Imisebenzi iyahluka ngobude. Utitshala angenza umsebenzi omnye omde okanye ibe mibini okanye ibe mithathu
emifutshane ngeveki.

Aqhube ephuhlisa isigama somlomo, (ukuphulaphula nokuthetha), esebenzisa imixholo okanye izihloko ezinjengezi:
Ezothutho
• Alandele anike imiyalelo
• Aqonde aphendule imibuzo elula
• Acele ukucaciselwa
• Aphendule enze izicelo
• Athathe inxaxheba kwincoko emfutshane ngesihloko esiqhelekileyo, umzekelo, indlela eya esikolweni
• Achonge into kwizinto ezichazwe ngomlomo, umzekelo, ‘Yinqwelo le. Inkulu kakhulu inde. Ihambisa izinto zisuka efektri
 izisa emarikeni’
• Athethe ngezinto ezisemfanekisweni ephendula imiyalelo, umzekelo, ‘Ungamibona uloliwe efotweni? Sibonise uloliwe.’
• Aphulaphule abuye abalise iziganeko ngomlomo, umzekelo, ‘Uhambo lwam oluya esikolweni’
• Aqonde abuaye asebenzise izakhiwo zolwimi kwimeko ezifaneleyo
• Afunde ngentloko, aze enze imidlalo elula ngemibongo, izicengcelezo neengoma, umzekelo, ‘Amavili enqwelo ayajikeleza
• Adlale imidlalo yolwimi

Ukuphulaphula nokuthetha okugxininiswa (imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki)

Ukuphulaphula amabali abaliswayo nafundwayo Ukufunda noTitshala)
• Utitshala ubalisa okanye afunde amabali (okanye iziganeko), kabini ngemini. Amabali abaliswayo anokudlalwa,
 esebenzisa izijekulo nempahla yeqonga, ukuxhasa intsingiselo. Amabali afundwayo mawathathwe kwiNcwadi
 eNkulu okanye iphowusta enemifanekiso apho abafundi banokubona imifanekiso kakuhle.

• Aphulaphule amabali amafutshane, iziganeko ezehlele umntu okanye iitekisi eziyinyani, umzekelo, amabali
 aneenkcukacha, imiyalelo,iingxelo ezingeenkcukacha, okanye kufundwe kwiNcwadi eNkulu okanye iphowusta
 yokuzonwabisa

• Aphulaphule amabali, neziganeko, aphendule imibuzo engengqiqo

UKUFUNDA NEZANDI IXESHA LOKUFUNDA ELICETYISWAYO

Izandi (imizuzu eli-15 ngeveki)

Utitshala uqalisa oonombini bezikhamiso namaqabane axutyiweyo amatsha, esebenzisa amagama abasele bewazi abafundi
okanye amagama aqhelekileyo. Xa eqalisa igama elitsha, utitsahala uyaqinisekisa ukuba abafundi bayayiqonda intsingiselo
yalo, kwaye bakwazi ukulisebenzisa kwizivakalisi. Uza nemisebenzi emanyelwayo efuna abafundi baphawule umahluko
kunxulumano phakathi kwezandi nopelo kulwimi lwasekhaya nolo longezelelweyo. Le misebenzi mayibe mifutshane (imizuzu
emi-5 ukuya kweli-10) yahlulwe inikane izithuba evekini apha. Basenokuyibandakanya nemisebenzi yokuPhulaphula
nokuThetha, naleyo yokufunda.

Iiveki eyoku-1 ukuya kweyesi-5
• Abazi ubuncinane oonombini bezikhamiso abahlanu
• Afunde, abhale amagama nezivakalisi esebenzisa izandi ezifundiweyo, agxininise koonontathu noonone

Iiveki eyesi-6 ukuya kweye-10
• Anakane iifamili zamagama ezinzima
• Anakane asebenzise izimamva ezithile
• Afunde, abhale amagama nezivakalisi esebenzisa izandi ezifundiweyo, agxininise koonontathu noonone

Ukufunda notitshala (Ixesha labiwe phantsi kokuPhulaphula nokuThetha)

Ufunda notitshala ngumsebenzi wokufunda nokuphulaphula; ukwachaphazela nokuthetha kuba abafundi bayathetha
notitshala ngetekisi. KwiBanga lesi-3, ukuFunda noTitshala kuya kuqhuba, kodwa njengenxalenye yokuPhulaphula
nokuThetha
• Afunde itekisi emfutshane ebhaliweyo notitshala kwiNcwadi eNkulu okanye itekisi eyandisiweyo, esebenzisa isihloko
 ukuqikelela
• Aphendule imibuzo elula ngebali
• Achaze ukuba ibali limenze waziva njani na, etshintsha-tshintsha iilwimi ukuba kuyimfuneko

Ukufunda beliQela noTitshala (Elona lincinane imizuzu engama- 45, elona lininzi iyure e-1 nemizuzum eli-15)

Apho ootitshala basebenzisa elona xesha lininzi kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, baya kuyahlula iklasi ibe
ngamaqela ama-5 alinganayo ngomgangatho wokufunda. Mabasebenzise inkqubo yokufunda ehleliweyo, baze
baqinisekise ukuba iqela ngalinye lisebenza ngeetekisi ezisemgangathweni wokufunda, ube umyinge wokunakana
amagama ngokuchanekileyo uphakathi kwamashumi alithoba ukuya kwalithoba aneihlanu ekhulwini (90 - 95%).
Utitshala makasebenze neqela elinye usuku ngalunye imizuzu eli-15. Eli xesha asebenza neli qela, abanye abafundi
baya kufunda ngababini okanye ngabanye okanye benze imisebenzi enxulumene netekisi efundwayo.

Apho ootitshala basebenzisa elona xesha lincinane kulwimi lokuqala oloNgezelelweyo, mabaqhube ufundo
notitshala lweklasi yonke kathathu ngeveki.
• Afunde ngokuvakalayo incwadi kufundo olukhokelwayo notitshala. Loo nto ithethe ukuthi lonke iqela lifunda ibali elinye
 okanye itekisi enye notitshala
• Asebenzise iindlela ezingamaqhinga okufunda ezifundiswe kuLwimi lwaseKhaya ukuze kubekho into evakalayo azilungise
 neziphoso xa efunda (izandi iingqiqo zikhokelo, ukuhlalutywa kwesakhiwo, amagama aqhelekileyo
• Afunde ngokungathintilizi nokuvakalelwa
• Abonise ukuziqonda iziphumlisi xa efunda ngokuvakalyo
• Aqhube esakha isigama samagama aqhelekileyo kufundo olukhokelweyo, kunye notitshala nayedwa

UKUSETYENZISWA KOLWIMI Ukubhala:

 Elona xesha lincinane : imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki

 Elona xesha lininzi: iyure e-1 ngeveki

 Ukusetyenziswa kulwimi: imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki
UMXHOLO/IINGQIQO/IZAKHONO

Imisebenzi yokubhala (imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki)

Umsebenzi wokubhala omnye okanye emibini ngeveki apho ixesha elininzi lisetyenziswayo, ube mnye apho
kusetyenziswa elona xesha lincinane

Kuya kufuneka ukuba utitshala azise uhlobo olulandelayo -ukubhala ibali. Kuya kufuneka abafundi benikwe inkxaso
enkulu, utitshala aqalise ukubhala ibali ekunye neklasi. Okokuqala utitshala ubalisela abafundi ibali abasele belazi
abafundi. Aze utitshala ababuze abafundi ukuba ngoobani na abalinganiswa, indawo elenzeka kuyo ibali nokuba
kwenzeka ntoni na ebalini. Ubabuza ukuba kusetyenziswe liphi na ixesha ebalini. Andule ukucela iklasi ukuba
bacinge ngabalinganiswa abaza kubasebenzisa kwawabo amabali, bagqibe nangendawo elenzeka kuyo ibali nento
eyenzeka ebalini. Abafundi baxonxa ibali bencediswa ngutitshala, othi yena abhale ebhodini. Akuba utitshala
egqibile, ucela abafundi abahluka-hlukileyo ukuba bafunde ibali ngokuvakalayo. Abafundi bangakhuphela ibali
kwiincwadi zabo zomsebenzi.
• Asebenzise izakhono zokubhala ngesandla ezifundiwe kuLwimi lwaseKhaya
• Abhale izintlu ezinezihloko eziya ziba nzima, umzekelo, Iindidi zothutho ezahlukileyo, ibhayisekile,imoto, uloliweieksi
• Abhale umhlathi omigca mi- 5 ukuya kwesi-8 ngesihloko esiqhelekileyo
• Alungelelanise iinkcukacha kwitshati, itheyibhile okanye ibha-grafu
• Abhale iziganeko zobom bomntu,
• Ekunye notitshala, abhale ibali elilula akugqiba alikhuphele (Ukubhala notitshala)
• Asebenzise inkqubo yokubhala, alandelelanise, (ukuyile, ukubhala, ukuhlela nokupapasha)
• Asebenzise iziphumlisi ezisele zifundisiwe kuLwimi lwaseKhaya, umzekelo,izingxi, iikoma,iimpawu zokubuza, iimpawu
 zokukhuza neempawu zokucaphula
• Apele amagama aqhelekileyo ngokuchanekileyo, abuye azame ukupela lawo angaqhelekanga esebenzisa ulwazi lwezandi
• Asebenzise ixesha eladlulayo ngokuchanekileyo
• Akhe ibhanki yakhe yesigama nesichazi-magama
• Asebenzise izichazi-magama zabafundi (ezilwimi nye nezilwimi mbini)

Imisebenzi yokusetyenziswa koLwimi (imizuzu engama-30 ngeveki)

 Kule misebenzi abafundi baqalisa ukugxininisa kwiukusetyenziswa kolwimi esetyenzisiweyo.Kumelwe ukuba
iukusetyenziswa kolwimi ifundiswe ngokusemxholweni.
• Aqonde asebenzise ixesha elizayo, umzekelo, ‘ Ndiza kuhamba ngomso’
• Aqonde asebenzise izichazi .
• Ahlaziye inxalenye yeukusetyenziswa kolwimi efundiswe ngokungekho sesikweni kwiBanga labaQalayo (R) ukuya
 kwelesi-2

Iingcebiso ngemiSebenzi yokuHlola okungekho seSikweni:

Ukubhala: (ukubhala)
• Abhale ibali elilula exhaswa ngutitshala
• Asebenzise inkqubo yokubhala (ukuyila, ukubhala,ukuhlela nokupapasha)

Umsebenzi wokuHlola okuseSikweni : 6

Ukubhala: (ukubhala)
• Abhale imihlathi emigca mi-4 ukuya kwemi-6 ngesihloko esiqhelekileyo

Iingcebiso ngemisebenzi yokuHlola okungekho seSikweni:

Ukusetyenziswa koLwimi: (okubhalwayo)
• Agqibezele izivakalisi esebenzisa izichazi

Umsebenzi wokuHlola okuseSikweni : 6

Ukusetyenziswa koLwimi: (okubhalwayo)
• Abhale izivakalisi ezinentsingselo esebanzisa izichazi

 • Imifanekiso neepowusta

 • Iitshathi zealfabhethi

 ezibonisiweyo

 elingabhalwanga elibubukhulu ngobukhulu (A3, A4, A5), irula, irabha, iincwadi zokubhalela ezingabhalwanga

 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
AMAGAMA ASETYENZISWA RHOQO

Apha ngezantsi luluhlu lwamagama asetyenziswa rhoqo afumaneka kwiincwadi zamabali abafundi. Amanye ala
magama abonisa iintlobo zezihloko ezifumaneka kwiincwadi zabafundi (umz. umvundla, umthi). Kuba amabali
ebaliswa ngexesha elidlulileyo, izenzi zidla ngokuvela zikwixesha elidlulileyo.

Olu luhlu lufakelwe ukuncedisa utitshala ekuqondeni isigama esingundoqo ekufuneka abafundi benaso kulwimi
lwabo oloNgezelelweyo. La magama baya kuwafumana ngokuphulaphula nokuthetha isiXhosa, yaye baya kuwenza
abe yinxalenye yesigama sabo abasisebenzisayo ngokuwathetha nokuwabhala. La magama makangafundiswa
ngokungekho mxholweni.

Amagama afundwa ngabafundi kwiiklasi zoLwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo lokuQala akayi kufana ncam nala adweliswe
apha ngezantsi. Amagama abawafundayo aya kuxhomekeka kwizihloko azisebenzisayo utitshala kunye neencwadi
azifunda nabafundi. Kuba ulwimi oloNgezelelweyo lufundwa ngokuphulaphula nangokufunda, kulindeleleke
ukusetyenziswa kakhulu kwexesha elidlulileyo.

Icandelo 3: Amagama asetyenziswa rhoqo

 umva phantsi lila thatha kum
 ewe usisi bini yena vala
 amehlo yona hayi umama utata
 umlomo kunye naye nabo nye
 qala zonke thina mna wena
 intloko bona isandla hlala vula
 isisu ngena phuma ninzi khulu
 ingalo emva phambili khwela pheza
 phezulu yima misa hambisa hleka
 indlebe hambile hleli goduka beka
 unyawo susa suka ncuma ixesha
 yona kubo kuye iminwe yam
 bam zam kwam kwakhe kwabo
 kwazo igama amagama isisu intloko
 nceda phandle endlwini ngoku phakamisa
 utata umntu umfundi ibhola esikolweni
 nguye ndim nguwe ndathi yathi
 zathi bathi lathi athi ndim
 nguwe nguye ngabo bonke bodwa
 buza yeka yiza nayo naye
 nantsi le leyo umvundla esi
 isitya sam kakhulu kodwa kuba

Kweli phepha lilandelayo ngamagama asetyenziswa kakhudlwana

Olu luhlu sisikhokelo nje. Ootitshala kufuneka bawagcine amagama abakwaziyo ukuweva nokuwafunda abafundi.
Bakuba abafundi bekwazi ukufunda ngolwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo, utsitshala angaqala uDonga lwamaGama
esiXhosa eklasini, akhuthaze abafundi ukuba basebenzise izichazi-magama zabafundi ezilula. Bakuba abafundi
bekwazi ukubhala, utitshala angabakhuthazela ukuba babhale amagama amatsha abadibana nawo kwizichazi-
magama (okanye iincwadi zesigama) zabo. Angamana ebanika isigama esiqhelekileyo ebabizela nokubabizela.

Amagama aqhelekileyo alandelayo

 amanzi ibhere khangela fumana dibana
 kude andikwazi enye ekuqaleni yithi
 kuhle phinda kwakhona inkwenkwe kwangoko
 ndifuna ikati ngqukuva izilwanyana ebusuku
 ngaphaya ende umthi zange umbalisi
 njani izinto umlingo elandelayo encinci
 omncinci entsha endala kuqala imoto
 indoda ekuqaleni kowabo umsebenzi emsebenzini
 phindela efuna nabanye abaninzi thathu
 phi yitya ukutya biza intloko
 bobabini ehlathini udyakalashe phandle inkosi
 okanye yethu mhlawumbi usana edolophini
 thathile kabini indlela intlanzi abanye
 thethisa qumbile ngoko nika buyisa
 cinga impuku zonke ezinye abanye
 ikhaya ukudlala kufuneka ethile egadini
 ngubani mhlophe bomvu ebhedini khawuleza
 cotha lumka wacinga waqonda walila
 wahleka inja ekunene ekhohlo abaninzi
 ndiyazi kangaka ulwandle hlekisa funisa

 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
ICANDELO 4: IINTLOBO ZEETEKISI EZENZIWA KWISIGABA
ESISISISEKO
KwisiGaba esisisiSeko, abafundi baya kuphulaphula ezi ntlobo zeetekisi zilandelayo:

• amabalana abalisa ngezinto ezibehleleyo

• iindlela zokwenza izinto (imiyalelo)

• iingxelo zeenkcukacha

• (amabali) abalisayo

KwiBanga lesi-3 kuya kulindeleka ukuba banike amabalana abalisa ngabo okanye imiyalelo elula.

Njengokuba beqala ukufunda nokubhala ngolwimi lwabo oloNgezelelweyo, abafundi baya kufunda amabali,
amabalana, imiyalelo neengxelo zeenkcukacha ezilula. KwiBanga lesi-3, baya kubhala amabalana, imiyalelo namabali
bencediswa ngutitshala. KuLwimi lokuQala oloNgezelelweyo kucetyiswa ukuba kungafundiswa ukusetyenziswa
kwexesha elidlulileyo ekuqaleni kweBanga loku-1, ukuze bawabalise kwixesha langoku amabalana abo.

Apha ngezantsi yimizekelo yeentlobo zeetekisi ezisetyenziswa kwisiGaba esisisiSeko.

 eglasini ukwenzela ukuba siyibukele xa ikhula.

 ngokokulandelelana kwazo
 sayinkcenkceshela.

 Siqale, emva koko
 siye sabona ingcambu encinane.

 Emva kweeveki ezimbini, ingcambu ibinkudlwana.
 Ngomhla wesi-5 kuNovemba
 Siye sabona nesiqwana.
 Amagama anxulumene nomxholo

 ngaphezu komhlaba. Besinamagqabi aluhlaza. Ngomhla ingcambu, isiqwana, isingxotyana
 wama-26 kuSeptemba isityalo sembotyi sisifake etotini. 4. Isiphumo

 20cm. Besinamagqabi alishumi.

 Ngomhla wesi-5 kuNovemba, imbotyi yethu siye sayityala
 egadini yesikolo. Iye yaphuma iintyatyambo ezininzi.

 Izingxotyana zeembotyi ziye zaya zikhula ngokukhula.
 Bekukho iimbotyi ngaphakathi. Besinokuzityala ezi mbotyi
 kukhule isityalo esitsha.

 ezirhubuluzayo zinamaxolo ezikhumbeni zazo. Idla ngokuba yinkcazelo

 ubushushu bemizimba yazo. Ubushushu bemizimba yazo Iinyoka zifakwa phantsi kodidi lwezilwanyana
 buyafana nobendawo ezihleli kuyo. Zibizwa ngokuba ezirhubuluzayo
 zinegazi elibandayo, kodwa emva kokuba isilwanyana Amagama anxulumene nomxholo

 umzimba waso uba shushu. Izinto zicaciswa ngokuphangaleleyo

 Iintlobo zeenyoka 3. Iinyani
 Iintlobo ezimbini zeenyoka liphimpi / unobiya nerhamba. zibekwa ngokwemihlathi, umz. ukuba zinjani, zitya ntoni
 Amaphimbi/oonobiya ayelo/aqanda okanye amdaka aze Ixesha langoku (imo emfutshane amaxesha amaninzi)
 abe nebala elimnyama eliyipateni engu-v. Angamade zitya, azibulala
 kangangemitha. Izivakalisi zidla ngokuqala ngamagama ayintloko
 Amarhamba makhulu kunamaphimpi/oonobiya. Anombala Amarhamba ...
 omdaka bukhrim onepateni emdaka. Angamade Amaphimpi/oonobiya ...
 kangangeemitha ezintlanu.
 Amaphimpi/oonobiya atya amabuzi neempuku. Ezi
 zilwanyana azibulala ngetyhefu yabo enobuhlungu.
 Amarhamba atya izilwanyana ezikhudlwana ezifana
 nemifudla neembabala ezincinane. Azibulala ngokuzikhama
 ezi zilwanyana.

CAPS 97
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
Uluhlu lwamagama

ukuhlola (assessment) - inkqubo ecwangcisiweyo neqhubekayo yokuqokelela ulwazi malunga nokusebenza
komfundi, nokuba yenziwa ngokusesikweni okanye ngokungekho sesikweni.

ukuhlola okusisiseko (baseline assessment) - ukuhlola kokuqala okwenzelwa ukufumana ukuba sele esazi ntoni
umfundi. UkuHlola koNyaka kukaZwelonke (i-Annual National Assessment - ANA) kuya kwenziwa ekuqaleni kwekota
yokuqala kuwo onke amabanga yaye iya kuba kukuhlola okusisiseko.

ukuhlola okuqhubekayo (continuous assessment) - ukuhlola okuyinxalenye yokufundisa nokukhuliswa
kwabafundi ngokusoloko benikwa ingxelo ngomsebenzi wabo.

ukubhala ngokudibanisa (cursive writing) - indlela yokubhala ngesandla eyenziwa ngokubhala amagama
ungakhange uluphakamise usiba (pen) okanye ipensile. Kuya kuphuma itekisi edibeneyo enoonobumba abenzeke
kuloo ndawo luphakanyiswe kuyo usiba okanye ipensile.

ilitheresi ekhulayo (emergent literacy) - luthetha ulwazi lwabafundi olukhulayo malunga namagama. Abafundi
babona amagama abhaliweyo baze bawaqonde ukuba athetha ukuthini. Bafunda ngeencwadi namabali befundelwa
ngabantu abadala okanye abafundi abadala kunabo. Basenokuqala ukuzama ukubhala amagama abo besebenzisa
iindlela abacinga ukuba apelwa ngazo (upelo oluyiliweyo okanye upelo olukhulayo) yaye basenokuqala ukulinganisa
ukufunda iincwadi. Zonke ezi zenzo zibonisa ukukhula kolwazi lwabo lwelitheresi - ilitheresi ekhulayo.

itekisi ezotyiweyo (graphic text) - itekisi apho ulwazi lungohlobo olubonwayo umz. imizobo, iigrafu, njalo-njalo.

ukufunda okuncediswayo (guided reading) - lithuba lokufunda kweqela apho abafundi bakwinqanaba elinye
lokufundiswa aze utitshala ababonise indlela yokufunda ukwenzela ukuba bona baye bengenelela befunda ngokwabo.

ukubhala okuncediswayo (guided writing) - kulapho umfundi ngamnye okanye amaqela amancinci abafundi
abhala uluhlu lweentlobo zeetekisi emva kokuba utitshala ebanike isikhokelwana malunga nokubhala umz. ifomathi,
ukuphawulwa kwentetho (ngeekhoma, izingxi, njalo-njalo), iukusetyenziswa kolwimi okanye upelo.

ukuprinta okudityaniswayo (joint script) - luhlobo lokudibanisa olusekelwe kuhlobo oluthile lwendlela yokubhala
(script) engadibanisiyo, olwenzeka ngokuba amagama amaninzi adityaniswe ngenjongo yokukhawulezisa ukubhala.

isandi (phoneme) - izandi ezizimeleyo zolwimi (umz. ‘t’, ‘th’, ‘ty’, ‘dy’) Igama u-‘apha’ lineefonimi ezintathu ezizezi:
‘a’ ‘ph’ ‘a’.

ulwazi lwezandi (phonemic awareness) - ukukwazi ukwahlula izandi ezahlukeneyo zolwimi.

izandi zopelo (phonics) - ubudlelwane phakathi kwezandi nendlela ezipelwa ngayo. Izandi zopelo zisetyenziswa
ekufundeni nasekubhaleni.

ulwazi lwezandi (phonological awareness) - ukukwazi ukuqwalasela izandi zolwimi uzahlula kwintsingiselo yazo.

isingqi (rhyme) - amagama okanye imiqolo yombongo ephela ngesandi esinye (umz. hlamba, hamba, thamba).

ukufunda notitshala (shared reading) - umsebenzi apho abafundi bafunda kunye notitshala kwitekisi eyandisiweyo
yaba nkulu. Esi sisifundo seklasi yonke. Le tekisi isetyenziswayo ijoliswe kwelona qela liqhuba kakuhle apha eklasini.
Abanye abafundi baya kuphulaphula, abanye baqale kancinci ukufunda ngelixa abaninzi beya kube befunda
ngokupheleleyo. Le tekisi inye isetyenziswa iintsuku ezininzi. Utitshala ujonga indawo ekuza kugxilwa kuyo ngosuku

 98 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3
ngalunye. Itekisi le isetyenziselwa ukwazisa ngokusemxholweni iimpawu zetekisi, izandi, iukusetyenziswa kolwimi
nezakhono zokufunda.

amagama abonakala lula (sight words) - ngamagama umntu ofundayo awaqonda ngokuzenzekelayo (xa
ewabona). Akukho mfuneko yokuba ade acazululwe. Xa esafundiswa ukufunda umfundi amagama aqondeka lula
adla ngokuxhaphaka okanye amagama ‘asetyenziswa kakhulu’ afana nala: ‘umama’ ‘usisi’.

CAPS 99
 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA YEBANGA 1-3

 100 INKCAZELO YEPOLISI YEKHARITYHULAM NOKUHLOLA
